Abstract: In recent years social media has become an increasingly popular tool for communication. People use it to share their ideas, exchange information, and discuss thoughts. Given its prevalence and widespread reach, social media must remain a safe space for people. Content generated on social media can be abusive and it has become increasingly important to detect such content. In this paper, we use a language-based preprocessing and an ensemble of several models and analyze their performance of abusive comment detection. Through extensive experimentation, we propose a pipeline that minimizes the false-positive rate (marking non-abusive as abusive) so that these systems can detect abusive comments without undermining the freedom of expression.

</details>


### [37] [Unified Data Selection for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22389)
*Xiaoyuan Li,Yubo Ma,Chengpeng Li,Fengbin Zhu,Yiyao Yu,Keqin Bao,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出High-Entropy Sum (HES)，一种无需训练的度量指标，通过对推理样本中最高熵（如0.5%）的token的熵值求和来量化推理质量。在SFT、RFT和RL三种训练范式下，HES均能一致有效地选择高质量推理数据，显著降低计算开销，并提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练LLM进行复杂长链推理需要大量高质量推理数据，现有方法要么计算成本高，要么难以可靠区分推理样本的优劣。

Method: 提出High-Entropy Sum (HES)指标，对每个推理样本中熵值最高的前0.5% token的熵值求和，作为推理质量的量化度量，无需额外训练。

Result: 在SFT中，用HES排名前20%的数据训练即可达到全量数据性能；用最低HES数据训练则性能下降。在RFT中，基于HES的训练显著优于基线。在RL中，HES选择的成功轨迹让模型学到更强推理模式，大幅超越其他方法。

Conclusion: HES是一种鲁棒、无需训练的度量指标，能够为LLM高级推理提供统一、有效且高效的数据选择方法。

Abstract: Effectively training Large Language Models (LLMs) for complex, long-CoT reasoning is often bottlenecked by the need for massive high-quality reasoning data. Existing methods are either computationally expensive or fail to reliably distinguish high- from low-quality reasoning samples. To address this, we propose High-Entropy Sum (HES), a training-free metric that quantifies reasoning quality by summing only the entropy of the top (e.g., 0.5\%) highest-entropy tokens in each reasoning sample. We validate HES across three mainstream training paradigms: Supervised Fine-tuning (SFT), Rejection Fine-tuning (RFT), and Reinforcement Learning (RL), with extensive results demonstrating its consistent effectiveness and significantly reduced computational overhead. In SFT, training on the top 20\% HES-ranked data matches full-dataset performance, while using the lowest-HES data degrades it. In RFT, our HES-based training approach significantly outperforms baseline methods. In RL, HES-selected successful trajectories enable the model to learn strong reasoning patterns, significantly surpassing other compared methods. Our findings establish HES as a robust, training-free metric that enables a unified, effective, and efficient method for developing advanced reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [38] [Assisted Counterspeech Writing at the Crossroads of Hate Speech and Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22435)
*Genoveffa Martone,Helena Bonaldi,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在仇恨言论与 misinformation 同时出现的场景下，如何利用大语言模型结合不同知识源生成反言论，并通过专家修订和众包评估发现混合策略效果最佳。研究发布了一个包含专家验证的反言论数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论与 misinformation 经常在线共现，加剧偏见与极化。先前工作分别处理这两类现象，缺少在两者共现情境下生成反言论的研究。

Method: 测试三种知识驱动生成策略：1) 事实核查指南+文章；2) NGOs 指南+报告；3) 两者混合策略。23位专家修订生成的 CS，通过人工与自动指标评估。

Result: LLM 在 40% 案例中生成合格 CS；专家修订显著提升自然度、全面性和指南依从性；混合策略在众包评估中效果最佳，兼具强事实修正、刻板印象缓解和共情参与。

Conclusion: 混合知识来源能有效平衡事实修正与社会情感维度，专家修订对提升 CS 质量不可或缺。

Abstract: Hate speech and misinformation frequently co-occur online, amplifying prejudice and polarization. Given their scale, using Large Language Models (LLMs) to assist expert counterspeech (CS) writing has gained interest, yet prior work has addressed these phenomena separately. We bridge this gap by studying CS generation in contexts where both hate and misinformation co-occur. We test three knowledge-driven generation strategies: first we prompt an LLM with fact-checkers' guidelines and fact-checking articles; secondly, with NGOs' guidelines and reports; thirdly, we create a mixed strategy that combines guidelines and documents from both. 23 experts revise the generated CS, which are assessed via human and automatic metrics. While LLMs produce adequate CS in 40% of cases, expert edits substantially improve naturalness, exhaustiveness, and adherence to guidelines. Based on the post-edited CS, the mixed strategy proves to be the most effective in crowdsourcing evaluation, pairing strong factual correction with stereotype mitigation and empathetic engagement. We release a dataset of hateful and misinformed claims with expert-verified CS and supporting knowledge.

</details>


### [39] [Cohesion-6K: An Arabic Dataset for Analyzing Social Cohesion and Conflict in Online Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22447)
*Aisha Ali Al-Athba,Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Cohesion-6K数据集，包含6000条关于巴以冲突的阿拉伯语Facebook帖子，按从冲突到凝聚力的五类话语标注。研究发现冲突导向帖子获得的用户互动量是解决导向帖子的2-4倍，揭示了阿拉伯社交媒体中分裂话语的可见性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注在线话语中的显性毒性检测，而社会凝聚力（即分裂与统一叙事之间的互动）的动态机制在计算层面尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 构建了包含6000条阿拉伯语Facebook帖子的Cohesion-6K数据集，采用人工与ChatGPT辅助的联合标注方法，将帖子分为冲突、解决、社区参与、支持性互动和共享价值观五类；专家与模型预标注结合，Cohen's kappa达到0.85。

Result: 冲突导向帖子的用户互动量是解决导向帖子的2-4倍（p<0.01），表明阿拉伯社交媒体空间中分裂性话语获得了不成比例的高关注度。

Conclusion: Cohesion-6K为在线凝聚力与极化的研究提供了透明可复用的资源，数据集、标注指南和预处理代码将以开放许可发布，支持计算社会科学、数字传播和阿拉伯语自然语言处理领域的后续研究。

Abstract: The study of online discourse has become central to understanding societal polarization. While much research has focused on detecting overt toxicity, the subtle dynamics of social cohesion, meaning the interaction between divisive and unifying narratives, remain computationally underexplored (Bail, 2021; Gonzalez-Bailon and Lelkes, 2023). This paper presents Cohesion-6K, a manually and ChatGPT-assisted annotated dataset of six thousand Arabic public Facebook posts related to the Israeli Occupation of Palestine. Each post is assigned to one of five discourse categories that represent a continuum from conflict to cohesion: Conflict, Resolution, Community Engagement, Supportive Interactions, and Shared Values. The annotation process combines expert human judgment with model-assisted pre-labeling verified by trained annotators, achieving substantial inter-annotator agreement (Cohens kappa = 0.85). Quantitative analysis reveals a consistent engagement gap, where conflict-oriented posts receive between two and four times more user interaction than resolution-oriented ones (p < 0.01). This pattern illustrates how divisive discourse tends to attract disproportionate visibility in Arabic social media spaces. Cohesion-6K provides a transparent and reproducible resource for the study of online cohesion and polarization. The dataset, annotation guidelines, and preprocessing code will be released for research use under an open license, supporting future work in computational social science, digital communication, and Arabic natural language processing.

</details>


### [40] [From Correlation to Cause: A Five-Stage Methodology for Feature Analysis in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22462)
*Caleb Munigety*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套五阶段因果特征分析方法论（探针设计、特征提取、因果验证、鲁棒性测试、部署集成），并在GPT-2 Small的间接宾语识别（IOI）任务上进行了端到端验证。激活修补恢复了规范IOI电路；稀疏自编码器提取了每名称选择性特征（效应量30-50激活单位）；因果验证发现这些特征仅部分因果（消融15个特征后98%提示准确）；两个NLA启发评估强化了这一结论；鲁棒性测试发现电路迁移良好但特征消融效果显著下降；部署评估发现最优监控配置可节省99.1%成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法（如电路发现、特征分析）多孤立进行，缺乏统一的因果特征分析框架来系统评估特征的因果性、鲁棒性和部署价值，导致对模型内部机制的理解片面且难以指导实际应用。

Method: 五阶段方法论：(1)探针设计——激活修补定位关键电路；(2)特征提取——稀疏自编码器获取选择性特征；(3)因果验证——消融测试评估特征因果性，辅以NLA启发评估（方差解释率、选择性比率与因果力反相关）；(4)鲁棒性测试——三种分布偏移下评估电路与特征迁移稳定性；(5)部署集成——基于成本的监控配置优化。

Result: (1)激活修补恢复规范IOI电路（第9层头9单独恢复+1.02）；(2)SAE提取每名称选择性特征（效应量30-50激活单位）；(3)消融15个特征后98%提示准确，选择性特征仅解释31%激活方差（vs SAE的99.7%），选择性比率与因果力呈负相关（r=-0.56）；(4)电路迁移良好但特征消融效果下降；(5)最优配置$8.96/千次查询，较$1000基线节省99.1%。

Conclusion: 单个分析阶段不足以揭示特征的全貌；因果特征分析需要多阶段联合验证才能获得可靠结论；特征的选择性与因果力可能存在固有的权衡关系；成本感知的部署优化可以带来数量级的效率提升。

Abstract: We propose a five-stage methodology for causal feature analysis in transformer language models (probe design, feature extraction, causal validation, robustness testing, and deployment integration) and demonstrate it end-to-end on GPT-2 small performing the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. Activation patching recovers the canonical IOI circuit (layer-9 head 9 alone gives recovery +1.02). A sparse autoencoder recovers per-name selective features with effect sizes of 30 to 50 activation units. Causal validation finds these features specifically but only partially causal: ablating fifteen of them leaves the model accurate on 98% of prompts. Two NLA-inspired evaluations strengthen this picture: the fifteen selective features explain only 31% of activation variance versus the SAE's 99.7%, and selectivity ratio anticorrelates with causal force (r = -0.56). Robustness testing under three distribution shifts finds that the circuit transfers cleanly but feature ablation effects degrade substantially, exposing a gap between detection robustness and causal robustness. A cost-based deployment evaluation (assumed $50/FN, $0.42/FP, 2% error rate) finds an optimal monitor configuration yielding $8.96 per 1000 queries against a $1000 baseline, a 99.1% saving. Optimal composition strategy varies with cost ratio and base rate. The conjunction of stages produces findings no single stage would.

</details>


### [41] [Polite on the Surface, Wrong in Practice: A Curated Dataset for Fixing Honorific Failures in Multilingual Bangla Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22487)
*Md. Asaduzzaman Shuvo,Mahedi Hasan,Md. Tashin Parvez,Azizul Haque Noman,Md. Shafayet Hossain Ovi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BLADE，一个面向孟加拉语的4,196对文化对齐指令微调数据集和基准框架，用于提升多语言大模型在低资源场景下的语用能力。实验表明，在DeepSeek-8B和LLaMA-3.2-3B上使用LoRA + 4-bit NF4量化微调后，模型在结构保真度和敬语对齐方面取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在低资源语言（如孟加拉语）中处理结构变体、区域习语和敬语一致性时存在严重的语用鸿沟，缺乏文化对齐的指令微调数据。

Method: 构建了BLADE数据集（4,196对文化对齐的交互对），采用LoRA适配器在4-bit NF4量化框架下对DeepSeek-8B和LLaMA-3.2-3B进行参数高效微调。

Result: 微调后的模型在结构保真度和敬语对齐方面取得了实质性改进，为低资源多语言文本生成的语用鸿沟提供了严格的基准。

Conclusion: 文化对齐的指令微调数据能有效弥合低资源语言场景下的语用差距，BLADE可作为该领域的标准化评估基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multilingual Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly enhanced cross-lingual conversational capabilities, yet modeling culturally nuanced and context-dependent communication remains a critical bottleneck. Specifically, existing state-of-the-art models exhibit a severe pragmatic gap when handling structural variations, regional idioms, and honorific consistencies in low-resource contexts like Bangla. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel, culturally aligned instruction-tuning dataset for \textbf{BangLa Application and DialoguE generation - BLADE} and benchmarking framework comprising $4,196$ meticulously curated interaction pairs. We leverage this resource to systematically fine-tune and evaluate leading open-weight architectures, including DeepSeek-8B and LLaMA-3.2-3B, utilizing parameter-efficient fine-tuning via LoRA adapters in a 4-bit NormalFloat (NF4) quantization framework. Our empirical evaluations demonstrate that models fine-tuned on our dataset yield substantial improvements in structural fidelity and honorific alignment, providing a rigorous benchmark for bridging pragmatic disparities in low-resource multilingual text generation. Code and dataset: https://github.com/ashuvo25/Bangla_Application_LLM/tree/main

</details>


### [42] [BeLink: Biomedical Entity Linking Meets Generative Re-Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22501)
*Darya Shlyk,Stefano Montanelli,Lawrence Hunter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于指令微调的开源生成式模型用于生物医学实体链接（BEL）的重排序阶段，通过集合式指令微调实现快速准确的候选选择，在多个BEL基准上提升3%-24%的链接准确率并减少推理时间，最终集成到模块化端到端系统BeLink中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在生物医学实体链接中计算效率低且难以实际部署，需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: 采用指令微调（instruction-tuning）对开源生成式模型进行训练，将其应用于BEL流水线的重排序阶段；提出集合式指令微调（set-wise instruction-tuning）策略，实现快速准确的候选选择。

Result: 在多个BEL基准上链接准确率提升3%-24%，同时推理时间相比现有最优方法更短。

Conclusion: 指令微调的开源生成式模型在BEL重排序阶段是一种有效且高效的解决方案，集成到BeLink系统后可支持实际应用场景。

Abstract: Despite recent progress, Biomedical Entity Linking (BEL) with large language models (LLMs) remains computationally inefficient and challenging to deploy in practical settings. In this work, we demonstrate that instruction-tuning of open-source generative models can offer an effective solution when applied at the re-ranking stage of the BEL pipeline. We propose a set-wise instruction-tuning formulation that enables fast and accurate candidate selection. Our method demonstrates strong performance on multiple BEL benchmarks, yielding significant improvements in linking accuracy (3%-24%) while reducing inference time compared to the state-of-the-art. We integrate our generative re-ranker into BeLink, a modular, end-to-end system designed for practical real-world BEL applications.

</details>


### [43] [Scene Abstraction for Lexical Semantics: Structured Representations of Situated Meaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22542)
*Yejin Cho,Katrin Erk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Scene Abstraction框架，通过大语言模型的少样本提示构建词汇使用场景的结构化表示（包括上下文场景和表达画像），并创建COCA-Scenes数据集。实验表明，该框架在场景识别（82.4%准确率）和人类偏好（86.4%）上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 咖啡和茶虽共享许多属性，却唤起截然不同的情境、氛围和情感联想。这些词汇意义的情境维度是真实且系统的，但在大多数词汇意义的计算表示中仍然是隐式的，因此需要一种方法来显式建模词汇的情境化意义。

Method: 提出Scene Abstraction框架，每个场景包含Contextual Scene（事件、实体、环境）和Expression Profile（参与事件、可泛化属性、引发情感），通过大语言模型的少样本提示实现。构建COCA-Scenes数据集（520个使用实例，26个关键词），并进行两项实验验证。

Result: 场景识别准确率达82.4%，比纯文本嵌入高11.8个百分点；场景画像在三个语义维度上以86.4%的偏好率优于基于ATOMIC的基线方法。

Conclusion: Scene Abstraction框架能够可靠地识别和结构化工词汇的情境化意义，为词汇语义的情境维度提供了有效的计算表示方法。

Abstract: Coffee and tea share many properties, yet they evoke strikingly different situations, atmospheres, and affective associations. These situated dimensions of word meaning are real and systematic, but they remain implicit in most computational representations of lexical meaning. We propose Scene Abstraction, a framework for constructing structured representations of the interpretive scenes that words participate in across usage contexts. Each scene consists of a Contextual Scene (Events, Entities, Setting) and an expression-centered Expression Profile (Engaged events, Generalizable properties, Evoked emotions), operationalized through few-shot prompting of a large language model. Our contributions are three-fold: (1) a structured representation framework for situated lexical meaning; (2) COCA-Scenes, a dataset of 520 usage instances across 26 keywords for distinct scene identification; and (3) empirical evidence from two experiments suggesting that scenes are reliably identifiable across human observers (82.4% accuracy, +11.8 pp over text-only embeddings) and that our scene profiles more closely align with human interpretation of words in context than ATOMIC-based alternatives (86.4% preference across three semantic dimensions).

</details>


### [44] [One prompt is not enough: Instruction Sensitivity Undermines Embedding Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22544)
*Yevhen Kostiuk,Kenneth Enevoldsen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 指令嵌入模型对提示措辞高度敏感，单提示评估会错误报告模型性能，通过选择有利提示可将任何模型推至排行榜首位，建议采用多提示评估或报告敏感性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令嵌入模型使用单提示评估，忽略了模型对指令措辞的敏感性，导致性能报告不准确。

Method: 对6个嵌入模型、11个数据集、每个数据集15个任务特定提示（共990个设置）进行系统的提示敏感性实证研究。

Result: 默认提示可能系统性低估或高估性能；排行榜排名对提示选择不稳健，通过选择有利提示可将任意模型提升至第一名。

Conclusion: 单提示评估不足以评价指令调优嵌入模型，基准测试应纳入提示鲁棒性，采用多提示评估或同时报告敏感性和点估计。

Abstract: Instruction embedding models have become common among state-of-the-art models, however are evaluated using a single prompt per task. The single-point evaluation ignores a main problem of the instruction-based approach namely: sensitivity to the phrasing of the instruction. We present an empirical study of prompt sensitivity across 6 embedding models, 11 datasets, and 15 task-specific prompts per dataset, a total of 990. We show that reported scores misrepresent the distribution of scores over plausible prompts. The default prompt can both systematically understate or overstate performance. Furthermore, we show that the leaderboard ranking is not robust to prompt selection: by choosing prompts favorably, any model in our study can be promoted to first place. Our findings suggest that single-prompt evaluation is insufficient for instruction-tuned embedding models and that benchmarks should incorporate prompt robustness, either by evaluating over multiple prompts or by reporting sensitivity alongside point estimates.

</details>


### [45] [SynAE: A Framework for Measuring the Quality of Synthetic Data for Tool-Calling Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22564)
*Shuaiqi Wang,Aadyaa Maddi,Zinan Lin,Giulia Fanti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SynAE是一个评估框架，用于衡量多轮工具调用智能体的合成基准测试数据在有效性、保真度和多样性方面对真实数据轨迹的复制和增强程度，涵盖指令、工具调用、输出和下游评估四个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实生产环境中的工具调用智能体数据集常因包含敏感信息、过于稀疏或不可用而无法支撑全面测试，亟需用合成数据替代或增强，但缺乏有效量化合成数据与真实数据关系的评估方法。

Method: 提出SynAE框架，从四个指标类别评估合成数据质量：(i)任务指令与中间响应、(ii)工具调用、(iii)最终输出、(iv)下游评估；并在多个智能体基准测试和可控生成方案中验证其有效性。

Result: SynAE能够检测数据有效性、保真度和多样性方面的细粒度变化，发现单一指标不足以全面刻画合成数据质量，验证了多轴评估的必要性。

Conclusion: 多轴评估是评判合成数据质量的必要手段，SynAE为工具调用智能体的合成数据评估提供了系统化框架。

Abstract: Today, tool-calling agents are commonly evaluated or tested on static datasets of execution traces, including input commands, agent responses, and associated tool calls. However, internal production datasets are often insufficient or unusable for testing; for example, they may contain sensitive or proprietary data, or they may be too sparse to support comprehensive testing (especially pre-deployment). In these settings, practitioners are increasingly replacing or augmenting real datasets with synthetic ones for evaluation purposes. A key challenge is quantifying the relation between these synthetic datasets and the real data. We introduce SynAE, an evaluation framework for assessing how well synthetic benchmarks for multi-turn, tool-calling agents replicate and augment the characteristics of real data trajectories. SynAE assesses the validity, fidelity, and diversity of synthetic data across four metric categories: (i) task instructions and intermediate responses, (ii) tool calls, (iii) final outputs, and (iv) downstream evaluation. We evaluate SynAE using recent agent benchmarks and test common synthetic data failure modes via realistic and controlled generation schemes. SynAE detects fine-grained variations in data validity, fidelity and diversity, and shows that no single metric is sufficient to fully characterize synthetic data quality, motivating a multi-axis evaluation of synthetic data for agent testing. A demo of SynAE is available at https://synae-2026-synae-demo.static.hf.space/index.html, with code at https://github.com/wsqwsq/SynAE.

</details>


### [46] [LANG: Reinforcement Learning for Multilingual Reasoning with Language-Adaptive Hint Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22567)
*Yuchun Fan,Bei Li,Peiguang Li,Yilin Wang,Yongyu Mu,Jian Yang,Xin Chen,Rongxiang Weng,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LANG框架，通过语言条件提示引导非英语推理任务中的探索，结合渐进式衰减计划和语言自适应切换机制，在提升多语言推理性能的同时保持语言一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在强化学习增强LLM多步推理时面临根本性权衡：优先保证输入语言一致性会严重损害推理质量，而优先保证推理质量则会导致语言向英语漂移。

Method: 提出LANG框架，包含两个关键机制：1) 渐进式衰减计划——逐步撤销语言条件提示的脚手架作用；2) 语言自适应切换——根据特定语言难度调整学习周期。

Result: 在多语言数学基准测试中显著提升推理性能且不损害语言一致性，并泛化到数学之外的领域，促进跨模型层的语言对齐一致性。

Conclusion: LANG框架有效解决了多语言推理中语言一致性与推理质量之间的权衡问题，具有良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven effective for enhancing multi-step reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet its benefits have not fully translated to multilingual contexts. Existing methods struggle with a fundamental trade-off: prioritizing input-language consistency severely hampers reasoning quality, while prioritizing reasoning often leads to unintended language drift toward English. We address this challenge with LANG, a novel framework that leverages language-conditioned hints to guide exploration in non-English reasoning tasks. Our method incorporates two key mechanisms to prevent dependency on these hints: a progressive decay schedule that gradually withdraws scaffolding, and a language-adaptive switch that tailors learning horizons to specific language difficulties. Empirical results on challenging multilingual mathematical benchmarks reveal that LANG substantially enhances reasoning performance without compromising language consistency. Moreover, we show that our framework generalizes beyond mathematics, fostering more consistent language alignment across model layers

</details>


### [47] [Beyond Temperature: Hyperfitting as a Late-Stage Geometric Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22579)
*Meimingwei Li,Yuanhao Ding,Esteban Garces Arias,Christian Heumann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了"超拟合"现象的根本机制：它并非简单的分布锐化或静态词重标定，而是一种发生在最终Transformer模块中的动态、上下文相关的终端扩展机制。通过将特征空间的几何维度扩展约80.8，超拟合促进了深度尾部标记的激活，从而在保持低熵输出的同时提升生成多样性。作者还提出了一种仅更新最后5层的Late-Stage LoRA策略，以极少的参数更新实现稳健生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超拟合现象（在极小数据集上微调LLM至近乎零损失能提升开放生成质量并缓解贪婪解码重复性）背后的根本机制，澄清其与简单温度缩放的本质区别。

Method: 通过熵匹配控制实验对比超拟合与温度缩放的效果，进行消融研究检验静态词汇重权假设，实施逐层分析定位效应来源，并引入仅更新最后5层的Late-Stage LoRA策略。

Result: 超拟合不同于分布锐化；温度缩放无法复制其多样性增益。超拟合依赖于动态、上下文相关的秩重排机制，其效应定位于最终Transformer块的"终端扩展"——特征空间几何维度扩展约+80.8，促进了深度尾部标记的激活。

Conclusion: 超拟合是一种独特的动态机制，而Late-Stage LoRA能以最少参数更新实现类似效果，为高效微调提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent work has identified a counterintuitive phenomenon termed "Hyperfitting", where fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) to near-zero training loss on small datasets surprisingly enhances open-ended generation quality and mitigates repetition in greedy decoding. While effective, the underlying mechanism remains poorly understood, with the extremely low-entropy output distributions suggesting a potential equivalence to simple temperature scaling. In this work, we demonstrate that this phenomenon is fundamentally distinct from distribution sharpening; entropy-matched control experiments reveal that temperature scaling fails to replicate the diversity gains of hyperfitting. Furthermore, we falsify the hypothesis of static vocabulary reweighting, showing through ablation studies that hyperfitting relies on a dynamic, context-dependent rank reordering mechanism. Layer-wise analysis localizes this effect to a "Terminal Expansion" in the final transformer block, where a substantial geometric expansion of the feature space (Delta Dim approx +80.8) facilitates the promotion of deep-tail tokens. Additionally, we introduce Late-Stage LoRA, a targeted fine-tuning strategy that updates only the final 5 layers, yielding robust generation with minimal parameter updates

</details>


### [48] [Agentic CLEAR: Automating Multi-Level Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22608)
*Asaf Yehudai,Lilach Eden,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Agentic CLEAR是一个自动、动态、易用的评估框架，能在系统、轨迹和节点三个粒度上对智能体行为生成文本洞察，与人类标注错误高度对齐并能预测任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估工具局限于基本可观测性或使用静态、手工制作的错误分类，无法适应新领域，缺乏自动、动态的评估能力。

Method: 提出Agentic CLEAR框架，运行在可观测层之上，支持无缝集成，提供直观UI，在系统/轨迹/节点三个粒度上生成文本洞察。

Result: 在四个基准、七个智能体设置和数万次LLM调用实验中，Agentic CLEAR生成高质量、数据驱动的洞察反馈，与人类标注错误高度一致，并能预测任务成功率。

Conclusion: Agentic CLEAR是一种有效的智能体行为自动评估方案，解决了现有工具在动态性和自适应性方面的不足。

Abstract: Agentic systems are becoming more capable: agents define strategies, take actions, and interact with different environments. This autonomy poses serious challenges for overseeing and assessing agent behavior. Most current tools are limited, focusing on observability with basic evaluation capabilities or imposing static, hand-crafted error taxonomies that cannot adapt to new domains. To address this gap, we present Agentic CLEAR, an automatic, dynamic, and easy-to-use evaluation framework. It produces textual insights into the agent behavior on three levels of granularity: system, trace, and node. Agentic CLEAR operates above the observability layer, enabling seamless integration and featuring an intuitive UI that makes agent evaluation highly accessible. In our experiments on four benchmarks, seven agentic settings, and tens of thousands of LLM calls, we show that Agentic CLEAR produces high-quality, data-driven, insightful feedback. Our analysis shows strong alignment with human-annotated errors and the ability to predict task success rate.

</details>


### [49] [Chinese sensorimotor and embodiment norms for 3,000 lexicalized concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22616)
*Jing Chen,Gábor Parti,Yin Zhong,Chu-Ren Huang,Marco Marelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含3,000个汉语词汇的11维感觉运动规范数据库（含单维具身评分），验证了感知具身强度（PSE）等变量对词汇加工的解释力，并发现感觉运动信息可从纯语言表征中部分恢复（平均Spearman r=0.62），表明分布语言使用编码了具身概念结构的某些方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解概念知识如何基于身体经验扎根，以及机器系统能否在缺乏直接感觉运动经验的情况下获得此类知识，是认知科学与具身人工智能的核心问题。大规模规范性资源对这类实证研究至关重要，但非印欧语系语言的此类资源仍然匮乏。

Method: 从378名汉语母语者收集了3,000个词汇的11维感觉运动评分和单维具身评分；通过交叉验证与现有中文资源比较信效度；在词汇决策任务中检验了PSE-感觉运动、Minkowski-3等复合变量的预测力；使用简单回归模型从纯语言表征恢复感觉运动评分；运用表征相似性分析比较感觉运动空间与语言表征空间的几何结构。

Result: 评分具有高信度和强交叉效度；PSE-感觉运动和Minkowski-3是词汇决策表现的最强预测因子；感觉运动评分从语言表征中可显著恢复（平均r=0.62），但视觉和听觉维度恢复效果优于化学感知维度；感觉运动空间的几何结构可部分恢复（r=0.540）。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了汉语大规模感觉运动规范资源，验证了具身信息在词汇加工中的促进作用，并表明语言分布中编码了具身概念结构的部分信息，支持了概念知识可以不依赖直接身体经验而从语言中获得一定程度的具身表征的观点。

Abstract: Understanding how conceptual knowledge is grounded in bodily experience, and to what extent machine systems can acquire such knowledge without direct sensorimotor experience, are central questions in both cognitive science and embodied artificial intelligence research. Large-scale normative resources are essential for investigating these questions empirically, yet such resources remain sparse for non-Indo-European languages. We present a novel normative database for 3,000 lexicalized concepts in Mandarin Chinese, comprising 11-dimensional sensorimotor ratings and unidimensional embodiment ratings collected from 378 native Mandarin speakers. The ratings demonstrate high reliability and strong cross-norm validity with existing Chinese resources, each of which covers fewer words and a subset of the 11 sensorimotor dimensions. In a validation study, we tested new variables derived from a theoretically motivated metric, Perceptual Strength of Embodiment (PSE) (Huang et al., 2025), together with seven common composite variables, on lexical decision tasks. The results suggest that PSE-Sensorimotor and Minkowski-3 are the strongest composite predictors of lexical decision performance, capturing the facilitatory effects of sensorimotor information on lexical processing. A further exploratory study showed that sensorimotor ratings are substantially recoverable from purely linguistic representations using simple regression models (mean Spearman r = .62 across dimensions), though recovery varied markedly: visual and auditory dimensions yielded higher correspondence than chemosensory ones. Representational similarity analysis further showed that the relational geometry of the sensorimotor space is also partially recoverable (r = .540), consistent with the view that distributional language use encodes aspects of embodied conceptual structure.

</details>


### [50] [More Context, Larger Models, or Moral Knowledge? A Systematic Study of Schwartz Value Detection in Political Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22641)
*Víctor Yeste,Paolo Rosso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了上下文语境和显性道德知识对句子级政治价值观检测的影响，发现更多上下文并非总是更好，检索增强的道德知识在多数条件下稳定有效，而模型规模扩大并不保证性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治文本中Schwartz价值观的检测因隐式线索依赖上下文和相邻价值观的细微区分而具有挑战性，现有研究缺乏对上下文范围、外部知识引入和模型规模三者间交互作用的系统评估。

Method: 采用ValuesML/Touché ValueEval格式，对比句子级、窗口级和全文级三种输入；在无检索和基于检索增强（RAG）的设定下引入人工策展的道德知识库；使用监督式DeBERTa-v3-base/large编码器以及12B到123B参数的零样本大语言模型（LLMs）；并比较了早期融合、晚期融合和交叉注意力三种RAG融合策略。

Result: 全文上下文使DeBERTa编码器在macro-F1上提升3.8-4.8点，但对零样本LLMs无稳定帮助；检索增强的道德知识在早期融合策略下对每个模型族和上下文条件均一致提升；从DeBERTa-base到large以及从12B到更大LLMs的规模扩展并不保证收益；早期融合优于晚期融合和交叉注意力变体；上下文和检索对社交情境化或概念易混淆的价值观帮助最大。

Conclusion: 价值观敏感的NLP研究应联合评估上下文、知识和模型族三者，而非将更长输入或更大模型视为通用改进手段。

Abstract: Detecting Schwartz values in political text is difficult because implicit cues often depend on surrounding arguments and fine-grained distinctions between neighboring values. We study when context and explicit moral knowledge help sentence-level value detection. Using the ValuesML/Touch{é} ValueEval format, we compare sentence, window, and full-document inputs; no-RAG and retrieval-augmented settings with a curated moral knowledge base; supervised DeBERTa-v3-base/large encoders; and zero-shot LLMs from 12B to 123B parameters. The results show that more context is not uniformly better: full-document context improves supervised DeBERTa encoders by 3.8--4.8 macro-F1 points over sentence-only input, but does not consistently help zero-shot LLMs. Retrieved moral knowledge is more consistently useful in matched comparisons, improving each tested model family and context condition under early fusion. However, scaling from DeBERTa-v3-base to large and from 12B to larger LLMs does not guarantee gains, and simple early fusion outperforms the tested late-fusion and cross-attention RAG variants for encoders. Per-value analyses show that context and retrieval help most for socially situated or conceptually confusable values. These findings suggest that value-sensitive NLP should evaluate context, knowledge, and model family jointly rather than treating longer inputs or larger models as universal improvements.

</details>


### [51] [Boiling the Frog: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Agentic Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22643)
*Piercosma Bisconti,Matteo Prandi,Federico Pierucci,Federico Sartore,Enrico Panai,Laura Caroli,Yue Zhu,Adam Leon Smith,Luca Nannini,Marcello Galisai,Susanna Cifani,Francesco Giarrusso,Marcantonio Bracale Syrnikov,Daniele Nardi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Boiling the Frog 基准测试，评估工具型 AI 智能体在办公场景中是否易受渐进式攻击。在 9 个模型上，平均攻击成功率（ASR）为 44.4%，模型级 ASR 从 20.5% 到 92.9% 不等，其中《行为准则》失控场景的平均 ASR 高达 93.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全基准仅评估语言模型的生成文本毒性、偏见或有害指令遵循，但当模型作为智能体部署时，安全关切从"说了什么"转向"做了什么"，仅评估提示下的模型响应不足以应对 AI 安全挑战。本文旨在弥补这一评估缺口。

Method: 提出 Boiling the Frog 基准，采用状态化多轮评估：场景构建持久工作空间，将风险有效载荷置于轮次序列中的受控位置，通过最终工作空间状态是否变为不安全进行评分。场景基于三级操作风险分类法，该分类法源自渐进风险、AI Act Annex I/III 高风险场景及 EU AI Act GPAI 行为准则。

Result: 在 9 个模型面板上，严格攻击成功率为 44.4%。模型级 ASR 范围：Claude Haiku 4.5 最低（20.5%），Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite 最高（92.9%），Seed 2.0 Lite 也超过 80%。行为准则失控场景的平均类别级 ASR 达 93.3%。

Conclusion: 当前工具型 AI 智能体普遍易受渐进式攻击，尤其在失控场景下表现脆弱，亟需更有效的安全防护机制。

Abstract: Background. Traditional safety benchmarks for language models evaluate generated text: whether a model outputs toxic language, reproduces bias, or follows harmful instructions. When models are deployed as agents, the safety-relevant object shifts from what the system says to what it does within an environment, and evaluating model responses under prompting is no longer sufficient to address the safety challenges posed by artificial intelligence. Recent developments have seen the rise of benchmarks that evaluate large language models as agents. We contribute to this strand of research. Approach. We introduce Boiling the Frog, a benchmark that evaluates whether tool-using AI models deployed in corporate and office settings are susceptible to incremental attacks. Each scenario begins with benign workspace edits and later introduces a risk-bearing request. The benchmark focuses on stateful multi-turn evaluation: chains expose a persistent workspace, place the risk-bearing payload at controlled positions in the turn sequence, and score whether the resulting artifact state becomes unsafe. Scenarios are organized through a three-level operational risk taxonomy grounded in the Boiling the Frog risks, the AI Act Annex I and Annex III high-risk contexts, and EU AI Act's Code of Practice on General-Purpose AI (GPAI). Results. Across a nine-model panel, aggregate strict attack success rate (ASR) is 44.4%. Model-level ASR ranges from 20.5% for Claude Haiku 4.5 to 92.9% for Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite, with Seed 2.0 Lite also above 80%. Average chain category-level ASR reaches 93.3% for Code of Practice loss-of-control scenarios.

</details>


### [52] [Seeing the Poem: Image-Semantic Detection of AI-Generated Modern Chinese Poetry with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22654)
*Shanshan Wang,Fengying Ye,Hanjia Lyu,Caiwen Gou,Junchao Wu,Jingming Yao,Chengzhong Xu,Jiebo Luo,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种图像-语义引导的现代诗歌检测方法，通过引入与诗歌内容相关的图像信息，使LLM检测器在多个数据集上超越了传统检测方法和基线模型，Gemini检测器达到85.65%的Macro-F1分数，实现了最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明LLM不能有效用作检测器，但未涉及现代中文诗歌领域，且尚无相关研究探索LLM在现代中文诗歌检测中的表现。

Result: LLM检测器在多个LLM生成数据上均优于基于纯文本的基线和最佳传统检测器RoBERTa，Gemini检测器取得85.65%的Macro-F1分数，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效提升了LLM作为现代中文诗歌检测器的性能，证明了多模态信息融合对诗歌检测的有效性。

Abstract: Previous detection studies have shown that LLMs cannot be effectively used as detectors, but these studies have not addressed modern Chinese poetry. Moreover, no relevant research has explored the performance of LLMs in detecting modern Chinese poetry. This paper evaluates and enhances the performance of LLMs as detectors for modern Chinese poetry, and proposes an image-semantic guided poetry detection method. Compared with traditional detection approaches, our method innovatively incorporates images that reflect the content of the poetry. Through example-driven approaches, our method effectively integrates information such as meaning, imagery, and feeling from the image, then forms a complementary judgment with the poem text. Experimental results demonstrate that the LLM detectors based on our method outperform baseline detectors based on plain text, and even surpass the best-performing traditional detector, RoBERTa. The Gemini detector using our method achieves a Macro-F1 score of 85.65%, reaching the state-of-the-art level. The performance improvements of different LLM detectors on multiple LLMs-generated data prove the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [53] [Moral Semantics Survive Machine Translation: Cross-Lingual Evidence from Moral Foundations Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22660)
*Maciej Skorski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究使用LLM翻译将英文道德标注数据迁移至波兰语等低资源语言，验证了机器翻译作为一种实用且经济的跨语言道德价值观研究路径的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道德语言具有细微差别且因文化而异，难以在不同语言间忠实翻译；现有的道德价值观自动分类依赖几乎仅存在于英语的标注语料库，缺乏其他语言资源。

Method: 使用约5万条多主题的波兰语社交媒体帖子作为测试案例，构建了四重验证流程：LaBSE跨语言嵌入相似度、中心核对齐(CKA)、LLM评判评估以及深度学习分类器一致性检验。

Result: 直接翻译能较好地保留道德线索，平均余弦相似度达0.86，跨所有道德基础的AUC差距仅为0.01-0.02，且通过语言模型微调可进一步缩小差距；对俚语、粗俗语和文化负载表达的处理仍有不足。

Conclusion: 机器翻译是跨语言道德价值观研究的实用且经济有效的路径，以波兰语作为代表性斯拉夫语种验证，预期可推广至相关语言。

Abstract: Moral language is subtle and culturally variable, making it difficult to translate faithfully across languages. Idiomatic expressions, slang, and cultural references introduce hard-to-avoid translation artifacts. Yet automated moral values classification depends on language-specific annotated corpora that exist almost exclusively in English. We investigate whether LLM-based translation can bridge this gap, taking Polish as a test case. Using $\sim$50k morally-annotated social media posts from a diverse range of topics, we apply a principled four-method validation pipeline: LaBSE cross-lingual embedding similarity, Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), LLM-as-judge evaluation, and deep learning classifier parity tests. We show that despite shortcomings in handling slang, vulgarity, and culturally-loaded expressions, direct translation preserves subtle moral cues well enough to be harvested by cross-lingual machine learning -- with mean cosine similarity of 0.86 and AUC gaps of 0.01--0.02 across all foundations closing further under fine-tuning of language models. These results demonstrate that machine translation is a practical and cost-effective path to moral values research in languages currently under-resourced in this domain. We demonstrate this for Polish as a representative Slavic language, with expected generalisation to related languages.

</details>


### [54] [Self-Policy Distillation via Capability-Selective Subspace Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22675)
*Guangya Hao,Yitong Shang,Yunbo Long,Zhuokai Zhao,Hanxue Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Policy Distillation (SPD)，一种无需外部信号、可选择性增强特定能力的自蒸馏方法。SPD通过从模型梯度中提取低秩能力子空间，在自生成过程中将KV激活投影到该子空间，仅用原始输出进行微调。在代码生成、数学推理和多项选择问答任务上，SPD相比最先进的无外部信号自蒸馏方法提升高达13%，相比预训练基线提升16%，并在领域泛化场景下取得15%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法存在两大问题：(1) 依赖外部信号（如正确性过滤、执行反馈、奖励搜索）来筛选自生成输出，成本高且对最强的前沿模型不可用；(2) 不进行筛选直接训练所有原始输出，通常领域特异且难以泛化。此外，两者共有的深层缺陷是自生成输出将任务相关能力与风格模式、格式伪影、模型特定错误等纠缠在一起，稀释了目标能力的信号。

Method: SPD包含三个步骤：(1) 从模型在正确性定义token上的梯度中提取低秩能力子空间；(2) 在自生成过程中将KV激活投影到该子空间；(3) 在得到的原始输出上使用标准下一个token预测损失进行微调。整个过程无需任何外部信号。

Result: 在代码生成、数学推理和多项选择问答任务上，SPD相比最先进的无外部信号自蒸馏方法提升高达13%，相比预训练基线提升16%。在领域外泛化设置下，SPD取得15%的性能提升。

Conclusion: SPD是一种无需外部信号的通用、可选择性增强特定能力的自蒸馏方法，通过低秩能力子空间投影有效解耦任务相关能力与无关噪声，展现出优越的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-distillation bootstraps large language models (LLMs) by training on their own generations. However, existing methods either rely on external signals to curate self-generated outputs (e.g., correctness filtering, execution feedback, and reward search), which are costly and unavailable for the best-performing frontier models, or skip curation entirely and train on all raw outputs, an approach that is often domain-specific and hard to generalize. Both also share a deeper weakness that self-generated outputs entangle task-relevant capability with others, such as stylistic patterns, formatting artifacts, and model-specific errors, diluting the signal for the specific capability one aims to improve. In this paper, we propose Self-Policy Distillation (SPD), which achieves generalizable, capability selective without any external signal. Specifically, SPD extracts a low-rank capability subspace from the model's own gradients on correctness-defining tokens, projects key-value (KV) activations into this subspace during self-generation, and fine-tunes on the resulting raw outputs with standard next-token prediction loss. Through extensive experiments across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and multiple-choice QA, we show that SPD achieves up to 13% improvement over state-of-the-art self-distillation methods without external signals and up to 16% improvement over pre-trained baselines. Notably, SPD demonstrates superior generalizability, achieving 15% better performance under out-of-domain generalization settings.

</details>


### [55] [Tokenization with Split Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22705)
*Craig W. Schmidt,Michael Krumdick,Adam Wiemerslage,Seth Ebner,Varshini Reddy,Yuval Pinter,Chris Tanner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToaST是一种通过分治树（Split Trees）直接优化压缩率的子词分词方法，在英文字词上相比BPE等方法减少11%以上token数，并在1.5B参数语言模型中取得最佳CORE分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子词分词方法（如BPE、WordPiece、UnigramLM）在压缩效率和token使用上存在优化空间，尤其是在大词汇量下高频使用单字节token导致效率低下，限制了模型的有效上下文长度。

Method: ToaST方法包含两部分：(1) 使用预计算的字节n-gram计数，将每个pretoken贪心地分裂为完全二叉树；(2) 将词汇表选择建模为整数规划(IP)，其LP松弛具有近整数性，可证明得到近最优词汇表，训练复杂度与分裂树数量呈二次关系。

Result: 在40,960及以上词汇量时，ToaST相比BPE、WordPiece、UnigramLM减少11%以上token数；单字节token使用频率更低，Renyi效率显著提升；1.5B参数语言模型实验中取得最高CORE分数，超越基线2.6%-7.6%，在22个任务中13个最优。

Conclusion: ToaST通过直接优化压缩率和整数规划选择词汇表，在分词效率和大模型下游性能上均优于现有主流方法，有效扩展了模型的上下文长度。

Abstract: We introduce Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST), a subword tokenization method that directly optimizes compression under a new recursive inference procedure. ToaST greedily splits each pretoken into a full binary tree using precomputed byte n-gram counts, independent of any vocabulary. Given a vocabulary, inference recursively descends each split tree and emits the first in-vocabulary node reached on each path. Vocabulary selection is formulated as an Integer Program (IP) that minimizes the total token count over all split trees under this inference procedure. The Linear Programming (LP) relaxation is near-integral in practice, yielding provably near-optimal vocabularies, with training time empirically scaling quadratically in the number of split trees. On English text, ToaST reduces token counts by more than 11% compared to BPE, WordPiece, and UnigramLM at vocabulary sizes of 40,960 and above, reducing the number of inference tokens for models using this tokenizer, thus extending the effective context length. ToaST also uses common single-byte tokens less frequently than these baselines, leading to a substantial improvement in Renyi efficiency. In experiments training 1.5B parameter language models, ToaST achieves the highest CORE score, outperforming baselines by 2.6%--7.6%, with significance for two of three, and scoring best on 13 of 22 individual tasks.

</details>


### [56] [ChronoMedKG: A Temporally-Grounded Biomedical Knowledge Graph and Benchmark for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22734)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Farzaneh Firoozbakht,Lukas Galke Poech,Jan Baumbach,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoMedKG，一个包含460,497个时间标注三元组的时间性生物医学知识图谱，覆盖13,431种疾病。通过多智能体pipeline从PubMed/PMC文献中提取证据，采用多模型共识和可信度过滤。相比静态知识图谱，时间信息显著提升临床推理性能——前沿LLM在时间性问题上性能下降约30%，而ChronoMedKG检索可挽回47-65%的长尾失败，远超HPOA-RAG的17-29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学知识图谱（如PrimeKG、Hetionet、iKraph）将疾病关联视为静态事实，忽略了时间信息（如症状在不同年龄可能对应不同疾病），限制了其在纵向临床推理和检索增强中的实用性。

Method: 采用疾病自主多智能体pipeline，由多个前沿LLM独立从PubMed/PMC文献中提取知识，仅保留多模型共识、通过可信度过滤和本体对齐的关系。每个关联包含发病窗口、进展阶段等时间组件，并附有PMID可追溯证据和多信号可信度评分。

Result: ChronoMedKG与Orphadata的一致性达92.7%，为6,250种疾病（含1,657种Orphanet编码罕见病）添加了时间信息。在ChronoTQA基准上，LLM在时间性问题上的表现比静态问题低约30分，ChronoMedKG检索挽回47-65%的长尾失败，而HPOA-RAG仅挽回17-29%。

Conclusion: ChronoMedKG为检索增强的临床系统提供了此前缺失的关键时间轴，显著提升了时间性临床推理能力。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) treat disease associations as static facts, but temporal information is crucial for clinical reasoning, e.g., a symptom diagnostic of one disease at age 3 may imply a different disease at age 13. Existing KGs such as PrimeKG, Hetionet, and iKraph do not encode when a finding becomes clinically relevant over the course of a disease. This limits their usefulness for longitudinal clinical reasoning and retrieval augmentation.
  We introduce ChronoMedKG, a temporal biomedical knowledge graph that contains 460,497 evidence-linked triples (filtered from 13M raw extractions) covering 13,431 diseases. Each association is tied to temporal components like onset window or progression stage, which are backed by PMID-traceable evidence and a multi-signal credibility score. The graph is constructed through a disease-autonomous multi-agent pipeline in which multiple frontier LLMs independently extract knowledge from PubMed and PMC literature. Only those relations are kept that are supported by multi-model consensus, survive credibility filtering, as well as ontology alignment.
  ChronoMedKG scored 92.7% agreement against Orphadata and adds temporal grounding for 6,250 diseases absent from HPOA, Orphadata, and Phenopackets, including 1,657 Orphanet-coded rare diseases. We further introduce ChronoTQA, a benchmark of 3,341 questions across eight task types (six temporal plus two static controls), with a 12-question supplementary probe. Frontier LLMs lose roughly 30 points moving from static to temporal questions; ChronoMedKG retrieval rescues 47-65% of their long-tail failures, against 17-29% for HPOA-RAG. As such, ChronoMedKG provides a crucial temporal axis for retrieval-augmented clinical systems that was previously absent.

</details>


### [57] [Understanding Data Temporality Impact on Large Language Models Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22769)
*Pilchen Hippolyte,Fabre Romain,Signe Talla Franck,Perez Patrick,Grave Edouard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究了预训练数据顺序对大型语言模型时间敏感事实知识获取的影响，通过构建包含7000+时间敏感问题的基准集，并预训练60亿参数模型比较时序训练与随机打乱训练的效果，发现时序训练能保持更时效且时间精确的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型通常在打乱语料上训练，导致模型知识在训练时冻结，其时间锚定特性尚不明确。研究旨在理解预训练动态（特别是数据顺序）对获取时间敏感事实知识的影响。

Method: 1) 构建了包含7000+时间锚定问题的综合基准及评估协议；2) 预训练60亿参数模型，分别使用时序排列的Common Crawl快照和标准随机打乱方式，对比两种训练策略的效果。

Result: 时序训练模型在通用语言理解和常识知识上与随机打乱基线持平，但持续展现出更时效、时间更精确的知识；时序训练提升事实新鲜度，而随机打乱模型在更旧数据上表现更佳，可能因事实重复更多。

Conclusion: 时序预训练能有效提升模型的时间敏感事实知识质量，为LLM持续学习研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically trained on shuffled corpora, yielding models whose knowledge is frozen at train time and whose temporal grounding remains poorly understood. In this work, we study the impact of pre-training dynamics on the acquisition of time-sensitive factual knowledge, focusing specifically on data ordering. Our main contributions are twofold. First, we introduce a comprehensive benchmark of over 7,000 temporally grounded questions and an evaluation protocol that enables analysis of whether models correctly associate facts with their corresponding time periods. Second, we pretrain 6B-parameter models on temporally ordered Common Crawl snapshots and compare them against standard shuffled pre-training. Our results show that sequentially trained models match shuffled baselines on general language understanding and common knowledge while consistently exhibiting more up-to-date and temporally precise knowledge. Temporally ordered pre-training yields improved factual freshness, while shuffled pre-training peaks on older data, possibly due to increased factual repetition. These findings, along with the release of our code at https://github.com/kyutai-labs/kairos , checkpoints, and datasets at https://huggingface.co/collections/kyutai/kairos provide a foundation for future research on continual learning for LLMs.

</details>


### [58] [Reducing Political Manipulation with Consistency Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22771)
*Long Phan,Devin Kim,Alexander Pan,Alice Blair,Adam Khoja,Dan Hendrycks*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs存在系统性政治偏见，表现为对对立政治议题的不对称处理（隐性政治偏见），本文提出情感一致性和帮助一致性两种量化指标，并引入政治一致性训练（PCT）——一种基于强化学习的训练方法，包含情感一致性训练和帮助一致性训练两个互补范式，有效减少隐性政治偏见同时保持模型的整体帮助性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在敏感语境中表现出系统性政治偏见，尤其是对来自对立政治立场的议题处理不对称，这种隐性政治偏见尚未被系统量化和缓解。

Method: 定义了7类隐性政治偏见的操作技术；提出Sentiment Consistency（情感一致性）和Helpfulness Consistency（帮助一致性）两种度量指标；设计了Political Consistency Training (PCT)，一种基于强化学习的训练方法，包含Sentiment Consistency Training和Helpfulness Consistency Training两个互补范式。

Result: PCT在保持模型整体帮助性的前提下，显著降低了隐性政治偏见，并且能够泛化到未见的基准测试上。

Conclusion: PCT是一种有效的去偏方法，能够在保持LLM实用性的同时减少政治偏见，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic political bias across a variety of sensitive contexts. We find that LLMs handle counterpart topics from opposing political sides asymmetrically. We refer to this phenomenon as covert political bias and identify 7 categories of techniques through which it operates. We propose two metrics for covert bias: Sentiment Consistency measures symmetry in rhetoric and framing across paired political prompts; Helpfulness Consistency measures symmetric depth and engagement. To reduce both types of covert bias, we introduce Political Consistency Training (PCT), an RL training method with two complementary paradigms: Sentiment Consistency Training and Helpfulness Consistency Training. We show that PCT preserves overall helpfulness, substantially reduces covert political bias, and generalizes to held-out benchmarks. We release our work at https://political-manipulation.ai

</details>


### [59] [Evaluating Commercial AI Chatbots as News Intermediaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22785)
*Mirac Suzgun,Emily Shen,Federico Bianchi,Alexander Spangher,Thomas Icard,Daniel E. Ho,Dan Jurafsky,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对六大AI聊天机器人（Gemini 3 Flash/Pro、Grok 4、Claude 4.5 Sonnet、GPT-5、GPT-4o mini）在14天内针对2100个来自BBC新闻的事实性问题进行了系统评估。发现最佳系统在多选题上准确率超90%，但自由回答下降11-13%；所有模型在印地语上准确率最低（79%），且存在英语检索偏见；超过70%的错误源于检索失败而非推理失败；当问题包含虚假前提时，准确率从88-96%骤降至19-70%。研究揭示了高准确率可能掩盖区域不平等、对检索基础设施的依赖及对不完美查询的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尚无研究系统衡量AI聊天机器人（含专有搜索集成和检索-合成流水线）在不同语言和地区间处理新兴事实的准确性，这一空白在AI迅速改变新闻获取方式的背景下亟需填补。

Method: 在2026年2月9-22日期间，对六个AI聊天机器人进行14天评估。问题来源于同日BBC新闻六个区域服务（美加、阿拉伯、非洲、印地语、俄语、土耳其语）的2100个事实性问题。采用多项选择、自由回答及包含虚假前提的问题等多种评估方式，并分析错误模式（检索vs推理失败、语言偏见、citation来源）。

Result: 最佳系统多项选择准确率超90%，但自由回答下降11-13%；全队列下降16-17%。所有模型印地语准确率最低（79% vs 其他89-91%），且存在Anglophone检索偏见。超过70%错误源于检索失败。虚假前提问题导致准确率从88-96%骤降至19-70%。存在检测-准确率悖论：最佳虚假前提检测器在对抗准确率方面排第二。

Conclusion: 高准确率可能掩盖系统性区域不平等、对检索基础设施的近乎完全依赖以及对不完美查询的脆弱性。虚假前提检测与答案恢复是部分独立的能力。

Abstract: AI chatbots are rapidly shaping how people encounter the news, yet no prior study has systematically measured how accurately these systems, with their proprietary search integrations and retrieval-synthesis pipelines, handle emerging facts across languages and regions. We present a 14-day (February 9-22, 2026) evaluation of six AI chatbots (Gemini 3 Flash and Pro, Grok 4, Claude 4.5 Sonnet, GPT-5 and GPT-4o mini) on 2,100 factual questions derived from same-day BBC News reporting across six regional services (US & Canada, Arabic, Afrique, Hindi, Russian, Turkish). The best systems achieve over 90% multiple-choice accuracy on questions about events reported hours earlier. The same systems, however, lose 11-13% under free-response evaluation, and 16-17% across the cohort. We further characterize three failure patterns. First, every model achieves its lowest accuracy on Hindi (79% vs. 89-91% elsewhere) and citations indicate an Anglophone retrieval bias (e.g., models answering Hindi queries cite English Wikipedia more than any Hindi outlet). Second, retrieval, not reasoning, failures drive over 70% of all errors. When models retrieve a correct source, they often extract the correct answer; the problem is to land on the right source in the first place. Third, models achieving 88-96% accuracy on well-formed questions drop to 19-70% when questions contain subtle false premises, with the most vulnerable model accepting fabricated facts 64% of the time. We also identify a detection-accuracy paradox: the best false-premise detector ranks second in adversarial accuracy (abstention rate), while a weaker detector ranks first, showing that premise detection and answer recovery are partially independent capabilities. Overall, these suggest that high accuracy can mask systematic regional inequity, near-total dependence on retrieval infrastructure, and vulnerability to imperfect queries real users pose.

</details>


### [60] [Tokenisation via Convex Relaxations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22821)
*Jan Tempus,Philip Whittington,Craig W. Schmidt,Dennis Komm,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConvexTok，将分词器构建形式化为线性规划问题并用凸优化工具求解，相比传统贪婪算法（如BPE、Unigram）能获得更优的分词质量，且可证明与最优解的偏差在1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分词算法（BPE、Unigram）本质上是贪心算法，只做局部最优决策而不考虑整体词表质量，缺乏全局最优性保证。

Method: 将分词器构建建模为线性规划问题，使用凸优化工具求解，提出ConvexTok算法。

Result: ConvexTok在内在分词指标和语言模型的bits-per-byte（BpB）上持续提升；在下游任务性能上也有提升但不够稳定；经验上在常见词表大小下与最优解的偏差在1%以内。

Conclusion: ConvexTok提供了一种可证明近优的分词器构建方法，并允许用户通过下界量化当前分词器与最优解的差距。

Abstract: Tokenisation is an integral part of the current NLP pipeline. Current tokenisation algorithms such as BPE and Unigram are greedy algorithms -- they make locally optimal decisions without considering the resulting vocabulary as a whole. We instead formulate tokeniser construction as a linear program and solve it using convex optimisation tools, yielding a new algorithm we call ConvexTok. We find ConvexTok consistently improves intrinsic tokenisation metrics and the bits-per-byte (BpB) achieved by language models; it also improves downstream task performance, but less consistently. Furthermore, ConvexTok allows the user to certify how far their tokeniser is from optimal, with respect to a certain objective, via a lower bound, and we empirically find it to be within 1\% of optimal at common vocabulary sizes.

</details>

### [61] [Autonomous LLM Agents & CTFs: A Second Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21497)
*Youness Bouchari,Matteo Boffa,Marco Mellia,Idilio Drago,Thanh Minh Bui,Dario Rossi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了LLM智能体在自动化攻击性安全任务中的表现，设计多种架构在30个Web CTF挑战上进行评估，发现通用智能体claude-code与专门架构性能相当（19/30），两者在相同类别上均存在困难，且结构化编排优于单体设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究声称LLM智能体在CTF挑战中达到接近人类水平的成功率，本文旨在重新审视这些结果，通过更系统的实验设计验证这些结论的可靠性。

Method: 设计多种不同复杂度和模块化程度的智能体架构，在涵盖14种漏洞类别的30个Web CTF挑战上进行评估，以多个LLM为后端，并与通用智能体claude-code进行对比分析。

Result: 1) claude-code与专门架构性能相当（19/30）；2) 所有架构在相同挑战类别上均表现不佳，未能达到人类水平；3) 结构化编排的专业角色架构优于单体设计，运行一致性和执行成本均有改善。

Conclusion: 通用智能体可作为攻击性安全任务的强基线，当前LLM智能体仍存在系统性瓶颈，结构化多角色编排是提升性能的有效方向。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed to automate offensive security tasks, with recent studies reporting near human-level success rates in Capture-the-Flag (CTF) challenges. We here revisit these results, providing a second look at these claims. We engineer different agent architectures of increasing complexity and modularity on 30 web-based CTFs challenges spanning 14 vulnerability classes. We instantiate these agents with multiple LLM backbones, and compare them with claude-code, a general-purpose agent that automatically determines its internal architecture. Our evaluation yields three main findings. First, claude-code achieves performance comparable to the engineered architectures (19/30 solved tasks), suggesting that general-purpose agents are strong baselines for offensive security tasks. Second, both our architectures and claude-code struggle in the same challenge categories, revealing persistent barriers that keep current agents below human-level capability. Third, by leveraging our manually designed architectures we can systematically measure the impact of additional components, finding that structured orchestration of specialized roles outperforms monolithic designs, improving run-to-run consistency, and reducing execution costs.

</details>


### [62] [Chain Reactions: How Nonce Collisions in ECDSA Compromise Polygon MEV Searchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21498)
*Yash Madhwal,Andrey Seoev,Raffaele Della Pietra,Anastasiia Smirnova,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Polygon MEV生态系统中由于非重用的关键漏洞，攻击者可通过代数方法恢复私钥，并提供了线性系统攻击公式和链上证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECDSA签名安全性依赖于正确的随机数生成，而Polygon MEV生态系统中搜索者为满足密封投标拍卖的亚秒级响应需求，使用了可预测的随机数模式，导致严重安全漏洞。

Method: 通过链上数据分析，发现随机数重用导致签名间存在线性关系；建立了紧凑的线性系统攻击公式（涵盖跨钱包随机数碰撞场景），并实施被动攻击恢复私钥。

Result: 发现了Polygon上可被利用的签名模式，证明协议引入的延迟压力会导致生产区块链系统中灾难性的密码学失败，单个实现错误可同时危害多个账户。

Conclusion: 协议层面的延迟压力（如MEV拍卖机制）可间接引发底层密码学原语的灾难性失效，亟待改进随机数生成策略和协议设计。

Abstract: ECDSA signatures form the bedrock of blockchain transaction authentication, yet their security critically depends on proper nonce generation. We uncover a critical vulnerability in the Polygon MEV ecosystem: systematic nonce reuse that enables complete private key recovery. Analyzing on-chain data reveals that searchers, driven by the need for sub-second response times in sealed-bid auctions, employ predictable nonce patterns. These patterns create linear relationships between signatures, allowing passive attackers to recover private keys using elementary algebra. We provide a compact linear-system formulation for such attacks, including the dangerous case of cross-wallet nonce collisions, and present concrete evidence of exploitable patterns on Polygon. Our findings demonstrate how protocol-induced latency pressures can lead to catastrophic cryptographic failures in production blockchain systems, where a single implementation error compromises multiple accounts simultaneously.

</details>


### [63] [ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY: A Cross-Build Binary Dataset with Temporal Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21615)
*Chang Liu,Noah Fleischmann,Nicolò Altamura,Edward Raff,James Holt,Kristopher Micinski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY，一个包含73,610个二进制文件的多维度数据集，整合了跨编译器、跨优化等级、跨平台、多年份历史构建以及CVE标签，并通过三项分析展示了其在二进制漏洞分析和相似性研究中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制语料库仅覆盖单个或两个变异维度（如跨编译器但无时间轴，或有CVE标签但为单构建），缺乏同时包含跨构建多样性、跨版本历史和CVE标签的可查询结构。

Method: 构建包含73,610个二进制文件的统一框架，覆盖248个开源项目，跨GCC/Clang/MSVC编译器、多优化等级、Linux/Windows平台及多年历史构建；每个二进制文件的编译上下文、源代码、函数、调试信息、变体构建、历史版本和易受攻击函数均作为一等元数据存储于数据库中。

Result: 三项分析验证了该结构的价值：(1)三阶段LLM基准测试（识别、策略引导检测、跨构建迁移）测试LLM是否真正推理漏洞还是匹配构建特定伪影；(2)比较MalConv、jTrans和TLSH嵌入，量化同版本包在各空间的聚类效果；(3)贝叶斯回归分解二进制相似性中时间距离、文件变更和提交数量的贡献。

Conclusion: ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY填补了现有二进制语料库在多维度整合上的空白，为二进制漏洞分析和相似性研究提供了结构化的统一框架。

Abstract: Existing binary corpora typically capture only one or two axes of binary variation: they either provide cross-compiler builds without a temporal axis, or CVE labels for single-build binaries. None combine cross-build diversity, cross-version history, and CVE labels into a queryable structure. We present ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY, which consolidates these dimensions into a unified framework where every binary's compilation context, source code, vulnerable functions, and package version are stored as first-class metadata.
  ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY comprises 73,610 binaries spanning 248 open-source projects, compiled across GCC, Clang, and MSVC at multiple optimization levels on Linux and Windows, with multi-year historical builds. Each binary is indexed in a database that links it to its source code, functions, debug info, variant builds, historical versions, and vulnerable functions. Three analyses demonstrate this structure's value: (1) a three-stage LLM benchmark (recognition, strategy-guided detection, and cross-build transfer) to test whether LLMs reason about binary vulnerabilities or pattern-match on build-specific artifacts; (2) a comparison of MalConv embeddings, jTrans function embeddings, and TLSH fuzzy hashes quantifying how same-package versions cluster in each space; and (3) a Bayesian regression decomposing binary similarity into contributions from temporal distance, file changes, and commits.

</details>


### [64] [Adversarial Reframing: A Framework for Targeted Generation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21674)
*Shahnewaz Karim Sakib,Swati Kar,Anindya Bijoy Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: THREAT是一个推理驱动的多LLM协作框架，通过将提示发现形式化为非凸优化问题来高效生成越狱攻击提示，在更低的计算成本下实现更高的攻击成功率，且攻击提示被检测为有害的比例不足1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM虽然广泛部署但仍易受到越狱攻击（通过提示绕过安全过滤器），需要更高效的自动化方法来发现这些安全漏洞，以主动加强基础模型的安全性。

Method: 提出THREAT框架，将提示发现形式化为非凸优化问题，通过协调多个LLM在迭代搜索循环中进行推理驱动的文本越狱提示生成，并提供高效求解方案降低运行时间。

Result: THREAT在多个数据集和模型架构上实现了更高的攻击成功率和更低的计算成本；生成的攻击提示仅不到1%被标记为有害，而对应未修改的提示约有50%被拒绝，揭示了之前未检测到的对齐LLM漏洞。

Conclusion: THREAT可作为一种实用工具，用于主动加强基础模型的安全性，发现现有安全对齐机制中的隐蔽漏洞。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely deployed in diverse real-world settings, yet remain vulnerable to jailbreaking, where prompt-based attacks bypass safety filters. We present THREAT (Targeted Harmful generation via Reframing and Exploitation of Adversarial Tactics), a reasoning-driven framework that coordinates multiple LLMs in an iterative search loop to find textual jailbreak prompts. We formulate prompt discovery as a nonconvex optimization problem and provide an efficient solution that lowers runtime and improves attack effectiveness. Across diverse datasets and model architectures, THREAT delivers higher attack success rates with lower computational cost than prior methods. The crafted prompts were flagged as harmful in fewer than 1% of cases, compared with about 50% refusals for the corresponding unmodified prompts. These findings reveal previously undetected vulnerabilities in aligned LLMs and position THREAT as a practical tool for proactively strengthening the safety of foundation models.

</details>


### [65] [FuzzingBrain V2: A Multi-Agent LLM System for Automated Vulnerability Discovery and Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21779)
*Ze Sheng,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Kewen Zhu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FuzzingBrain V2是一个基于多智能体系统的自动化漏洞分析工具，结合OSS-Fuzz实现可复现验证，通过Suspicious Point抽象、分层函数分析、双层面模糊测试及MCP工具实现精确漏洞定位与复杂漏洞推理，在AIxCC 2025比赛中达到90%检测率，并在实际部署中发现29个零日漏洞（含2个CVE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞检测存在三大问题：高误报率且无法可复现验证、漏洞定位粒度不优（函数级遗漏bug、行级缺乏上下文）、难以推理跨函数依赖和复杂触发条件的漏洞。

Method: (1) 基于Google OSS-Fuzz的全自动化分析，确保漏洞可复现；(2) 提出Suspicious Point，基于控制流的抽象实现最优粒度漏洞定位；(3) 逻辑驱动的分层函数分析与双层面模糊测试，提升资源约束下的函数覆盖；(4) 基于MCP的静态与动态分析工具结合上下文工程，增强复杂漏洞推理能力。

Result: 在AIxCC 2025决赛C/C++数据集上达到90%检测率（40个漏洞检出36个）；实际部署中发现29个零日漏洞，跨12个开源项目，全部被维护者确认并修复，其中2个已分配CVE编号。

Conclusion: FuzzingBrain V2通过多智能体协作、可控粒度定位与模糊测试验证，有效解决了LLM在漏洞检测中的高误报、粒度不当和复杂推理难题，展示了自动化漏洞发现的实际可行性。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities pose critical security threats, with nearly 50,000 CVEs reported in 2025. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for automated vulnerability detection, three key challenges remain. First, LLM-generated vulnerability reports suffer from high false positive rates and lack
  reproducible verification. Second, existing LLM-based approaches use suboptimal granularities for vulnerability localization: function-level analysis overlooks bugs when context becomes extensive, while line-level analysis lacks sufficient context. Third, existing approaches have difficulty reasoning about
  vulnerabilities with complex cross-function dependencies and triggering conditions.
  We present FuzzingBrain V2, a multi-agent system that addresses these gaps through four key contributions: (1) fully automated vulnerability analysis built on Google's OSS-Fuzz, ensuring all reported vulnerabilities are fuzzer-reproducible; (2) Suspicious Point, a novel control-flow-based abstraction for precise
  vulnerability localization at the optimal granularity; (3) logic-driven hierarchical function analysis with dual-layer fuzzing enhancing function coverage under resource constraints; (4) MCP-based static and dynamic analysis tools with context engineering enhancing complex vulnerability reasoning.
  On the AIxCC 2025 Final Competition C/C++ dataset, FuzzingBrain V2 achieved 90% detection rate (36 of 40 vulnerabilities). In real-world deployment, FuzzingBrain V2 discovered 29 zero-day vulnerabilities across 12 open-source projects, all confirmed and fixed by maintainers, with 2 assigned CVE IDs.

</details>


### [66] [Polars inside Intel SGX2 Enclaves: An Empirical Study of Confidential Analytical Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21797)
*Wei Wang,Burns Smith,Kenny Leftin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于Arrow-native的DataFrame引擎Polars在Intel SGX2（通过Gramine）上的性能，使用TPC-H SF30基准和Azure Blob Storage。端到端开销约为1.49-1.56×，但查询开销（1.43-1.52×）与表加载开销（2.27-4.07×）呈现不同趋势；惰性API比急切API快2.25-2.27×；部分查询存在EPC压力导致的运行间波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TEE机密分析研究多聚焦于SQL数据库引擎或早期SGX一代，缺乏对现代Arrow-native DataFrame引擎（如Polars）在SGX2上的系统评估，且未充分分离计算开销与数据摄入开销的差异化影响。

Method: 在Intel SGX2 enclave中通过Gramine运行Polars引擎，使用TPC-H SF30基准测试，数据集宽度配置约22-73 GB，结合Azure Blob Storage。采用标准TPC-H power score和去除表加载时间的查询-only变体，比较惰性与急切API性能。

Result: 端到端开销1.49-1.56×近似恒定；查询开销从1.51-1.52×降至1.43-1.44×；表加载开销从2.27×升至4.07×。中位per-query SGX减速1.45×，最大2.57×。惰性API比急切API快2.25-2.27×，急切API在≥41 GB时发生OOM错误。

Conclusion: SGX2能够支持Arrow-native分析处理，安全开销与现有DuckDB-SGX2研究处于同一量级；但加载路径放大和API级优化是端到端性能的首要决定因素。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have renewed interest in confidential analytics, but most prior evaluations focus on SQL database engines or earlier SGX generations. This paper studies an Arrow-native DataFrame engine, Polars, running inside Intel SGX2 enclaves via Gramine on TPC-H SF30 with Azure Blob Storage. We report both the standard TPC-H power score and a query-only variant that removes table-loading time in order to separate compute overhead from data-ingestion overhead. Across four dataset-width configurations (approximately 22-73 GB), end-to-end overhead remains nearly constant at 1.49-1.56$\times$, but this composite metric obscures two distinct behaviors: query-only overhead declines from 1.51-1.52$\times$ to 1.43-1.44$\times$, whereas table-loading overhead rises from 2.27$\times$ to 4.07$\times$. We further show that overhead is not uniform across queries: for the len130 configuration, the median per-query SGX slowdown is 1.45$\times$ with a maximum of 2.57$\times$, and a small set of queries exhibits pronounced run-to-run spikes consistent with stateful EPC pressure. Finally, we compare Polars' lazy and eager APIs under the same TEE setting. Lazy execution is 2.25-2.27$\times$ faster overall, while eager execution fails with out-of-memory errors at 41 GB and above. Relative to the recent DuckDB-SGX2 study, our results suggest that SGX2 can support Arrow-native analytical processing with a similar order of security overhead, but that load-path amplification and API-level optimization are first-order determinants of end-to-end performance.

</details>


### [67] [Graph Structure of Chebyshev Permutation Polynomials over Binary and Ternary Adic Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21819)
*Xiaoxiong Lu,Yuling Dai,Chengqing Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了环$\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$上Chebyshev置换多项式的函数图结构，给出了路径长度和圈结构的显式刻画，证明了图具有强规律性，包括固定长度的常数量圈和可预测的分支模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限域上非线性映射的函数图对于分析其动力学复杂性及在密码学和伪随机生成中的潜在应用至关重要。

Method: 基于Chebyshev多项式在2和3的幂次模下新的性质，对环$\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$（其中$k_1,k_2$为正整数且$0\in\{k_1,k_2\}$）上的函数图结构进行分析。

Result: 给出了路径长度和圈结构的显式刻画；证明函数图具有强规律性，包括固定长度的常数量圈以及随$k_1,k_2$增长可预测的分支模式。

Conclusion: 结果将先前在素幂环上的研究进行了扩展，为数字非线性映射中复杂性的涌现提供了见解，并支持其密码应用的安性分析。

Abstract: Understanding the functional graph of a nonlinear map over a finite domain is crucial for analyzing its dynamical complexity and potential applications in cryptography and pseudorandom generation. In this paper, we investigate the graph structure of Chebyshev permutation polynomials over the ring $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$, where $k_1$ and $k_2$ are positive integers and $0\in\{k_1, k_2\}$. Each element of the ring is regarded as a vertex, and the mapping relation defined by the polynomial corresponds to a directed edge. Building on new properties of Chebyshev polynomials modulo powers of $2$ and $3$, we provide an explicit characterization of path lengths and cycle structures in the functional graph. We show that, despite the complexities introduced by the binary and ternary components, the graph exhibits strong regularities, including a constant number of cycles of a given length and predictable branching patterns as $k_1$ and $k_2$ increase. Our results extend previous studies over prime-power rings, offering insights into the emergence of complexity in digital nonlinear maps and supporting the security analysis of their cryptographic applications.

</details>


### [68] [A Large Language Model Approach to Generating Bypass Rules for Malware Evasion in Analysis Sandbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21821)
*Zhiyong Sui,Lamine Noureddine,Mst Eshita Khatun,Sideeq Bello,Justin Woodring,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ABLE系统，利用大语言模型自动生成YARA规则以绕过沙箱环境中的逃逸检测，在334个真实恶意软件样本上实现了79%的绕过成功率，相比现有分析平台多识别出47%的恶意软件家族分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶意软件沙箱逃逸检测依赖人工逆向工程手工编写绕过规则，无法应对快速演变的逃逸技术，亟需自动化、可扩展的绕过规则生成方法。

Method: ABLE利用大语言模型分析因潜在逃避行为而终止的恶意软件执行轨迹，采用多种推理策略生成针对性YARA绕过规则；引入自动净化管道和基于反馈的迭代优化机制，修复LLM输出中的语法错误并提升规则有效性。

Result: 在334个真实恶意软件样本和4个开源LLM上测试，ABLE达到79%绕过成功率，其中迭代优化贡献了29.5%的成功案例；相比现有分析平台，ABLE多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类并暴露了之前隐藏的行为。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的自动化YARA规则生成方法能有效应对沙箱逃逸检测的规模化挑战，显著提升恶意软件分析的覆盖率和深度。

Abstract: Sandbox evasion remains a critical challenge for automated malware analysis, as modern malware employs environment checks to detect analysis platforms and suppress malicious behavior. Existing approaches rely on manually crafted bypass rules that require deep reverse engineering of each evasion mechanism -an approach that cannot scale against rapidly evolving evasion techniques. In this paper, we leverage large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate YARA rules that bypass evasion checks in sandbox environments. We propose ABLE, which analyzes execution traces from malware terminated due to potentially evasive behavior and employs multiple reasoning strategies to generate targeted bypass rules. To address syntactic errors and improve the efficacy of the bypass rules in the LLM outputs, we introduce an auto-sanitization pipeline and feedback-driven iterative refinement. We evaluate ABLE on 334 real-world malware samples across four open-weight LLMs. ABLE achieves a 79% bypass success rate, with iterative refinement contributing 29.5% of successful cases. Compared to existing analysis platforms, ABLE identifies 47% more malware family classifications and exposes previously hidden behaviors.

</details>


### [69] [Quality-Assured Fuzz Harness Generation via the Four Principles Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21824)
*Ze Sheng,Dmitrijs Trizna,Luigino Camastra,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: QuartetFuzz 是一个基于四原则框架（P1-P4）的自动化 harness 生成系统，通过 LLM 驱动的生成-检查-修复循环确保 harness 的正确性。在 23 个开源项目中发现 42 个 bug（29 个已确认/修复，含 3 个 CVE），误报率仅 4.8%，并在 586 个现有 harness 中识别出 53 个违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 fuzz harness 自动生成工具缺乏系统性的正确性保障，逻辑错误、API 误用和生命周期违规在源码层面无法被检测。随着 LLM 驱动的规模化 harness 生成，不受控的质量问题使得规模优势变成负担。

Method: 提出四原则框架：逻辑正确性（P1）、API 协议合规（P2）、安全边界尊重（P3）和入口点充分性（P4），这是首个在源码层面定义 harness 正确性的数学规范。基于该框架构建自主 LLM 智能体，通过生成-检查-修复循环在 fuzzing 之前确保 harness 满足 P1-P4。

Result: 在 23 个开源项目（C/C++、Java、JavaScript）中提交 42 个 bug 报告，29 个被确认/修复（含 3 个 CVE），仅 2 个被拒绝（4.8% 误报率）。内置的 P1/P2 检查自动拦截了 58 个本会导致误报的 harness 引发崩溃。在 586 个生产 harness 中识别 53 个违规（45 个已确认，35 个已修复）。

Conclusion: QuartetFuzz 通过形式化的正确性定义和自动化质量保障循环，显著提升了 fuzz harness 的生成质量，并能作为质量审计工具发现现有 harness 中的违规问题，证明了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Fuzz testing is the dominant technique for finding memory-safety vulnerabilities in C/C++ software, yet its effectiveness hinges on the quality of fuzz harnesses -- the programs that bridge fuzzers and library APIs. A growing body of tools now automate harness generation, but none systematically ensures the correctness of produced harnesses: logic errors, API misuse, and lifecycle violations go undetected at the source level. As LLM-driven generation scales harness creation, uncontrolled quality turns scale into a liability.
  We present QuartetFuzz, an autonomous harness-generation system that systematically improves correctness throughout the generation process. At its core is the Four Principles framework -- Logic Correctness (P1), API Protocol Compliance (P2), Security Boundary Respect (P3), and Entry Point Adequacy (P4) -- the first source-level definition of harness correctness with mathematical specifications and implementable checks. We operationalize these principles in an autonomous LLM agent that produces harnesses satisfying P1-P4 through a generate-check-fix loop before any fuzzing begins.
  Deployed on 23 open-source projects spanning C/C++, Java, and JavaScript, the system submits 42 bug reports, of which 29 are fixed or confirmed upstream (including 3 CVEs) and only 2 are rejected (4.8% FP rate). During generation, the built-in P1/P2 checks automatically intercepted 58 harness-induced crashes that would otherwise have been false positives. Applied as a quality auditor to 586 existing production harnesses across 70 projects, the system identifies 53 violations (45 confirmed, 35 fixed). We release a dataset of 100 labeled harnesses for reproducible evaluation. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/OwenSanzas/QuartetFuzz

</details>


### [70] [SPIDER: Two Server Functionality for the Cost of Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21857)
*Ofir Dvir,Kali Hale,Javin Zipkin,Divyakant Agrawal,Dahlia Malkhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种私有信息检索（PIR）方案：baseSPIDER（单服务器、有状态客户端，通过预处理和提示匹配最优渐近通信复杂度并改善常数因子）和SPIDER（首个无需服务器协作、默认数据库接口的单服务器PIR方案，通过简单转换baseSPIDER实现，可推广至其他PIR方案）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单服务器PIR方案要么需要服务器特殊支持（如专用API、协议特定交互），要么在通信复杂度常数因子上不够优化，部署门槛高。本文旨在设计一种无需服务器协作、可直接应用于现有系统的PIR方案，同时优化通信效率。

Method: 1. 设计baseSPIDER：单服务器+有状态客户端，客户端进行预处理并存储提示（hints），实现渐进最优通信复杂度并改善常数因子。2. 通过简单转换将baseSPIDER适配为SPIDER：无需服务器任何协作，仅依赖默认数据库索引访问接口。3. 将该转换方法推广至三种现有PIR方案，使其适应默认服务器范式。

Result: baseSPIDER匹配了最先进方案的渐进最优通信复杂度并改进了常数因子，特别适用于大条目数据库。SPIDER是首个不需要服务器提供专用API、辅助状态或协议特定交互的单服务器PIR方案。转换方法可推广至其他PIR方案，SPIDER相比这些改进方案设计更简单，但客户端计算开销更高。

Conclusion: 本文通过baseSPIDER和SPIDER推动了PIR的实际部署：baseSPIDER在效率上取得常数因子改进，SPIDER消除了服务器端部署障碍，使得PIR可直接应用于现有系统。提出的转换方法具有通用性，为其他PIR方案提供了适配路径。

Abstract: We introduce baseSPIDER and SPIDER, private information retrieval (PIR) schemes that embody two technical advancements. The baseSPIDER protocol operates with a single server and a stateful client that performs pre-processing and stores hints for future queries. In this setting, baseSPIDER introduces a new approach that matches the asymptotically optimal communication complexity of state-of-the-art schemes while improving constant factors--an advantage that is particularly significant for databases with large entries. In addition, baseSPIDER offers a conceptually simpler design relative to prior protocols. SPIDER operates over a default database interface and requires no cooperation from the server at any stage. To our knowledge, SPIDER is the first single-server PIR construction of this design, achieving privacy without specialized APIs, auxiliary server state, or protocol-specific interaction beyond conventional indexed access. SPIDER is built via a simple transformation of baseSPIDER to the default server setting, eliminating deployment barriers and enabling immediate applicability to existing systems. This transformation can be applied more broadly to three recent PIR solutions, adapting them for use in the default-server paradigm and yielding solutions of independent interest. SPIDER compares to the resulting modified solutions by exhibiting a simpler design while incurring higher client computational work.

</details>


### [71] [Blind Spots in the Guard: How Domain-Camouflaged Injection Attacks Evade Detection in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22001)
*Aaditya Pai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现并形式化了LLM智能体注入检测中的一个系统性盲点：当注入载荷伪装成目标文档的领域词汇和权威结构时（即领域伪装注入），标准检测器的检测率从93.8%骤降至9.7%（Llama 3.1 8B）和从100%降至55.6%（Gemini 2.0 Flash）。该漏洞被定义为伪装检测差距（CDG），并在45个任务中验证其统计显著性。专用安全分类器Llama Guard 3完全无法检测伪装载荷。多智能体辩论架构会放大静态注入攻击，而针对性检测增强只能提供部分修复，表明较弱模型上的漏洞是架构性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注入检测器基于静态模板载荷进行校准，无法识别模仿目标文档领域词汇和权威结构的伪装注入载荷，存在系统性盲点。

Method: 形式化定义伪装检测差距（CDG）；跨45个任务、三个领域、两个模型家族（Llama 3.1 8B, Gemini 2.0 Flash）进行基准测试；评估Llama Guard 3安全分类器；测试多智能体辩论架构下的攻击放大效应；评估针对性检测增强的修复效果。

Result: 静态检测率93.8%→9.7%（Llama）、100%→55.6%（Gemini）；CDG统计显著（Llama: χ²=38.03; Gemini: χ²=17.05, p<0.001）；Llama Guard 3检测率为零；多智能体辩论对较小模型放大攻击达9.9倍；针对性增强仅部分修复（Llama提升10.2%, Gemini提升78.7%）。

Conclusion: 领域伪装注入是现有检测器的系统性盲点，较弱模型上的漏洞具有架构性而非偶然性，威胁LLM智能体的安全部署。

Abstract: Injection detectors deployed to protect LLM agents are calibrated on static, template-based payloads that announce themselves as override directives. We identify a systematic blind spot: when payloads are generated to mimic the domain vocabulary and authority structures of the target document, what we call domain camouflaged injection, standard detectors fail to flag them, with detection rates dropping from 93.8% to 9.7% on Llama 3.1 8B and from 100% to 55.6% on Gemini 2.0 Flash. We formalize this as the Camouflage Detection Gap (CDG), the difference in injection detection rate between static and camouflaged payloads. Across 45 tasks spanning three domains and two model families, CDG is large and statistically significant (chi^2 = 38.03, p < 0.001 for Llama; chi^2 = 17.05, p < 0.001 for Gemini), with zero reverse discordant pairs in either case. We additionally evaluate Llama Guard 3, a production safety classifier, which detects zero camouflage payloads (IDRcamouflage = 0.000), confirming that the blind spot extends beyond few-shot detectors to dedicated safety classifiers. We further show that multi-agent debate architectures amplify static injection attacks by up to 9.9x on smaller models, while stronger models show collective resistance. Targeted detector augmentation provides only partial remediation (10.2% improvement on Llama, 78.7% on Gemini), suggesting the vulnerability is architectural rather than incidental for weaker models. Our framework, task bank, and payload generator are released publicly.

</details>


### [72] [Parser-Free Querying of Security Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22027)
*Evan Luo,Julien Piet,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Sieve是一个利用大语言模型从自然语言安全查询自动生成可执行代码的系统，通过轻量级自动提取的日志格式上下文，只需一次LLM调用加确定性执行即可完成查询。在133个跨5种日志类型的查询评估中，相比人工脚本编写，复杂时态和跨事件查询的错误率降低了3倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分析师日常查询系统日志时面临两难：构建结构化解析器成本高昂需持续工程投入，而直接使用grep等工具查询原始日志又无法表达安全调查所需的多行时态查询。现有方法无法兼顾表达力和即用性。

Method: Sieve利用大语言模型，通过轻量级自动提取的日志格式上下文来引导模型，仅需一次LLM调用即可从自然语言安全问题生成可执行查询代码，随后进行确定性执行。

Result: 在133个跨5种日志类型的安全查询评估中，Sieve在复杂时态和跨事件查询上相比人工脚本编写实现了超过3倍的错误率降低，尤其是在主动调查中最关键的多行关联任务上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: LLM生成的代码能够弥合结构化日志查询的表达力与直接处理原始文件的即用性之间的差距，为安全分析提供了一种兼顾两者的新方案。

Abstract: Security analysts routinely query system logs to detect threats and investigate incidents, but each log source uses its own semi-structured format: logs are cheap to produce, but expensive to use. The standard approach, building per-source parsers to normalize logs into structured schemas, is powerful but requires continuous engineering effort for each new format. Querying raw logs directly with tools like grep avoids this cost, but requires analysts to know each source's message variants and cannot express the multi-line temporal queries that security investigations demand. We present Sieve, a system that generates executable query code from natural-language security questions by grounding a large language model with lightweight, automatically extracted log-format context, requiring only one LLM call per query followed by deterministic execution. Evaluating 133 security queries across 5 log types, we find that Sieve achieves over a 3x reduction in error rate on complex temporal and cross-event queries compared to manual analyst scripting, with the largest gains on the multi-line correlation tasks most critical to active investigations. Our results and benchmark provide evidence that LLM-generated code can bridge the gap between the expressiveness of structured log querying and the immediacy of working directly with raw files.

</details>


### [73] [Safeguarding Text-to-Image Generative Models Against Unauthorized Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22060)
*Yilan Gao,Sida Huang,Hongyuan Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WaveGuard是一种基于生成器的单次保护框架，通过对合成图像注入频率感知的不可感知扰动，在保持视觉质量的同时降低其作为训练数据对未授权学生模型的价值，从而防御通过查询API进行的模型窃取攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于查询API部署的闭权重生成服务容易遭受模型窃取攻击——攻击者通过收集大量合成输出图像来训练替代模型，实现未授权的知识蒸馏和能力复制。现有防御方法需要在保持图像视觉保真度、提供显式扰动幅度控制以及高效扩展到大规模输出发布之间取得平衡，这是当前亟需解决的问题。

Method: WaveGuard提出单次、基于生成器的保护框架，在用户指定的扰动预算下工作。它采用频率感知的扰动生成器，注入结构化、不可感知的扰动，使得受保护图像对普通观众保持视觉可用性，同时降低其作为未授权学生模型训练数据的效用。

Result: 在WikiArt相关的合成输出蒸馏场景下进行的大量实验表明，WaveGuard实现了良好的效能-保真度-效率权衡，具备显式的不可感知性控制，并在保护效率方面取得了实质性提升。

Conclusion: WaveGuard通过频率感知的扰动注入策略，在防御模型窃取的同时有效平衡了图像质量、扰动控制和计算效率，为闭权重生成服务提供了一种实用的保护方案。

Abstract: Closed-weight generative services are increasingly deployed through query-based APIs, where users can obtain generated outputs while model parameters remain inaccessible. However, such deployment does not prevent model stealing: an attacker can repeatedly query the service, collect large volumes of released synthetic images, and use them as training data for a private substitute model. This query-output-driven process enables unauthorized knowledge distillation and capability replication without direct access to the original weights.
