To mitigate this threat, a practical defense should preserve the visual fidelity of released images, provide explicit control over perturbation magnitude, and scale efficiently to large-volume output release. We present WaveGuard, a single-pass, generator-based protection framework that safeguards released synthetic images under a user-specified perturbation budget. WaveGuard employs a frequency-aware perturbation generator to inject structured, imperceptible perturbations that maintain perceptual utility for benign viewers while reducing the usefulness of protected images as training data for unauthorized student models. Extensive experiments under WikiArt-related synthetic-output distillation settings show that WaveGuard achieves a favorable efficacy--fidelity--efficiency trade-off, with explicit imperceptibility control and substantial gains in protection efficiency.

</details>


### [74] [QT-PUF: Quantum Tunneling Leakage Based PUF for Implantable IoMT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22113)
*Yueqi Ma,Vivek Mohan,Chip-Hong Chang,Emmanuel M. Drakakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出QT-PUF，一种基于量子隧穿栅极泄漏的物理不可克隆函数，用于IoMT设备身份认证。采用伪电阻I-V前端差分读出电路，在65nm CMOS下实现0.9999998熵值、96.04 nW/bit功耗，并在植入式器件工作条件下BER低于0.000163。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoMT设备面临物理篡改和仿冒威胁，现有PUF（存储器型、环形振荡器型、仲裁器型）因功耗高、需要外部激励或稳定性差，不适合超低功耗的植入式/可穿戴医疗设备。

Method: 利用标准CMOS工艺中量子隧穿栅极泄漏电流的工艺变异作为熵源，设计伪电阻I-V转换前端实现皮安级电流的差分读出，无需外部激励或稳定电路，静态偏置即可工作。

Result: 65nm CMOS仿真：熵值0.9999998，片间汉明距离0.5001，平均功耗96.04 nW/bit（19.21 fJ/bit），在1.0-1.3V和10-70°C范围内平均BER低于0.000163。

Conclusion: QT-PUF凭借极低功耗、高可靠性和无需外部激励的特性，适用于资源受限的植入式IoMT设备的安全认证场景。

Abstract: The Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) marks a shift toward decentralized healthcare, enabling continuous monitoring and personalized care through connected wearable and implantable devices. However, ensuring the trust and integrity of these devices themselves remains a major challenge, as physical compromise or counterfeiting can directly endanger patient safety, privacy, and data integrity. This work presents QT-PUF, a gate-tunneling-leakage-based physical unclonable function (PUF) that leverages quantum-mechanical gate leakage resulting from process-induced variations in standard CMOS devices. A differential readout circuit with a pseudo-resistor I-to-V frontend is proposed to convert the picoampere-level leakage variations into digital responses. Unlike existing PUFs such as those based on memory, ring oscillators, or arbiters, which are less suitable for ultralow-power IoMT devices (due to additional circuitry, power overhead, or poor stability), QT-PUF requires no external excitation or stabilization and operates under static bias. Simulation-based measurements for a $\mathbf{65}$~nm CMOS process demonstrate an entropy of $\mathbf{0.9999998}$, an FHD of $\mathbf{0.5001}$, and an average power (energy) consumption of $\mathbf{96.04}$~nW/bit ($\mathbf{19.21}$~fJ/bit, respectively) at $\mathbf{1.2\,V}$ and $\mathbf{35\,^{\circ}C}$ for the proposed PUF. It operates reliably across $\mathbf{0.9}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{0}\text{--}\mathbf{100\,^{\circ}C}$ with an average BER below $\mathbf{0.000163}$ across $\mathbf{1.0}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{10}\text{--}\mathbf{70\,^{\circ}C}$ within the operating conditions of typical implantable devices.

</details>


### [75] [Adversarial Trust Poisoning in Vehicular Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22122)
*Yutong Liu,Chenyi Wang,Ming F. Li,Qingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustFlip攻击，利用协同感知系统的一致性检测防御机制本身，通过部署物理对抗性物体使正常车辆间产生不一致观测，导致防御系统错误惩罚良性车辆，最终将其排除出协作。同时提出TrustReflect轻量级缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知防御系统通过交叉车辆不一致性检测和信任评估来对抗数据篡改攻击，但作者发现这些防御机制本身存在新的攻击面，可以被反向利用来毒害对良性车辆的信任分配。

Method: TrustFlip攻击部署物理对抗性物体（真实存在的物体），这些物体导致多辆良性车辆之间产生不一致的观测数据；防御系统将不一致性错误归因于目标车辆，导致其信任分数下降，最终被降权或排除协作。TrustReflect缓解机制通过自反射标记争议区域为不确定状态，将其排除出信任评估流程。

Result: 在多种协同感知架构和防御机制上，TrustFlip在高达87.7%的场景中成功将目标良性车辆移除协作，平均精度（AP）下降最多达13%。TrustReflect可将攻击成功率降低35-100%。

Conclusion: 协同感知中的一致性检测防御存在被武器化的安全漏洞，需要设计更鲁棒的信任评估机制；TrustReflect作为一种轻量级缓解方案展示了可行性，但更全面的防护仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Collaborative perception (CP) enables connected and autonomous vehicles to share sensor data and jointly reason about their environment. To defend against adversaries that fabricate or manipulate shared data, existing systems employ cross-vehicle inconsistency detection and trust estimation, penalizing vehicles whose observations conflict with the majority. In this work, we show that these defenses themselves introduce a new attack surface. We present TrustFlip, a novel attack that weaponizes consistency-based defenses to poison the trust assigned to benign vehicles. Instead of injecting false data into the collaboration pipeline, it deploys physical adversarial objects that are genuine but induce inconsistent observations among benign vehicles. The resulting inconsistencies are misattributed by the defense to the targeted vehicle, causing its trust score to degrade and eventually leading to its downweighting or exclusion from collaboration. Consequently, the system loses reliable sensing contributors, degrading perception capability and potentially inducing safety-critical failures. We evaluate TrustFlip across multiple collaborative perception architectures and defense mechanisms. Our results show that state-of-the-art defenses can be significantly affected: the attack removes the targeted benign vehicle from collaboration in up to 87.7% of scenarios and drops Average Precision (AP) by up to 13%. As an initial mitigation, we introduce TrustReflect, a lightweight self-reflection mechanism that marks disputed regions as uncertain and excludes them from trust evaluation, reducing the attack success rate by 35-100%.

</details>


### [76] [PACT: Reducing Alert Fatigue in Low-Prevalence SOC Streams with Triggered Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22324)
*Samuel Ndichu,Tao Ban,Seiichi Ozawa,Takeshi Takahashi,Daisuke Inoue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PACT是一种帕累托感知的触发式主动学习控制器，通过自适应窗口触发和混合采样规则，在低流行率安全告警场景中以少量分析师查询大幅降低误报负担，同时保持可接受的召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心面临持续的告警疲劳问题：在低流行率数据流中，即使很低的假阳性率也会产生大量调查负担，而聚合F1分数掩盖了分析师的真实工作负荷。

Method: PACT包含三个组件：①已部署的冻结XGBoost-Focal筛查器；②自适应窗口得分漂移触发器；③结合阈值相对不确定性与高得分采样的混合获取规则。

Result: 在AIT-ADS和BOTSv1两个低流行率基准上，PACT相比冻结基线分别降低43%和21%的误报负担，分析师查询量仅为周期性均匀随机更新的1/3.8和1/5.2。

Conclusion: PACT在误报负担与召回率之间实现了有效的帕累托权衡；纯误报最小化会以不可接受的召回率下降为代价。

Abstract: Security operations centers face persistent alert fatigue: in low-prevalence streams, even low false-positive rates generate substantial investigation load, while aggregate F1 scores obscure analyst burden. We introduce PACT, a Pareto-aware controller for triggered active learning, which wraps an already-deployed frozen XGBoost-Focal screener with an adaptive windowing score-shift trigger and a hybrid acquisition rule combining threshold-relative uncertainty with high-score sampling. On two public low-prevalence benchmarks, AIT-ADS (AIT Alert Data Set), and BOTSv1 (Boss of the SOC version 1), PACT attains the lowest benign-normalized false-positive (FP) burden among the adaptive methods tested. It reduces burden by 43% and 21%, respectively, relative to a frozen baseline, while using 3.8x and 5.2x fewer analyst queries than periodic uniform-random updating. A matched-trigger ablation controls trigger timing and shows that acquisition contributes beyond timing alone, at the cost of approximately ten percentage points of positive-window recall under free-running triggers. A frozen threshold-only baseline pushes FP lower still but collapses BOTSv1 recall by 55 percentage points. Under the evaluated workload assumptions, pure FP minimization trades unacceptable recall for that lower burden.

</details>


### [77] [Building Europe's Quantum Shield: The Strategic view for a Continent-Wide Quantum Key Ditribution (QKD) Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22332)
*Leandros Maglaras,Ilias Papastamatiou,Alexios Aivaliotis,Evangelos Markatos,Konstantinos Karantzalos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SEEWQCI项目通过结合光纤、卫星通信和增强安全规则，构建欧洲量子通信基础设施（EuroQCI），以应对量子计算对现有安全系统的威胁，保护电网、医院等关键服务的数据安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的快速发展在带来科学突破的同时，也威胁现有安全系统，因此需要部署欧洲通信基础设施（EuroQCI）来应对这一挑战。

Method: SEEWQCI项目采用光纤电缆、卫星通信和增强安全规则相结合的方式，构建多层次数字防护体系。

Result: 构建了强大的数字防护盾，重点保护电网、医院等关键基础设施，确保欧洲公民数据安全。

Conclusion: SEEWQCI项目通过融合多种通信技术和安全规则，有效应对量子计算带来的安全威胁，为欧洲关键基础设施提供保护。

Abstract: The fast growth of quantum computing can lead to amazing scientific breakthroughs while on the same time can be used to break today's security systems, raising new risks for existing digital systems. Facing this challenge, the European's Union's deployement of the European Communication Infrastructure (EuroQCI) is crucial. The SEEWQCI project combines fiber cables, satellite communications and enhanced security rules to build a strong digital shield. Its focus is to protect vital services like power grids and hospitals keeping Europeans' data safe.

</details>


### [78] [A First Measurement Study on Authentication Security in Real-World Remote MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22333)
*Huijun Zhou,Xiaohan Zhang,Haozhe Zhang,Haoyang Zhang,Mi Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对真实世界远程MCP服务器的认证安全进行大规模测量研究，发现40.55%的服务器无认证暴露工具。在OAuth认证的服务器中，存在开放客户端环境、动态客户端注册和委托授权三大特征，导致新的攻击面。基于此提出了包含4大类9种具体缺陷类型的认证缺陷分类体系，并通过半自动化检测框架对119个可测试的OAuth MCP服务器进行评估，发现每个服务器至少存在一个缺陷（共325个），其中动态客户端注册缺陷影响96.6%的服务器，9个CVE已获分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP作为连接LLM与外部服务的关键接口，远程部署日益重要，但认证边界的安全问题尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 大规模测量（识别7,973个远程MCP服务器）+ 半自动化检测框架（被动流量检测+主动动态探测）

Result: 40.55%的服务器无认证；119个OAuth服务器均存在至少一个缺陷（共325个），动态客户端注册缺陷影响96.6%的服务器；9个CVE

Conclusion: MCP生态系统存在普遍认证弱点，亟需加固基于OAuth的远程部署安全

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is emerging as a common interface connecting large language models (LLMs) with external services. Remote deployments are becoming increasingly important as agents connect to user-linked online services, such as social, productivity, and financial services. In such deployments, the authentication boundary between MCP clients and remote servers becomes security-critical, yet remains underexplored.
  We present the first measurement study of authentication security in real-world remote MCP servers. We identify 7,973 live remote MCP servers, finding that 40.55% expose tools without authentication. Among authenticated servers, OAuth is the dominant authorization mechanism for reaching remote services, and OAuth deployments in the MCP ecosystem commonly exhibit three characteristics: open client environments, dynamic client registration, and delegated authorization. These characteristics distinguish MCP deployments from traditional OAuth and introduce new attack surfaces. Guided by this observation, we derive a taxonomy of authentication flaws comprising three MCP-specific categories and conventional OAuth misconfigurations, for a total of four categories and nine concrete flaw types. To evaluate these flaws at scale, we implement a semi-automated detection framework that combines passive traffic inspection with active dynamic probing. Applying it to 119 testable real-world OAuth-enabled MCP servers, we find that each server exhibits at least one flaw, with a total of 325 flaws identified, among which dynamic client registration flaws affect 96.6% of tested servers. Many of these flaws can lead to sensitive information leakage and account takeover. Through responsible disclosure, we obtained 9 CVE IDs. Our findings expose pervasive authentication weaknesses in the MCP ecosystem and underscore the urgent need for hardened OAuth-based remote deployments.

</details>


### [79] [TimeGuard: Channel-wise Pool Training for Backdoor Defense in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22365)
*Quang Duc Nguyen,Siyuan Liang,Yiming Li,Fushuo Huo,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了13种后门防御方法在时间序列预测（TSF）中的失效原因，发现数据纠缠导致通道级信号稀释、任务形式转变导致训练损失退化两个根本问题。据此提出TimeGuard，采用通道级池训练范式，结合时间感知准则和距离正则化损失选择策略，在多数据集、多架构下将鲁棒性提升1.96倍，干净性能损失控制在5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测（TSF）易受后门攻击威胁，但针对TSF的后门防御研究严重不足，主要面临数据纠缠和任务形式转变两大挑战。现有防御方法在TSF场景下失效，亟需针对TSF特性的专用防御机制。

Method: 提出TimeGuard防御框架：(1) 采用通道级池训练（channel-wise pool training）为核心范式；(2) 基于时间感知准则初始化高置信度池，缓解信号稀释问题；(3) 引入距离正则化损失选择策略，在训练过程中逐步扩展可靠池，缓解损失退化问题。

Result: 在多个数据集、不同预测架构和多种TSF后门攻击下，TimeGuard将MAE_P（中毒指标）提升1.96倍（优于最佳基线），同时干净性能MAE_C退化控制在5%以内。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了TSF后门防御失败的两大根本原因，并提出首个针对TSF的训练时后门防御方法TimeGuard，有效兼顾了鲁棒性与干净性能，填补了TSF领域后门防御研究的空白。

Abstract: Time Series Forecasting (TSF) plays a critical role across many domains, yet it is vulnerable to backdoor attacks. However, backdoor defenses tailored to TSF remain underexplored, due to data entanglement and task-formulation shift challenges. To fill this gap, we conduct a systematic evaluation of thirteen representative backdoor defenses across the TSF life cycle and analyze their failure modes. Our results reveal two fundamental issues: (1) data entanglement induces channel-level signal dilution, rendering sample-filtering and trigger-synthesis defenses ineffective at localizing backdoors; and (2) task-formulation shift leads to training-loss degeneration, causing poisoned and clean windows to become indistinguishable at training stages. Based on these findings, we propose a training-time backdoor defense for TSF, termed TimeGuard. Our method adopts channel-wise pool training as the core paradigm and initializes a high-confidence pool using time-aware criteria to mitigate signal dilution. Moreover, we introduce distance-regularized loss selection to progressively expand the reliable pool during training and ease loss degeneration. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, forecasting architectures, and TSF backdoor attacks demonstrate that TimeGuard substantially improves robustness, boosting $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{P}$ by $1.96\times$ over the leading baseline, while preserving clean performance within 5% $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{C}$.

</details>


### [80] [EnCAgg: Enhanced Clustering Aggregation for Robust Federated Learning against Dynamic Model Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22506)
*Tianyun Zhang,Zhen Yang,Haozhao Wang,Ru Zhang,Yongfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于少量已知良性客户作为参考的鲁棒聚合方法，通过密度-based低维梯度聚类、增强聚类低维梯度生成器和低维梯度重新聚类三个步骤，有效抵御动态模型投毒攻击，在MNIST、CIFAR-10和MIND数据集上展现出优越的保真度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临日益严重的模型投毒攻击威胁，现有防御方法依赖固定阈值或固定聚类数量来区分恶意梯度与良性梯度，难以适应恶意客户端的动态投毒策略，且常因客户端本地数据集的异质性导致良性梯度丢失。

Method: 1) 密度-based低维梯度聚类：将梯度投影到两个最大发散维度上，应用密度聚类识别恶意梯度，同时保留聚类良性梯度和潜在良性离群点；2) 增强聚类低维梯度生成器模型：学习生成与良性聚类边界对齐的伪梯度，作为连接稀疏良性梯度离群点的桥梁；3) 低维梯度重新聚类：将生成的伪梯度与真实梯度一起聚类，恢复被误分类为噪声点的良性梯度，使更多良性梯度参与聚合。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和MIND数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在动态投毒场景下展现出优越的保真度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过利用少量已知良性客户作为参考，能够在恶意客户数量未知且可变的情况下，准确识别并过滤恶意梯度，同时尽可能保留良性梯度，有效解决了现有方法难以适应动态投毒策略的问题。

Abstract: Federated learning faces increasing threats from model poisoning attacks, which harms its application to improve privacy. Existing defense methods typically rely on fixed thresholds or perform clustering with a fixed number of clusters to distinguish malicious gradients from benign ones. However, these methods are difficult to adapt to dynamic poisoning strategies of malicious clients, and often result in the loss of benign gradients due to the heterogeneity of clients' local datasets. To address these problems, we propose a novel robust aggregation method that leverages a small number of known benign clients as references, enabling accurate identification and filtering of malicious gradients while retaining as many benign gradients as possible, even when the number of malicious clients is unknown and variable. First, we introduce a density-based low-dimensional gradient clustering method, which projects gradients onto the two most divergent dimensions and applies density-based clustering to identify malicious gradients while retaining clustered benign gradients and potentially benign outliers. Second, we design an enhancing clustering low-dimensional gradient generator model, which learns to generate pseudo-gradients aligned with the boundary of the benign cluster. These pseudo-gradients act as bridges to connect sparse benign gradient outliers. Third, we introduce low-dimensional gradient re-clustering that clusters the generated pseudo-gradients together with real gradients to recover benign gradients misclassified as noise points, enabling more benign gradients to participate in aggregation. Extensive experiments on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and MIND datasets demonstrate that our method exhibits superior fidelity and robustness under dynamic poisoning scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [Measuring Security Without Fooling Ourselves: Why Benchmarking Agents Is Hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22568)
*Sahar Abdelnabi,Chris Hicks,Konrad Rieck,Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出当前用于评估安全关键型AI agent的基准测试存在三大核心缺陷：基准漏洞、时间滞后性和运行时不确定性，并提出了构建更稳健评估框架的实用方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估基准存在严重缺陷，导致对AI agent在安全关键型任务中的能力评估不可靠，亟需识别这些问题并寻找改进方向。

Method: 基于近期实证证据，系统性地表征了安全评估中面临的三大核心挑战：基准漏洞（benchmark vulnerabilities）、时间滞后性（temporal staleness）和运行时不确定性（runtime uncertainty）。

Result: 明确了当前安全评估基准的三个根本性问题，并提出了构建更健壮、更可信的评估框架的实践方向。

Conclusion: 现有的AI安全评估基准存在严重缺陷，需要通过解决基准漏洞、时间滞后性和运行时不确定性问题来建立更可靠的评估体系。

Abstract: The benchmarks used to evaluate AI agents in security-critical roles suffer from crucial weaknesses. Building on recent empirical evidence, we characterize three core challenges that undermine security evaluations: benchmark vulnerabilities, temporal staleness, and runtime uncertainty. We then outline practical directions toward building more robust and trustworthy evaluation frameworks.

</details>


### [82] [A Formal Basis for Quantum Cryptographic Exposure Measurement under HNDL Threat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22569)
*Matheus Rufino,Rafael Duarte Marcelino,Julio Smanioto Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明HNDL妥协概率在三个假设下分解为时间风险、加密漏洞与操作暴露的乘积项以及防御-攻击强度比决定的饱和分母，各维度的边际敏感性内生于组织在漏洞-暴露平面中的位置，而非固定常数。加法评分框架因缺失加密漏洞与操作暴露的交互项而无法再现这一结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化"先复制后解密"量子威胁下组织暴露程度的结构性度量方法，批判当前加法评分框架缺乏理论依据。

Method: 基于三个假设（对抗生产与价值衰减动力学），推导HNDL妥协概率的因子分解形式，包含时间风险项、加密漏洞与操作暴露的乘性项、以及防御-攻击强度比决定的饱和分母。

Result: HNDL概率因子分解结构成立，各维度边际敏感性内生于组织在漏洞-暴露平面中的位置，加法评分框架无法复现该结构。

Conclusion: 该框架为部分可观测条件下的HNDL暴露优先级排序提供了结构化的理论基础。

Abstract: An adversary copies your encrypted traffic today and waits for a quantum computer to decrypt it later. How exposed are you?
  We show that the functional form of the answer is not merely a calibration choice -- it is structurally justified by three assumptions about adversarial production and value-decay dynamics. Under those assumptions, the HNDL compromise probability factorises into a temporal hazard, a multiplicative cryptographic-vulnerability and operational-exposure term, and a saturation denominator governed by the defense-attack intensity ratio; the marginal sensitivity to each dimension is endogenous to the organisation's position in the vulnerability-exposure plane, not a fixed global constant. Additive scoring frameworks cannot reproduce this structure because the interaction between cryptographic vulnerability and operational exposure is absent by construction, regardless of calibration. The resulting framework provides a structurally grounded basis for operational HNDL exposure prioritisation under partial observability.

</details>


### [83] [Building an Open Source Operational Technology Pentesting Platform: Lessons from LINICS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22590)
*Awais Rashid,Joseph Gardiner,Louise Evans*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LINICS，一个面向操作技术(OT)环境的开源渗透测试与安全分析平台，旨在填补OT领域缺乏类似Kali Linux等开源安全工具平台的空白。作者分享了该平台的架构设计、构建和发布经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IT安全专业人员有Kali Linux等开源平台可用，但支撑工业控制系统的操作技术(OT)领域缺乏类似的开源安全平台，存在工具空白。

Method: 作者基于实际经验，架构、构建并发布了LINICS这一面向OT渗透测试和安全分析的开源平台。

Result: 成功构建并发布了LINICS开源平台，可供OT安全人员进行渗透测试和安全分析使用。

Conclusion: LINICS填补了OT安全领域缺乏开源渗透测试平台的空白，为OT安全专业人员提供了类似Kali Linux的专用工具平台。

Abstract: Information Technology (IT) security professionals have ready access to open-source platforms such as Kali Linux. But no such platform exists for Operational Technology (OT) that underpins Industrial Control Systems. We discuss experiences of architecting, building and releasing LINICS, an open-source platform for OT pentesting and security analysis.

</details>


### [84] [UNAD+: An Explainable Hybrid Framework for Unknown Network Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22621)
*Saif Alzubi,Frederic Stahl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出UNAD+框架，结合良性样本无监督集成、加权多数投票、伪标记监督精炼和可解释性层，在CICIDS2017和NSL-KDD数据集上实现F1值超过98%，有效降低了误报率并增强了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有入侵检测系统中，监督方法无法检测未知攻击，无监督方法误报率高，亟需一种既能检测零日攻击又能保持低误报率且具备可解释性的框架。

Method: UNAD+框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 良性样本无监督集成通过加权多数投票进行检测；(2) 基于伪标签的监督精炼阶段；(3) 事后可解释性层提供局部和全局解释。

Result: 在CICIDS2017和NSL-KDD基准数据集上，UNAD+的F1分数超过98%，相比原始UNAD框架显著降低了误报率，并提高了透明度和部署适用性。

Conclusion: UNAD+通过结合无监督与监督方法的优势并集成可解释性，有效解决了未知攻击检测中误报率高和缺乏透明度的问题，适用于实际部署场景。

Abstract: The detection of previously unseen network attacks remains a major challenge for intrusion detection systems. Although supervised learning methods often perform well on known attack classes, they are limited when new attack types are not represented in the training data. Unsupervised methods are more suitable for detecting zero-day attacks, as they do not require labelled attack samples, but they often suffer from high false positive rates, which limits their real-world usefulness. This paper presents UNAD+, an enhanced framework for unknown network attack detection derived from the previously proposed Unknown Network Attack Detector (UNAD). UNAD+ combines a benign-only unsupervised ensemble with Weighted Majority Voting (WMV), a supervised refinement stage trained on pseudo-labelled detections, and a post hoc explainability layer that provides both local and global explanations. The framework was evaluated on the CICIDS2017 and NSL-KDD benchmark datasets. The results show that UNAD+ improves on the original UNAD framework, achieving F1-scores above 98% across the benchmark datasets while significantly reducing false positives and enhancing transparency and deployment suitability through integrated explainability.

</details>


### [85] [TriSweep: A Four-Drone Swarm Framework for Electromagnetic Side-Channel Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22709)
*Eric Yocam,Varghese Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TriSweep是一个四无人机蜂群仿真框架，用于对嵌入式微控制器进行远距离（0.25-1.5米）电磁侧信道分析。通过三架专用采集无人机（Anchor全频谱、Mask Probe掩码寄存器泄漏、Cipher Probe掩码SubBytes输出泄漏）和一台累加无人机（相干合并+4.8 dB SNR增益、二阶掩码抵消），在ASCAD数据集上实现了18±1.7的密钥排名，并利用互相关对齐补偿了无人机悬停振动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电磁侧信道分析假设探头静止且紧贴芯片，这种威胁模型低估了空中 adversary 的能力。作者旨在证明无人机蜂群可以在远距离（0.25-1.5米）实现有效的自主电磁侧信道攻击，扩展威胁模型。

Method: 设计四无人机蜂群架构：(1) Anchor（全频谱采集）、(2) Mask Probe（掩码寄存器泄漏）、(3) Cipher Probe（掩码SubBytes输出泄漏）三架采集无人机，(4) Accumulator累加无人机执行相干合并（+4.8 dB SNR增益）和二阶掩码消除（两空间分离泄漏流的中心化乘积）。使用互相关对齐补偿悬停振动，双通道CNN进行密钥分类。

Result: 在ANSSI ASCAD数据集（ATmega8515掩码AES-128）上，0.25米距离实现密钥排名18±1.7（五种子）。互相关对齐将单无人机在100样本抖动数据集上的排名从89降至21。双通道CNN损失降至0.454（随机基线5.545）。

Conclusion: 所提出的四无人机蜂群架构在仿真中展示了远距离EM-SCA的可行性，通过空间分离采集和相干合并显著提升攻击能力。尚未制造物理硬件，原型构建为下一步工作。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel analysis traditionally assumes a stationary, close-proximity probe - a threat model that underestimates aerial adversaries. TriSweep is a simulation framework that designs and evaluates a four-drone swarm architecture for autonomous standoff EM-SCA of embedded microcontrollers at 0.25-1.5 m. Three spatially specialized collector drones - Anchor (full-spectrum), Mask Probe (mask-register loading leakage), and Cipher Probe (masked SubBytes output leakage) - feed a stationary Accumulator drone that performs coherent combining (+4.8 dB SNR gain) and second-order mask cancellation via a centered product of the two spatially separated leakage streams. Evaluated against three real ANSSI ASCAD datasets (ATmega8515 masked AES-128 and 50/100-sample desynchronized variants), the framework achieves a simulated key rank of 18 +/- 1.7 (five-seed) at 0.25 m on the primary masked dataset. Profiling-trace cross-correlation alignment reduces single-drone rank from 89 to 21 on the 100-sample-jitter variant, demonstrating compensation for drone hover vibration. A two-channel CNN in the Accumulator converges to a loss of 0.454 (vs. random baseline 5.545) and improves rank on desynchronized datasets. No physical hardware has been fabricated; prototype construction is the planned next step.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Benchmarking and Improving Monitors for Out-Of-Distribution Alignment Failure in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21602)
*Dylan Feng,Pragya Srivastava,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOOD基准测试，系统研究LLM监控管线能否检测分布外对齐失败。研究发现守卫模型难以泛化到OOD场景，通过结合守卫模型与马氏距离和困惑度OOD检测器，召回率从39%提升至45%，且OOD检测带来的召回率增益优于将守卫模型参数扩大20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全和对齐失败多源于分布外（OOD）场景，但现有监控方法能否检测这类OOD对齐失败缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建MOOD基准（含受限训练集和7个OOD测试集），训练守卫模型并测试其OOD泛化能力，提出将守卫模型与四种OOD检测器（马氏距离、困惑度等）结合的方法。

Result: 守卫模型在OOD场景下泛化失败；结合马氏距离和困惑度OOD检测器可将召回率从39%提升至45%；OOD检测的召回率增益优于将守卫模型参数扩大20倍。

Conclusion: OOD检测应成为LLM监控的关键组件，为后续研究提供基础。

Abstract: Many safety and alignment failures of large language models (LLMs) occur due to out-of-distribution (OOD) situations: unusual prompt or response patterns that are unforeseen by model developers. We systematically study whether LLM monitoring pipelines can detect these OOD alignment failures by introducing a benchmark called Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD). It is difficult to find failures that are truly OOD for off-the-shelf models trained on vast safety datasets. We sidestep this by including a restricted training set in MOOD that we use to train our own monitors, as well as seven test sets with diverse alignment failures that are outside the training distribution. Using MOOD, we find that guard models (safety classifiers) often fail to generalize OOD. To fix this, we propose combining guard models with OOD detectors. We test four types of OOD detectors and find that a combination of a guard model with Mahalanobis distance and perplexity-based OOD detectors can improve recall from 39% to 45%. We also establish positive scaling trends across model scales for monitors that combine a guard model and OOD detector; we find that incorporating OOD detection into monitoring achieves a higher recall gain than using a guard model with 20 times more parameters. Our work suggests that OOD detection should be a crucial component of LLM monitoring and provides a foundation for further work on this important problem.

</details>


### [87] [TO-Agents: A Multi-Agent AI Pipeline for Preference-Guided Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21622)
*Isabella A. Stewart,Hongrui Chen,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TO-Agents多智能体AI框架，将自然语言设计意图与迭代拓扑优化连接，通过视觉-语言推理和独立评判智能体自动调整求解器参数，在悬臂梁和手机支架设计中实现偏好对齐（60%成功率），支持端到端的意图到原型制造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化能生成高效结构，但设计师需手动将定性设计意图（如视觉风格、产品体验、可制造性）转化为与这些偏好无直接关联的求解器设置，缺乏自动化连接。

Method: 构建多智能体框架：将自然语言问题描述转化为验证后的求解器输入，运行拓扑优化求解器，渲染3D拓扑，使用多视图视觉-语言推理结合独立评判智能体对结果进行评价并迭代修改求解器参数，最后通过制造智能体进行后处理。

Result: 在悬臂梁和手机支架两个案例中，60%的试验产生至少一个偏好对齐的设计，比无视觉或历史反馈的消融管线最多高6倍。评判评分和人工评估表明该管线能识别有效参数杠杆、从不良修订中恢复并扩展设计探索。

Conclusion: 智能体拓扑优化可将设计师从低层参数调优转向高层形式与功能规格说明，同时需为可靠自主工程设计建立安全机制；识别了过冲、选择性记忆、工具误用和参数推理错误等失败模式。

Abstract: Topology optimization can generate efficient structures, but designers often must manually translate qualitative intent, such as desired visual style, product experience, or manufacturability into solver settings that are not directly tied to those preferences. We present TO-Agents, a multi-agent AI framework that connects natural-language design intent with iterative topology optimization. The framework converts a human-provided problem description into validated solver inputs, runs a topology optimization solver, renders the resulting 3D topology, and uses multi-view vision-language reasoning with an independent judge agent to critique each result and revise solver parameters. We evaluate the framework on two long-horizon design tasks: a cantilever beam benchmark and a phone-stand product design. In both tasks, the designer specifies an aesthetic preference for hierarchically branched structures inspired by natural tree morphologies, and the system performs four revision cycles across ten independent replicates. TO-Agents produces at least one preference-aligned design in 60% of trials for each case study, corresponding to up to 6x more successful trials than an ablated pipeline without visual or historical feedback. Judge scores and human evaluations show that the pipeline can identify effective parameter levers, recover from poor revisions, and expand design exploration. A manufacturing agent further post-processes top-ranked designs for additive manufacturing, enabling end-to-end intent-to-prototype design. We also identify failure modes, including overshooting, selective memory, misplaced tools, and incorrect parameter reasoning. These results suggest that agentic topology optimization can shift designers from low-level parameter tuning toward higher-level specification of form and function, while highlighting safeguards needed for reliable autonomous engineering design.

</details>


### [88] [The Shape of Testimony: A Scalable Framework for Oral History Archive Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21623)
*Itamar Trainin,Renana Keydar,Amit Pinchevski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模计算分析（包含1600余份证词），运用话语分割、主题建模和LLM分析，量化了USC Shoah Foundation和Yale Fortunoff Video Archive两大 Holocaust 口述证词库的结构化程度，证实了先前研究中发现的结构差异，同时也揭示了两者之间的显著重叠，挑战了简单的"结构化vs.自由形式"二分法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Holocaust研究中长期存在一种区分：USC证词偏向结构化、访谈者引导，而Yale Fortunoff则更自由、开放式。这一区分影响了学术研究和后续档案建设。本文旨在通过大规模计算分析，批判性地检验这一经典论断。

Method: 对1600余份来自两个档案库的证词进行大规模计算分析，运用话语分割、主题建模和基于大语言模型的分析方法，通过主题连贯性、访谈者-幸存者互动动态以及问题类型分布来量化证词的"结构化程度"。

Result: 结果总体上证实了先前研究识别的结构差异，但同时也发现两个档案库之间存在显著重叠——无论是单个访谈内部还是跨常见叙事模式层面，从而挑战了简单的"结构化vs.自由形式"二元对立。

Conclusion: 本文不仅重新审视了Holocaust研究中的一个基础性论断，还提供了一个可扩展、可复制的比较语料库分析框架，作为概念验证，为数字口述史、叙事分析以及公民科学标注平台设计提供了更广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Researchers in Holocaust studies have often distinguished between two styles of oral survivor testimony: the USC Shoah Foundation's interviews tend to follow a structured, interviewer-guided format, whereas the Yale Fortunoff Video Archive generally favors a more free-form, open-ended style. This distinction has influenced both scholarly research and the development of later archives. In this study, we critically examine that claim by conducting a large-scale computational analysis of more than 1,600 testimonies from both collections. Leveraging discourse segmentation, topic modeling, and large language model (LLM) based analysis, we quantify the "structuredness" level of testimonies through topic coherence, interviewer-survivor dynamics, and the distribution of question types. Our results generally corroborate the structural differences identified in earlier research, while also revealing significant overlaps between the collections, both within individual interviews and across common narrative patterns. This complicates the simple "structured vs. free-form" dichotomy often applied to these oral histories. Beyond revisiting a foundational claim in Holocaust studies, our work provides a scalable, replicable framework for comparative corpus analysis. As a proof of concept, it suggests broader applications for digital oral history, narrative analysis, and the design of citizen-science annotation platforms.

</details>


### [89] [MindLoom: Composing Thought Modes for Frontier-Level Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21630)
*Haiyang Shen,Taian Guo,Xuanzhong Chen,Mugeng Liu,Weichen Bi,Wenchun Jing,Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Yudong Han,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Jinsheng Huang,Ming Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindLoom通过将难题分解为思维模式链，并利用检索模型组合这些模式来合成前沿推理数据，在九个基准测试上取得了优于基线模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理数据合成方法对问题难度的结构性因素可见性有限，导致多样性狭窄且难度控制不稳定，需要一种能够系统合成前沿推理数据的方法。

Method: 1) 将难题解分解为思维模式链；2) 训练检索模型将问题状态与兼容思维模式匹配；3) 通过迭代应用检索到的思维模式组合新问题，并采用分布对齐采样；4) 基于rollout的评判阶段标注难度并提供经过验证的正确响应。

Result: 在涵盖5个STEM学科和4个数学推理任务的9个基准上，MindLoom微调的模型在性能上优于基础模型、蒸馏和外部数据基线。

Conclusion: MindLoom能够覆盖广泛的推理模式，同时保持有效的难度控制，各组件均有贡献，且已开源。

Abstract: Although LLMs have made substantial progress in reasoning, systematically producing frontier-level reasoning data remains difficult. Existing synthesis methods often have limited visibility into the structural factors that govern problem difficulty, which can result in narrow diversity and unstable difficulty control. In this work, we view the difficulty of a reasoning problem as arising from the accumulation of atomic knowledge-reasoning transformations, which we term thought modes. Building on this perspective, we propose MindLoom, a framework for synthesizing frontier-level reasoning data through compositional thought mode engineering. Given a collection of hard problems with verified solutions, MindLoom first decomposes those solutions into thought mode chains that reveal each problem's construction logic. It then trains a retrieval model that matches problem states to compatible thought modes, providing guidance on which reasoning challenges to introduce during synthesis. New problems are composed by iteratively applying retrieved thought modes to seed questions, with distribution-aligned sampling to encourage diverse reasoning coverage. Finally, a rollout-based judging stage labels generated questions by difficulty and supplies judged-correct responses for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate MindLoom on nine benchmarks covering five STEM disciplines and four mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple model families and sizes. Models fine-tuned on MindLoom-generated data achieves favorable performances over base models, distillation, and external-data baselines across the reported benchmarks. Ablation studies indicate the contribution of each component, and further analysis suggests that MindLoom covers a broad range of reasoning patterns while maintaining useful difficulty control. We have open-sourced our implementation at https://github.com/EachSheep/MindLoom.

</details>


### [90] [Investigating Concept Alignment Using Implausible Category Members](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21683)
*Sunayana Rane,Brenden M. Lake,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过询问不符合常理的类别成员（如“橄榄是交通工具吗？”）来探测AI系统对基础概念边界的理解，并与人类认知进行比较，发现AI在概念边界上与人类存在显著差异，包括将“词汇”归为“交通工具”或“服装”，将某些“蔬菜”识别为“水果”，以及将非武器类实例归入“武器”类别，这些概念对齐问题会转化为有安全隐患的下游行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展具有人类水平概念理解的AI系统是构建安全、可靠且行为可解释系统的关键步骤，而现有研究多通过询问合理的类别成员（如“汽车是交通工具吗？”）来探测概念理解，可能只是复现训练数据中的模式；因此需要一种替代策略来真正检验AI的概念边界知识。

Method: 基于Rosch和Mervis经典心理学研究，选取一组基础概念，研究AI系统将对象分配到上位类别的结果，同时分析同一对象分配到错误上位类别的情况；将这些分配结果与人类参与者在完整类别内和跨类别分配任务中的表现进行比较。

Result: 发现AI模型与人类在多个概念上存在显著且令人惊讶的差异：模型将“词汇”归为“交通工具”和“服装”等类别；将某些“蔬菜”类别成员识别为“水果”；将非武器类别的样例归入“武器”类别。

Conclusion: AI系统的概念边界与人类认知存在系统性偏差，这些概念对齐错误会转化为有问题的下游行为，对AI安全构成实际影响。

Abstract: Developing AI systems with a human-like understanding of everyday concepts is a key step towards developing safe, reliable systems whose behavior makes sense to humans. When probing concept understanding, asking questions about plausible category members (e.g., "Is a car a vehicle?") is likely to recall patterns in the model's vast training data. We pursue an alternative strategy, characterizing the boundaries of conceptual categories by asking about implausible category members (e.g., "Is an olive a vehicle?") to probe the kind of concept-level knowledge we take for granted in fellow humans. We characterize concept boundaries for a set of fundamental concepts by studying AI systems' assignments of objects to superordinate categories from a classic psychological study by Rosch and Mervis, as well as their assignments of the same objects to mismatched superordinate categories. We compare these assignments to those made by human participants on the full range of within-category and cross-category assignment tasks. Our results reveal a range of concepts for which which models differ in meaningful and surprising ways from humans, including treating "words" as belonging to categories like "vehicles" and "clothing," identifying several "vegetable" category members as "fruit," and assigning exemplars from non-weapon categories to the "weapons" category. We also demonstrate how these instances of concept misalignment translate into problematic downstream behavior with implications for AI safety.

</details>


### [91] [The Impact of AI Usage and Informativeness on Skill Development in Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21695)
*Shang Wu,Hongyu Yao,Catarina Belem,Shuyuan Fu,Mark Steyvers,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI使用强度和AI信息量对逻辑推理任务中人类技能发展的影响。发现重度AI使用会削弱技能发展，而低信息量的AI既不能提升即时表现，也会损害长期学习效果；高信息量的AI能改善短期表现，但对长期技能发展的影响具有异质性。AI既可能成为人类技能发展的补充，也可能成为替代品，关键在于使用方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI越来越多地融入人类问题解决过程，但AI对个体技能发展的具体影响尚不明确，本研究旨在填补这一认知空白。

Method: 通过控制条件下的逻辑推理任务实验，设置按需获取AI辅助的机制，考察AI使用强度和信息量对学习效果的影响。

Result: 1) 重度AI使用者表现劣于可比同龄人，而轻度使用者与不使用AI的匹配用户表现相似；2) 低信息量AI既不能改善即时表现，移除AI后表现也下降，整体学习效果较差；3) 高信息量AI能改善短期表现，且平均而言不降低移除AI后的表现，但存在异质性效应。

Conclusion: AI对技能发展的影响具有情境依赖性：高信息量AI可补充人类推理能力，低信息量AI或过度使用则会削弱独立思考。合理管控AI的访问和使用对于促进技能发展至关重要。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is being increasingly integrated into human problem-solving, yet its effects on individual skill development remain unclear. We examine how both AI usage and informativeness can shape learning in the context of a controlled logical reasoning task with on-demand access to AI assistance. We find that greater AI usage is associated with weaker skill development: heavy AI users underperform relative to comparable peers, whereas light AI users perform similarly to matched users who do not use AI. We also find in our study that these patterns are mediated by AI informativeness. Low-information AI neither improves immediate performance nor preserves performance after AI assistance is removed, and is linked to weaker learning overall. On the other hand, high-information AI was found to improve short-run performance without reducing post-AI outcomes on average in our experiments, but with heterogeneous effects. Our findings in general suggest that AI can, depending on context, either complement human skill development by amplifying independent reasoning or can act as a substitute that undermines such reasoning, with the implication that regulating AI access and usage will be important for promoting skill development in the presence of AI assistance.

</details>


### [92] [Latent-space Attacks for Refusal Evasion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21706)
*Giorgio Piras,Raffaele Mura,Fabio Brau,Maura Pintor,Luca Oneto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新解读了拒绝抑制（refusal suppression）问题，将其形式化为一种对线性探针的潜在空间逃逸攻击。作者指出现有方法本质上是将表示投影到决策边界上（最小置信度逃逸），并提出了受控潜在空间逃逸攻击（CLE），通过优化置信度将表示推向边界之外的合规区域，在多个模型上取得了最先进的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拒绝抑制方法（如消融拒绝方向）虽然经验成功，但缺乏对其潜在空间变换的严格理论解释，且不清楚为何能抑制拒绝行为。已有的差均值方向消融方法本质上是将表示投影到决策边界，但这种"刚好到达边界"的策略存在局限。

Method: 1) 将拒绝抑制重新定义为对线性探针的潜在空间逃逸攻击；2) 指出差均值方向消融等价于投影到决策边界的逃逸攻击；3) 提出受控潜在空间逃逸攻击（CLE），将表示投影超越决策边界并优化置信度。

Result: 在15个指令微调、多模态和推理模型上取得了最先进的攻击成功率，超越了现有的拒绝消融基线和专门的越狱攻击方法。

Conclusion: 将拒绝抑制理论化为对抗性逃逸攻击框架，不仅解释了现有方法的成功，还揭示了其"止步于决策边界"的局限，进而提出CLE方法实现了更强的攻击效果。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models are trained to refuse harmful requests, yet refusal behavior can be suppressed by steering their internal representations. Existing methods do so by ablating a refusal direction from model activations, aiming to remove refusal from the model's residual stream. Despite their empirical success, these methods lack a principled account of the latent-space transformation they induce and why it suppresses refusal. In this work, we recast refusal suppression as a latent-space evasion attack against linear probes trained to separate refused from answered prompts. Under this view, prior work's difference-in-means direction naturally defines such a probe, and its ablation is exactly a projection onto its decision boundary, i.e., a minimum-confidence evasion attack. This perspective not only explains the empirical success of prior work but also admits a key limitation: evasion stops at the decision boundary, motivating the need to push representations further into the compliant region, i.e., where the model answers. We leverage this by proposing a Controlled Latent-space Evasion attack that projects representations past the boundary with an optimized confidence. We achieve state-of-the-art attack success rate across 15 instruction-tuned, multimodal, and reasoning models, outperforming existing refusal-ablation baselines and specialized jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [93] [AttuneBench: A Conversation-Based Benchmark for LLM Emotional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21739)
*Kate M. Lubrano,Faisal Sayed,Ankita Rathod,Akshansh,Craver Corbyn Thomas-Smith,Mark E. Whiting,Karina Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AttuneBench是一个基于200场真实多人-模型多轮对话的情感智能基准测试，发现情感识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断是相互独立的能力，其中偏好对齐和响应质量判断比情感标签准确度更能区分模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感智能基准测试依赖合成提示、单轮对话或第三方标注，无法直接测量模型在真实多轮对话中推断和响应参与者情绪状态的能力。

Method: 基于200场真实人类与匿名化LLM的多轮对话，收集参与者逐轮标注的情绪状态、模型行为及偏好响应，构建AttuneBench基准，并在11个模型上进行评估。

Result: 情感识别、行为分类、偏好预测和响应质量判断的模型排名在很大程度上相互独立；偏好对齐和响应质量判断比情感标签准确度更能区分模型性能。

Conclusion: 情感智能行为可分解为可分离的能力维度，评估需关注上下文中的偏好预测，单轮或合成格式无法直接捕捉多轮间差异。

Abstract: Emotional intelligence (EI), the ability to perceive, understand, and respond appropriately to others' emotional states, is central to human communication, and increasingly important to assess as LLMs assume conversational roles in everyday life. Existing EI benchmarks rely on synthetic prompts, single-turn cases, or third-party annotation. These approaches do not directly measure how models infer and respond to a participant's emotional state over the course of a real conversation. We introduce AttuneBench, a benchmark grounded in 200 genuine multi-turn human-model conversations in which participants conversed with anonymized LLMs and provided turn-by-turn annotations of their emotional state, the model's behavior, and their preferred responses. Across 11 evaluated models, we find that model rankings on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality are largely independent, indicating that emotionally intelligent behavior decomposes into separable capabilities. Preference alignment and response-quality judgments are substantially more model-discriminating than emotion-label accuracy. These results indicate that emotionally intelligent behavior requires predicting what kind of response a specific user wants in context, a distinction that aggregate scoring can obscure and that single-turn or synthetic formats cannot directly capture across turns. AttuneBench provides a framework for assessing each of these capabilities and for diagnosing model-specific strengths and failure modes in emotionally salient conversation.

</details>


### [94] [Who Uses AI? Platforms, Workforce, and AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21743)
*Michelle Yin,Burhan Ogut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现基于AI平台对话日志测量的职业暴露度存在系统性偏差：这些分数部分反映了平台用户基础而非真实劳动力市场结构。改变平台输入、调整消费者vs企业渠道权重、以及使用BLS劳动力份额重新加权后，估计系数变化显著，且非经典测量误差会导致替代效应被低估的程度大于增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献越来越多地使用AI平台对话日志来测量职业暴露度，但这种测量方法的有效性和潜在偏差尚未得到充分检验。

Method: 通过固定结果变量、样本、控制变量和估计方法，仅改变平台输入来源，比较不同平台（消费者vs企业渠道）以及使用BLS劳动力份额重新加权后的估计差异，并形式化非经典测量误差理论，推导概率极限和部分识别边界。

Result: 1) 仅改变平台输入，ChatGPT后的就业系数变化达1.9倍；2) 同一供应商的消费者与企业渠道估计符号相反；3) 使用BLS劳动力份额重新加权后估计值衰减42%-93%。

Conclusion: 基于AI平台对话日志的职业暴露度测量存在非经典测量误差，会导致替代效应被低估的程度大于增强效应，研究者需谨慎使用并考虑测量偏差。

Abstract: A growing literature uses artificial intelligence platform conversation logs to measure occupation exposure. We show that these scores partly measure platform user base rather than the workforce. Holding outcome, sample, controls, and estimator fixed while varying only the platform input changes the post-ChatGPT employment coefficient by a factor of 1.9, and within-vendor consumer-versus-enterprise channels produce estimates that disagree in sign. Reweighting to Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce shares attenuates estimates by 42 to 93 percent. We formalize the non-classical measurement error, derive probability limits and partial-identification bounds for employment elasticities. The bias understates substitution more than augmentation.

</details>


### [95] [A Causal Argumentation Method for Explainability of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21758)
*Henry Salgado,Meagan R. Kendall,Martine Ceberio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合因果发现与双极论证框架(BAF)的方法，通过半稳定语义识别特征扩展，解释模型为何做出特定预测，并在两个基准数据集上验证优于传统事后可解释性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法能识别哪些特征与预测相关，但无法解释为何模型做出特定决策，缺乏对决策因果逻辑的深层理解。

Method: 先使用因果发现方法识别变量间的因果关系，再将这些关系转换为双极论证框架(BAF)以表示特征间的支持与反对交互，最后通过半稳定语义找到能够解释模型预测结果的特征扩展。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上验证了该方法，并与标准事后可解释性方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 融合因果关系与论证推理能够提供比传统事后XAI方法更清晰的决策原因解释。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods identify which features are relevant to a model's predictions but often fail to clarify why certain decisions are made. In this work, we present a novel method that integrates causality with argument-based reasoning to explain why models may be making predictions. Our approach first identifies causal relationships among variables using causal discovery methods and then translates these into a Bipolar Argumentation Framework (BAF) to represent supportive and opposing interactions among features. By using semi-stable semantics, we find extensions of features that explain why certain outcomes may have been chosen. We demonstrate our method on two benchmark datasets and compare its results against standard post-hoc explainability approaches.

</details>


### [96] [What Counts as AI Sycophancy? A Taxonomy and Expert Survey of a Fragmented Construct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21778)
*Meryl Ye,Lujain Ibrahim,Jessica Y. Bo,Myra Cheng,Ida Mattsson,Daniel Vennemeyer,Robert Kraut,Steve Rathje*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过审查70篇论文和调查106位专家，建立了AI阿谀奉承行为分类法，区分了行为指向（用户观点/信念 vs. 个人特质/情感）和行为方式（显性直接 vs. 隐性微妙），揭示了该领域定义不一致的问题——94.3%的专家认为这是重大问题，但对具体行为认定存在显著分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "AI sycophancy"（阿谀奉承）这一术语缺乏统一定义，不同研究将其用于描述从同意用户错误观点到过度赞美、隐瞒修正反馈等多种行为，导致评估结果难以比较、缓解策略无法迁移、系统对一种奉承形式有抵抗力却仍表现出其他形式。

Method: 1) 审查了70篇关于AI阿谀奉承的论文，开发行为分类法，按两个维度区分：行为指向（用户观点/信念 vs. 用户个人特质/情感）和行为方式（显性直接 vs. 隐性微妙，如框架、省略、语气）。2) 调查了106位AI阿谀奉承及相关领域专家，考察研究者对哪些模型行为属于阿谀奉承的共识程度。

Result: 分类法映射显示现有研究集中于对用户信念的显性奉承，更微妙和面向个人的行为研究不足。调查结果显示94.3%的专家认为阿谀奉承是当前AI系统的重大问题，但对具体哪些行为属于奉承存在显著分歧。

Conclusion: AI阿谀奉承是一系列广泛的行为类别，具有不同的测量挑战、干预需求和治理影响。所提出的分类法为理解和应对这些行为提供了共享词汇。

Abstract: AI sycophancy has become a prominent concern in large language model (LLM) research. Yet the term lacks a consistent definition and has been applied to behaviors ranging from agreeing with a user's false claim to excessively praising the user to withholding corrective feedback. When researchers, companies, and policymakers use the same term to describe different behaviors, evaluation results become difficult to compare, mitigation strategies fail to transfer, and systems that are resistant to one form of sycophancy continue exhibiting other forms. To address this, we make two contributions. First, we reviewed 70 papers on AI sycophancy to develop a taxonomy of how the behavior has been defined and measured. The taxonomy distinguishes (1) whether a model is sycophantic toward a user's positions and beliefs, or toward the user's broader personal traits and emotions, and (2) whether this occurs through explicit, direct language or more implicit, subtle behaviors such as framing, omission, or tone. Mapping existing literature to our taxonomy reveals that current research has focused on overt forms of sycophancy toward users' beliefs, leaving more subtle and person-directed behaviors relatively understudied. Second, we surveyed 106 experts in AI sycophancy and related fields to examine whether researchers agree on which model behaviors are sycophantic. While experts are nearly unanimous in believing that sycophancy is a significant problem in current AI systems (94.3% agree), they disagree substantially on which specific behaviors qualify. Together, these findings demonstrate that AI sycophancy is a broad family of behaviors with different measurement challenges, intervention requirements, and governance implications. Our taxonomy provides a shared vocabulary for understanding and addressing these behaviors.

</details>


### [97] [Trace2Skill: Verifier-Guided Skill Evolution for Long-Context EDA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21810)
*Zijian Du,Nathaniel Pinckney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace2Skill 是一个无需微调硬件专用模型的测试时扩展框架，通过将智能体的自然语言技能视为可进化策略，利用重复 rollout 轨迹挖掘成功/失败模式，结合稠密验证器反馈生成任务特定技能，显著提升复杂 Verilog 设计任务的通过率，甚至实现先前未解决任务的突破性通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂 Verilog 设计问题（CVDP）对硬件 LLM 智能体构成挑战，因为需要在大型仓库快照中定位验证器相关的 RTL、测试平台、包含路径和构建依赖，进行精确编辑，并从稀疏的隐藏验证器失败中恢复。现有方法要么训练新模型，要么仅采样更多候选解，效率低下。

Method: Trace2Skill 将智能体的自然语言技能视为可进化策略，通过重复 rollout 轨迹挖掘成功和失败模式，将其转换为密集诊断和 oracle 经验，利用 oracle、mutator 和 selector 循环生成任务特定技能，以指导后续搜索、编辑、验证和恢复。同时支持有界运行时稠密验证器反馈，返回经消毒的功能观测结果，同时隐藏测试平台和参考解。

Result: 在击败种子 CVDP 智能体的困难任务上（包括也击败前沿编码智能体的任务），Trace2Skill 结合稠密验证器反馈显著提升了任务通过率，并在先前未解决的任务上取得了突破性通过。

Conclusion: Trace2Skill 提供了一种通用的测试时扩展策略，无需高质量微调数据、专用 RTL 模型训练或模型权重更新，即可显著提升硬件智能体在复杂 Verilog 设计任务上的表现，且可扩展到数字设计之外的其他可验证 EDA 任务。

Abstract: Complex Verilog Design Problems (CVDP) challenge hardware LLM agents because solving them requires localizing verifier-relevant RTL, testbenches, include paths, and build dependencies inside large repository snapshots, making precise edits, and recovering from sparse hidden-verifier failures. We present Trace2Skill, a test-time scaling framework that improves a hardware agent without RTL-specialized model fine-tuning. Rather than training a new model or only sampling more candidate solutions, Trace2Skill treats the agent's natural-language skill as an evolvable policy. It mines repeated rollout traces for success and failure modes, converts them into dense diagnostics and oracle lessons, and uses an oracle, mutator, and selector loop to produce task-specific skills that guide later search, editing, validation, and recovery. Because final pass/fail labels are often too coarse for hard failures, Trace2Skill also supports bounded runtime dense verifier feedback that returns sanitized functional observations while keeping hidden harnesses and reference solutions inaccessible to the agent. This feedback helps guide skill evolution and agent execution by connecting skill text, verifier evidence, and downstream behavior. Across hard CVDP tasks that defeat the seed CVDP agent, including tasks that also defeat frontier coding agents, Trace2Skill with dense verifier feedback substantially improves task pass rates and produces breakthrough passes on previously unsolved tasks, without requiring high-quality fine-tuning data, specialized RTL model training, or model weight updates. The same framework provides a general test-time scaling strategy that can extend beyond digital design to other verifiable EDA tasks.

</details>


### [98] [Implicit Safety Alignment from Crowd Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21822)
*Qian Lin,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Safe Crowd Preference-based RL框架，从群体偏好中提取安全对齐技能，以层级方式组合解决下游任务，在不获取显式安全奖励的情况下显著降低安全成本，同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中的群体偏好数据集蕴含共享安全准则，但不同用户可能表达不同偏好。现有直接奖励组合方法存在固有局限性，需要一种能从群体偏好中提取共享安全准则并将其迁移到下游任务的方法。

Method: 提出Safe Crowd Preference-based RL层级框架：首先从群体偏好数据中提取安全对齐的技能，然后通过高层策略将这些技能组合起来，以安全地解决下游任务。

Result: 在安全RL环境和包含大语言模型风格任务的实验中，该方法在不需要显式安全奖励的情况下，显著降低了安全成本，且任务性能与使用真实安全信号的oracle方法相当。

Conclusion: 从群体偏好中提取共享安全准则并迁移到下游任务是可行且有效的，Safe Crowd Preference-based RL克服了直接奖励组合的局限性，为无需显式安全信号的安全RL提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) can reveal implicit objectives such as safety considerations that go beyond task completion. In this work, we focus on the common safety criteria embedded in crowd preference datasets, where different users may express distinct preferences or objectives, yet follow similar safety principles. Our aim is to discover shared safety criteria from crowd preferences and then transfer them to downstream RL tasks to regularize agent behavior and enforce safety. We first show that direct reward combination-optimizing a preference-learned reward model together with downstream task rewards-has inherent limitations. Motivated by this, we propose Safe Crowd Preference-based RL, a hierarchical framework that extracts safety-aligned skills from crowd preferences and composes them via a high-level policy to safely solve downstream tasks. Experiments across safe RL environments and a preliminary LLM-style task with diverse user goals and shared safety constraints demonstrate that our approach substantially lowers safety costs without access to explicit safety rewards, while achieving task performance comparable to oracle methods trained with ground-truth safety signals.

</details>


### [99] [Toward AI VIS Co-Scientists: A General and End-to-End Agent Harness for Solving Complex Data Visualization Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21825)
*Haichao Miao,Zhimin Li,Kuangshi Ai,Kaiyuan Tang,Chaoli Wang,Peer-Timo Bremer,Shusen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端自主智能体系统，仅根据数据和高层任务描述即可独立设计定制化可视化分析应用（VIS Apps），并在IEEE SciVis竞赛数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学领域中检查、解释和沟通复杂数据的能力至关重要，但往往需要跨领域专业知识（数据管理、分析、可视化设计与实现）。现有方法缺乏端到端自主性，无法基于高层指令独立完成长周期任务。

Method: 提出VIS co-scientist框架，包含多个智能体和专门技能模块，协调完成探索性分析、规划、环境配置、实现、界面验证以及任务完成度评估。每个阶段生成文档和指令制品以指导下游工作并支持迭代优化。

Result: 在IEEE SciVis竞赛数据上验证，系统仅凭数据和目标任务即可自主生成功能性单页VIS Apps，具备验证过的联动视图行为，高度定制化以满足领域专家的指定任务和需求。

Conclusion: 该工作代表了向通用AI co-scientist愿景迈出的重要一步，VIS co-scientist作为其核心组件，能够自主分析数据并设计可视化解决方案。

Abstract: The ability to inspect, interpret, and communicate complex data is crucial for virtually any scientific endeavor, but often requires significant expertise outside the core domain ranging from data management and analysis to visualization design and implementation. We present an end-to-end agentic harness that, based on only the data and a high level description of the tasks, independently designs custom visual analysis applications (VIS apps). This represents an important step towards a general AI co-scientist envisioned by many as an autonomous system that can autonomously execute long horizon tasks based on high-level directions. Our proposed VIS co-scientist is an essential component of this broader AI co-scientist vision: a harness that can autonomously analyze data and design visualization solutions using a collection of agents and specialized skills that coordinate exploratory analysis, plan, configure the environment, implement, validate the interface, and most importantly evaluate the overall task completion. Each stage produces document and instruction artifacts that guide downstream work and enable iterative refinement. We validate this approach on IEEE SciVis Contests spanning multiple science and engineering fields. These contests serve as ideal proving grounds because they encode real-world complexity: ambiguous requirements, diverse data modalities, design trade-offs, and task-driven validation. Given only the data and target tasks, our system autonomously produces functional single-page VIS Apps with verified linked-view behavior, highly customized to domain experts' specified tasks and needs.

</details>


### [100] [FLUID: From Ephemeral IDs to Multimodal Semantic Codes for Industrial-Scale Livestreaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21832)
*Xinhang Yuan,Zexi Huang,Anjia Cao,Xudong Lu,Zikai Wang,Penghao Zhou,Chang Liu,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLUID是首个在生产规模的直播推荐系统中完全移除物品ID的框架。它通过跨领域多模态编码器（联合训练短视频和直播）生成离散层次化编码LUCID，采用无ID的晚期融合设计，将切片级和房间级LUCID作为独立token注入，并通过分阶段热身训练稳定在线增量学习。在超十亿用户规模的工业直播推荐系统中部署后，FLUID在质量观看时长、冷启房间观看数和活跃小时数上分别获得+0.55%、+2.05%和+0.05%的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播推荐面临ID冷启动难题：直播间仅持续数十分钟，物品ID嵌入在持久冷启动状态下难以充分学习，导致基于ID的排序模型无法泛化到新开播房间。

Method: FLUID采用跨领域多模态编码器（联合训练短视频和直播）生成离散层次化编码LUCID，替代传统ID嵌入；采用无ID的晚期融合设计，将切片级和房间级LUCID作为独立token注入排序模型；通过分阶段热身训练来稳定在线增量学习。

Result: 在超十亿全球用户的工业直播推荐系统中部署后，FLUID在质量观看时长、冷启房间观看数和活跃小时数上分别提升+0.55%、+2.05%和+0.05%。

Conclusion: FLUID验证了在直播推荐场景中完全移除候选侧物品ID的可行性和有效性，为冷启动问题提供了全新的解决范式。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems rely heavily on ID-based collaborative filtering: each item is represented by a unique ID embedding that accumulates collaborative signals from user interactions. Livestreaming recommendation, however, faces a unique challenge in this paradigm: a live room typically broadcasts for only tens of minutes, so its item ID remains poorly learned in a persistent cold-start state and ID-centric ranking models fail to generalize. We present FLUID, the first framework to fully retire the candidate-side item ID from a production-scale livestreaming ranker. FLUID couples a cross-domain multimodal encoder, jointly trained on short videos and livestreams to produce discrete hierarchical codes (LUCID), with a late-fusion, ID-free design that injects slice-level and room-level LUCID as independent tokens, stabilized by a staged warmup under online incremental training. Deployed on our industrial livestreaming recommenders with a cross-platform combined user base of over one billion globally, FLUID delivers significant online gains of +0.55% Quality Watch Duration, +2.05% Cold-Start Room Views, and +0.05% Active Hours.

</details>


### [101] [Planning in the LLM Era: Building for Reliability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21902)
*Michael Katz,Harsha Kokel,Kavitha Srinivas,Shirin Sohrabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了LLM在规划任务中的应用从单次生成、混合搜索到规划器生成的演变趋势，指出研究重点正转向利用LLM生成可验证的符号规划器，以提升可靠性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在规划任务中的局限性日益明显（不完备、资源消耗大），研究者需要更可靠、高效的规划方法。本文旨在梳理这一转变趋势，并指引未来研究方向。

Method: 本文通过文献综述和分类分析，将LLM-based规划器生成方法分为三大类，并分别讨论其优缺点、局限性及未来研究步骤。

Result: 识别出LLM在规划中从单次生成→混合搜索→规划器生成的三阶段演变；指出当前方法在可靠性、效率和可维护性方面的不足；提出未来研究方向。

Conclusion: LLM时代规划领域正在经历一次广泛的重整，从直接使用LLM进行规划转向利用LLM生成可验证、高效的符号规划器，这一趋势有望提升智能体的可靠性和资源效率。

Abstract: Growing attention to intelligent agents has put a spotlight on one of their central capabilities: planning. Early attempts to leverage large language models (LLMs) for planning relied on single-shot plan generation, followed by hybrid approaches that coupled LLMs with limited external search. These methods, unsound and incomplete by their very nature, often require substantial resources without yielding better solutions on unseen problems. As the limitations of LLMs become clearer, recent work has shifted toward using them at solution construction time -- generating symbolic solvers for a family of problems that can be verified and then used efficiently at inference time. This trend reflects the growing need for agents that are both reliable and resource-efficient. It also offers a path towards generating maintainable planners with minimal dependence on language models at inference time. In this paper, we argue that this shift reflects a broader realignment of the planning field in the LLM era. We examine three major categories of planner-generation methods, discuss their current limitations, and outline research steps towards a more reliable and efficient LLM-based generation of planners.

</details>


### [102] [AI-Enabled Serious Games: Integrating Intelligence and Adaptivity in Training Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21962)
*Priyamvada Tripathi,Bill Kapralos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何利用当代AI方法（如大语言模型、强化学习和基于智能体的架构）支持严肃游戏中的实时教学适应，区分了教学智能和适应性概念，并系统综述了从早期计算机辅助教学到AI驱动系统的演进历程，同时指出了可解释性、验证、计算成本等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严肃游戏在医疗、国防、教育等领域广泛应用，但仍面临场景设计静态化、创作瓶颈、学习者建模不足以及难以实现有意义的实时教学适应等长期挑战。

Method: 通过历史综合分析方法，追溯了从早期计算机辅助教学、智能辅导系统、动态难度调整、创作平台、学习分析到现代AI架构的发展脉络，并系统讨论了LLM、RL和智能体架构在严肃游戏中的潜在贡献。

Result: 当代AI方法（LLM、RL、智能体架构）能够实现动态场景变化、上下文反馈、自适应节奏和学习者状态建模，有望缓解严肃游戏的现有局限；同时揭示了AI集成带来的可解释性、验证、计算成本和长期学习效果证据不足等问题。

Conclusion: AI为严肃游戏的实时教学适应提供了新可能，但在实际落地前需解决透明度、系统控制、学习者信任以及实证证据缺乏等关键挑战。

Abstract: Serious games are widely used for learning and training across domains such as healthcare, defense, and education. Persistent challenges remain, however, including static scenario design, authoring bottlenecks, limited learner modeling, and difficulty implementing meaningful real-time instructional adaptation. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) introduce novel capabilities such as dynamic scenario variation, contextual feedback, adaptive pacing, and learner-state modeling that may help address some of these limitations. At the same time, integrating AI into serious games raises important questions related to validity, transparency, system control, and learner trust. This chapter examines how contemporary AI approaches may support real-time instructional adaptation in serious games. It distinguishes between instructional intelligence, defined as a system's capacity to infer learner knowledge and reason about pedagogically appropriate responses, and adaptivity, defined as the ability to modify instructional actions during interaction. A historical synthesis of adaptive learning systems is presented, tracing developments from early computer-assisted instruction through intelligent tutoring systems (ITS), dynamic difficulty adjustment (DDA), authoring platforms, learning analytics, and recent AI-enabled architectures. Building on this perspective, the chapter discusses how large language models (LLMs), reinforcement learning (RL), and agent-based architectures may contribute to more integrated forms of intelligence and adaptivity in serious games. It also highlights practical and research challenges associated with AI-enabled systems, including explainability, validation, computational cost, and the limited empirical evidence regarding long-term learning outcomes in AI-enabled serious games.

</details>


### [103] [Format-Constraint Coupling in Knowledge Graph Construction from Statistical Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21974)
*Jingxuan Qi,Zhiqiang Ye,Yuxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了一种称为"格式-约束耦合"的现象：当统计CSV（尤其是宽格式Type-II矩阵）应用不匹配的抽取模式时，模式约束与序列化格式会超加性地交互，导致知识图谱保真度严重下降（事实覆盖率低于无约束基线），并通过列名引用的表面形式锚定机制解释该现象。同时发现标准检索模式会掩盖构建质量问题，而直接图访问可暴露高达+47.6pp的差距。为支持保真度感知评估，发布了包含15个数据集、1892个黄金标准事实的CSVFidelity-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱抽取研究忽视了抽取模式与数据序列化格式之间的交互效应。在开放数据门户上常见的国家-年份时间序列矩阵（CSV格式）中，这种交互可能导致知识图谱保真度意外降低，即使模式设计本身合理。作者旨在系统研究并量化这种耦合效应，并开发相应的评估基准。

Method: 采用2×2因子实验设计（有/无模式约束 × 匹配/不匹配格式），在6个数据集上量化联合效应与独立效应之和的差异（超加性效应达+1.180）。通过Bootstrap 95%置信区间检验统计显著性。利用探针分析（probing）和令牌消融（token ablation）验证表面形式锚定（surface-form anchoring）的解释机制。在跨格式-模式配对、GraphRAG宿主、LLM家族等维度进行控制变体实验。对比标准检索模式（3种）与直接图访问的质量暴露能力。

Result: 格式与模式约束的联合效应超过各自独立效应之和，最高达+1.180；6个数据集中有4个的Bootstrap 95%置信区间严格为正，宽Type-II矩阵证据最强。不匹配的格式-模式组合导致6/4数据集事实覆盖率低于无约束基线，表现为实体膨胀或抽取拒绝。标准检索模式将质量差距掩盖在≤1个百分点内，而直接图访问暴露的差距高达+47.6个百分点（p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: 格式与模式约束之间存在显著的超加性耦合效应（格式-约束耦合），可导致知识图谱保真度灾难性下降，原因在于表面形式锚定机制。标准检索模式会掩盖图谱构建质量问题，需采用直接图访问进行保真度感知评估。CSVFidelity-Bench为相关研究提供了标准化评估资源。

Abstract: An extraction schema should not reduce knowledge graph fidelity. On statistical CSV, however, it can. We study country-by-year time-series matrices, a common layout on open-data portals. In this setting, serialization format and schema constraints interact super-additively. Their joint effect exceeds the sum of independent effects by up to +1.180 (2x2 factorial, 6 datasets). Bootstrap 95% CIs are strictly positive on 4/6 datasets, with strongest evidence on wide Type-II matrices. More critically, a schema applied to a mismatched format can trigger catastrophic mismatch. Fact coverage falls below the unconstrained baseline on 4/6 datasets through entity inflation or extraction refusal. We call this observed pattern format-constraint coupling. Probing and token ablation support a surface-form anchoring explanation centred on column-name references. Controlled variants across format-schema pairings, GraphRAG hosts, and LLM families show the same direction within the measured scope; one LLM family shows only partial activation. The observation also has a diagnostic consequence. Three standard retrieval modes largely mask construction quality (delta <= 1pp), whereas direct graph access exposes gaps up to +47.6pp (p < 0.0001). To support fidelity-aware evaluation, we release CSVFidelity-Bench. It contains 15 datasets, 11 Type-II matrices, 4 Type-III tables, and 1,892 Gold Standard facts across 6 domains.

</details>


### [104] [Echo: Learning from Experience Data via User-Driven Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21984)
*Hande Dong,Xiaoyun Liang,Jiarui Yu,Jiayi Lin,Changqing Ai,Feng Liu,Wenjun Zhang,Rongbi Wei,Chaofan Zhu,Linjie Che,Feng Wu,Xin Shen,Dexu Kong,Xiaotian Wang,Qiuyuan Chen,Bingxu An,Yueting Lei,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Echo框架，通过利用用户对AI智能体输出的修正序列，将原始交互经验转化为高质量训练信号，实现模型持续对齐。在代码补全生产环境中验证，将接受率从25.7%提升至35.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态人类数据存在扩展成本高且受限于创建者知识的问题；连续学习来自于智能体与环境交互的"经验数据"有望突破这些限制，但原始交互日志噪声大、信息密度低，直接用于模型训练效率低下。

Method: 提出Echo框架：利用用户对智能体初始输出的修正过程——用户出于对结果负责，会将有缺陷的智能体提案转化为经过验证的解决方案，这些修正序列天然蒸馏出高质量训练信号。Echo系统性地收集这些信号，持续将智能体与真实世界需求对齐。

Result: 在代码补全生产环境中大规模验证，将指标接受率从25.7%提升至35.7%，突破了静态性能天花板。

Conclusion: Echo有效利用用户修正流水线，将原始经验转化为可学习的知识，证明了从智能体与环境的交互反馈中持续学习能够超越静态数据的性能上限。

Abstract: Static "human data" faces inherent limitations: it is expensive to scale and bounded by the knowledge of its creators. Continuous learning from "experience data" - interactions between agents and their environments - promises to transcend these barriers. Today, the widespread deployment of AI agents grants us low-cost access to massive streams of such real-world experience. However, raw interaction logs are inherently noisy, filled with trial-and-error and low information density, rendering them inefficient for direct model training.
  We introduce Echo, a generalized framework designed to operationalize the transition from raw experience to learnable knowledge, effectively "echoing" environmental feedback back into the training loop for model optimization. In today's agent ecosystem, user refinement serves as a primary source of such feedback: driven by responsibility for the outcome, users rigorously transform flawed agent proposals into verified solutions. These user-driven refinement sequences inherently distill agents' crude attempts into high-quality training signals. Echo systematically harvests these signals to continuously align the agent with real-world needs. Large-scale validation in a production code completion environment confirms that Echo effectively harnesses this pipeline, breaking the static performance ceiling by increasing the acceptance rate from 25.7% to 35.7%.

</details>


### [105] [The Log is the Agent: Event-Sourced Reactive Graphs for Auditable, Forkable Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21997)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActiveGraph提出了一种翻转传统智能体框架架构的运行时系统，以仅追加的事件日志为唯一真理源，通过确定性工作图投影和基于图变化的反应式行为，实现了确定性重放、廉价分支和端到端血缘追溯三大特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架以语言模型为中心，将日志和可观测性作为事后附加层，状态以可检索的"记忆"方式持久化，缺乏确定性重放、分支和完整血缘追溯能力。

Method: 构建以仅追加事件日志为真理源的运行时系统，工作图为日志的确定性投影，行为（普通函数、类、LLM例程或附着在类型化边上的逻辑）响应图变化并发射新事件，组件间通过共享图而非直接指令进行协调。

Result: 实现了确定性重放（可从日志重构任意运行）、廉价分支（从任意事件分支运行而无需重放共享前缀）和端到端血缘追溯（从高层目标到产生每个制品的单个模型调用）。

Conclusion: 该架构特别适合自改进智能体，扩展了BabyAGI谱系和先前图记忆研究的成果。

Abstract: Most agent frameworks are built around the language model: a conversation loop comes first, then tools, then rules, and finally a logging layer bolted on for observability, with state persisted as retrievable "memory." We describe ActiveGraph, a runtime that inverts this arrangement. The append-only event log is the source of truth; the working graph is a deterministic projection of that log; and behaviors--ordinary functions, classes, LLM-backed routines, or logic attached to typed edges--react to changes in the graph and emit new events. No component instructs another; coordination happens entirely through the shared graph. This single design decision yields three properties that retrieval-and-summarization memory systems do not provide: deterministic replay of any run from its log, cheap forking that branches a run at any event without re-executing the shared prefix, and end-to-end lineage from a high-level goal down to the individual model call that produced each artifact. We present the architecture, a determinism contract that makes replay sound, and a worked diligence example whose full causal structure is reconstructable from the log alone. We discuss--without claiming to demonstrate--why this substrate is unusually well suited to self-improving agents, and how it extends the BabyAGI lineage and prior graph-memory research.

</details>


### [106] [Active Evidence-Seeking and Diagnostic Reasoning in Large Language Models for Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22047)
*Chen Zhan,Xihe Qiu,Xiaoyu Tan,Xibing Zhuang,Gengchen Ma,Yue Zhang,Shuo Li,Peifeng Liu,Xiaoxiao Ge,Liang Liu,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在静态医学考试中表现良好，但该研究通过引入OSCE风格的标准化患者模拟器和主动诊断查询基准发现，在多轮证据采集场景下，模型的诊断准确率下降12.75%，支持证据质量下降24.36%，主要归因于过早诊断闭合和低效提问。这表明静态全上下文基准可能高估了模型在实际交互式诊断中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多基于静态医学考试，无法反映临床诊断中在不确定性下迭代收集证据的真实需求。作者希望构建一个更贴近实际临床交互的诊断评估方法。

Method: 基于OSCE（客观结构化临床考试）理念设计了标准化患者模拟器，构建了包含468个病例和15个模型的可控可重复的主动诊断查询基准，对比多轮交互式证据采集与全上下文静态评估的表现差异。

Result: 多轮证据采集相比全上下文评估，诊断准确率下降12.75%，支持证据质量下降24.36%。错误分析表明性能下降主要源于过早诊断闭合和低效提问策略。

Conclusion: 静态全上下文基准可能高估模型在交互式证据采集环境中的表现，建议引入互补的交互式评估方法以实现更安全的临床决策支持。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on static medical examinations, yet clinical diagnosis often requires iterative evidence gathering under uncertainty. Building on prior interactive evaluation efforts, we introduce an OSCE-inspired standardized patient simulator and a controlled, reproducible benchmark for active diagnostic inquiry. Across 468 cases and 15 models in our protocol, we observe that multi-turn evidence seeking reduces diagnostic accuracy by 12.75% and lowers supporting-evidence quality by 24.36% relative to full-context evaluation; error analyses associate these drops with premature diagnostic closure and inefficient questioning. Together, these results suggest that static full-context benchmarks may overestimate performance in interactive evidence-seeking settings, motivating complementary interactive assessment for safer clinical decision support.

</details>


### [107] [Enhancing Visual Token Representations for Video Large Language Models via Training-Free Spatial-Temporal Pooling and Gridding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22078)
*Bingjun Luo,Tony Wang,Hanqi Chen,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ST-GridPool，一种无需训练的视频视觉Token增强方法，通过金字塔时间网格化（PTG）和基于范数的空间池化（NSP）分别捕获多粒度时空交互和保留高信息区域，显著提升Video LLM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM（如LLaVA系列）在视频理解中采用简单的池化或插值技术压缩视觉token，忽略了视觉token的复杂时空动态，导致效率与性能瓶颈。

Method: 1）金字塔时间网格化（PTG）：通过分层时间网格化捕获多粒度时空交互；2）基于范数的空间池化（NSP）：利用token范数与语义丰富度的相关性，保留高信息视觉区域。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，ST-GridPool无需重新训练即可持续提升Video LLM的性能，是一种高效、即插即用的解决方案。

Conclusion: ST-GridPool作为一种训练自由的视觉token增强方法，有效解决了视频理解中视觉token压缩与时空交互保持的权衡问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly advanced video understanding tasks, yet challenges remain in efficiently compressing visual tokens while preserving spatiotemporal interactions. Existing methods, such as LLaVA family, utilize simplistic pooling or interpolation techniques that overlook the intricate dynamics of visual tokens. To bridge this gap, we propose ST-GridPool, a novel training-free visual token enhancement method designed specifically for Video LLMs. Our approach integrates Pyramid Temporal Gridding (PTG), which captures multi-grained spatiotemporal interactions through hierarchical temporal gridding, and Norm-based Spatial Pooling (NSP), which preserves high-information visual regions by leveraging the correlation between token norms and semantic richness. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate that ST-GridPool consistently enhances performance of Video LLMs without requiring costly retraining. Our method offers an efficient and plug-and-play solution for improving visual token representations. Our code is available in https://github.com/bingjunluo/ST-GridPool.

</details>


### [108] [A Camera-Cooperative ISAC Framework for Multimodal Non-Cooperative UAVs Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22090)
*Wenfeng Wu,Luping Xiang,Kun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种相机协作的ISAC框架（CC-ISAC），通过视觉与雷达回波的多模态融合，实现无人机波束指向与跟踪。在DeepSense 6G数据集上的实验表明，该框架平均减少71%的波束指向开销和1.69-11.15%的跟踪开销，同时保持高角度估计精度，有效缓解了感知与通信之间的资源竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作无人机检测对ISAC系统构成重大挑战：单模态感知能力固有局限，且共享的通信与感知资源存在竞争关系，导致感知精度和资源效率受限。

Method: 提出CC-ISAC框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) Vision-to-Echo Data Alignment (V2EDA)模型，通过交叉注意力机制对齐视觉与回波域特征；(2) Multimodal Fusion-Based Estimation (MMFE)模型，融合历史多模态数据与当前观测进行鲁棒状态估计。相机负责粗粒度空域监控，ISAC负责细粒度高精度感知，形成互补感知回路。

Result: 在DeepSense 6G数据集上，波束指向开销平均降低71%，跟踪开销降低1.69-11.15%，同时保持高角度估计精度。

Conclusion: CC-ISAC框架有效缓解了感知与通信之间的资源竞争，实现可靠的无人机监视，同时释放大量系统资源用于额外通信任务，代表了ISAC系统设计的实际进展。

Abstract: The detection of non-cooperative unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) presents significant challenges for Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) systems due to the inherent limitations of single-modal perception and the competition for shared communication and sensing resources. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel Camera-Cooperative ISAC (CC-ISAC) framework that employs multimodal sensing to enable efficient UAV beam steering and tracking. The proposed framework employs cameras for coarse-grained airspace monitoring and utilizes ISAC for fine-grained, high-precision sensing, forming a complementary perception loop that enhances both sensing accuracy and resource efficiency. Within this framework, two key modules are developed: (1) a Vision-to-Echo Data Alignment (V2EDA) model that aligns visual and echo-domain features through cross-attention mechanisms, and (2) a Multimodal Fusion-Based Estimation (MMFE) model that integrates historical multimodal data with current observations for robust state estimation. Extensive evaluations conducted on the DeepSense 6G dataset demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves an average reduction of 71% in beam steering overhead and 1.69-11.15% in tracking overhead while maintaining high angular estimation accuracy. The CC-ISAC framework effectively mitigates resource contention between sensing and communication, enabling reliable UAV surveillance while freeing substantial system resources for additional communication tasks, thereby representing a practical advancement in ISAC system design.

</details>


### [109] [Knowledge Graph Re-engineering Along the Ontological Continuum (extended version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22093)
*Enrico Daga,Valentina Tamma,Terry Payne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"本体连续统"这一理论框架，通过语义vs语用、属性vs可供性两个正交维度来描述、比较、导航和转换知识图谱，以解决KG建模实践多样性带来的集成困难问题，并通过溯源知识案例研究加以验证，最后提出五个开放研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱建模实践的多样性（从轻量级词汇表到丰富公理化的本体）使得集成和重用变得昂贵且脆弱，尤其在神经符号AI中，桥接神经和符号组件需要对KG进行再工程以适应新需求。现有GenAI提供了自动化能力，但缺乏对KG空间的原理性理解。

Method: 提出本体连续统概念，通过两个正交维度（语义vs语用、属性vs可供性）定义表征框架；采用经验主义方法论，基于对真实世界KG工程实践的观察来构建理论，并可通过形式概念分析（FCA）进行形式化显式表达。

Result: 通过溯源知识案例研究展示了同一关注点在本体连续统上的不同表现形式；识别并阐述了五个开放研究挑战。

Conclusion: 本体连续统为理解和操作KG的全谱系建模实践提供了理论框架，邀请社区将其发展为共享的研究议程。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have become the primary vehicle for data integration and are critical to the success of modern AI, but the diversity of KG modelling practices, from lightweight vocabularies to richly axiomatised ontologies, makes integration and reuse expensive and brittle. This challenge is particularly acute in neuro-symbolic AI, where bridging neural and symbolic components depends on the ability to reengineer KGs to fit new requirements; GenAI now offers unprecedented automation capability, but without a principled understanding of the KG space, such automation remains conceptually ungrounded. We introduce the ontological continuum as that missing conceptualisation, a theoretical construct a theoretical construct whose characterisation framework is defined by two orthogonal distinctions: semantics vs pragmatics, and properties vs affordances; together these define a vocabulary to describe, compare, navigate, and transform KGs across the full range of modelling practices. The methodological stance is empirical: rather than prescribing how KGs should be modelled, the continuum aims to define a theory of the existent, derived from observation of real-world KG engineering practices and whose structure can be made formally explicit, for example, through Formal Concept Analysis (FCA). We ground the vision through a case study on provenance knowledge, showing how a single concern manifests differently across the continuum. We articulate five open research challenges and invite the community to develop the ontological continuum as a shared research agenda.

</details>


### [110] [MPDocBench-Parse: Benchmarking Practical Multi-page Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22100)
*Bangbang Zhou,Hangdi Xing,Yifan Chen,Jianjun Xu,Qi Zheng,Feiyu Gao,Zhibo Yang,Shuai Bai,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MPDocBench-Parse是一个面向多页文档解析的全面基准测试，包含433份手动标注文档（3246页），涵盖15种文档类型和英中双语，支持端到端评估内容保真度和逻辑结构，实验表明现有模型在语义连续性、视觉内容解析和层次结构恢复方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析基准存在三大不足：仅关注单一任务或单页、文本中心场景；缺乏对语义连续性、层次结构恢复和视觉内容保真度的细粒度评估；无法满足实际多页文档解析需求。

Method: 构建包含433份文档（3246页）的多页文档解析基准，覆盖15种中英文文档类型及多样化布局风格；设计涵盖文本/表格/公式识别、截断文本与表格合并、图形提取、阅读顺序和标题层次恢复等内容保真度和逻辑结构的综合评估协议。

Result: 现有模型在基础文本提取上表现良好，但在语义连续性整合、视觉内容解析和层次结构恢复方面存在明显局限性。

Conclusion: MPDocBench-Parse为将文档解析推向更真实的应用场景提供了统一基础。

Abstract: Document parsing converts visually rich documents into machine-readable structured representations, forming a crucial foundation for information systems. Although many benchmarks have been proposed for document parsing, they remain inadequate for realistic scenarios. Existing benchmarks either focus on specific tasks or assess only single-page, text-centric settings, making them insufficient for practical multi-page parsing. Moreover, they lack fine-grained evaluation of semantic continuity, hierarchical structure recovery, and visual content preservation. To address these gaps, we propose MPDocBench-Parse, a benchmark for multi-page document parsing in real-world applications. It contains 433 manually annotated documents with 3,246 pages, covering 15 document types in English and Chinese, with diverse layout styles, and supports document-level end-to-end evaluation. We further design a comprehensive protocol for content fidelity and logical structure, covering text, table, and formula recognition, truncated text and table merging, figure extraction, reading order, and heading hierarchy recovery.
