Experiments show that, while existing models perform well on basic text extraction, they still suffer clear limitations in semantic continuity integration, visual content parsing, and hierarchical structure recovery. MPDocBench-Parse provides a unified foundation for advancing document parsing toward more realistic scenarios.

</details>


### [111] [ExComm: Exploration-Stage Communication for Error-Resilient Agentic Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22102)
*Woomin Song,Beomjun Kim,Daewon Choi,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Saket Dingliwal,Jinwoo Shin,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ExComm是一种用于探索阶段智能体测试时扩展的通信协议，通过周期性审计智能体信念状态、检测跨智能体事实冲突、执行基于工具的验证循环以及提供针对性反馈，有效缓解长程任务中的错误传播问题。实验表明，ExComm在AIME 2024/2025和GAIA基准上比强基线方法平均提升5.7%和5.0%，并展现出更优的错误恢复能力、扩展行为和性能-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体测试时扩展中存在错误传播问题：中间步骤引入的事实错误或无效推演会持续污染智能体信念状态并影响后续推理。现有测试时扩展方法对此控制有限，多依赖智能体自行检测错误、在错误轨迹中选择或在错误已影响推理路径后才进行修正。

Method: ExComm协议包含三个核心组件：(1) 周期性审计智能体信念状态以检测跨智能体事实冲突；(2) 通过专用的基于工具的验证循环解决冲突，并返回简洁、针对性的反馈；(3) 采用软信念更新机制（附加已验证反馈而非覆盖原有信念）来整合修正。此外，引入轨迹多样化模块，将冗余轨迹重定向至正交策略，防止通信导致轨迹多样性崩溃。

Result: 在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和GAIA基准上，使用Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite和Qwen3.5-4B模型，ExComm一致优于强测试时扩展基线，平均性能增益分别为5.7%和5.0%。进一步分析表明，ExComm具有更优的错误恢复能力、有利的扩展行为、比适配的通信基线更强的多样性，以及最佳的性价比。

Conclusion: ExComm通过结构化通信协议有效缓解了长程智能体推理中的错误传播问题，为探索阶段测试时扩展提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案，在性能和成本之间取得了最佳平衡。

Abstract: A common failure mode in long-horizon agentic test-time scaling is error propagation, where factual errors or invalid deductions introduced at intermediate steps persist in the agent's belief state and contaminate later reasoning. Existing test-time scaling methods provide limited control over this process, as they often rely on agents to detect their own mistakes, select among flawed trajectories, or refine solutions only after errors have already shaped the reasoning path. We propose ExComm, a communication protocol for exploration-stage agentic test-time scaling. ExComm is motivated by the empirical observation that the majority of intermediate errors in parallel agentic reasoning produce detectable cross-agent factual conflicts. Leveraging the iterative structure of agentic workflows, ExComm periodically audits agent belief states to detect such conflicts, resolves them through a dedicated tool-based verification loop, and returns concise, targeted feedback to the involved agents. Corrections are incorporated through soft belief updates, which append verified feedback rather than overwriting existing beliefs. Furthermore, to prevent collapsing trajectory diversity due to communication, ExComm further introduces a trajectory diversification module that redirects redundant trajectories toward orthogonal strategies. Experiments on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and GAIA with Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite and Qwen3.5-4B show that ExComm consistently outperforms strong test-time scaling baselines, achieving average performance gains of 5.7% and 5.0% over the best-performing baselines, respectively. Further analyses demonstrate improved error recovery, favorable scaling behavior, stronger diversity than adapted communication baselines, and the best performance-cost trade-off among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [112] [ArborKV: Structure-Aware KV Cache Management for Scaling Tree-based LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22106)
*Yeqiu Chen,Ziyan Liu,Zhenxin Huang,Runquan Gui,Hong Wang,Lei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ArborKV是一种结构感知的KV缓存淘汰框架，通过树形搜索动态特性（活跃分支与祖先节点高复用、非活跃子树低短期复用但需可恢复）设计轻量值估计器与树感知分配策略，结合纯token提取淘汰与懒重水化机制，在ToT类推理中实现~4x峰值KV内存缩减且保持近乎完整保留精度，支持在固定硬件预算下运行更大搜索配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tree-of-Thoughts(ToT)推理中，树结构搜索的KV缓存成为内存瓶颈：保留前沿部分轨迹的KV状态会显著放大缓存占用，在固定硬件预算下限制搜索深度和宽度。

Method: 提出ArborKV框架：1) 轻量值估计器预测各分支的短期复用概率；2) 树感知分配策略按搜索动态差异化分配缓存资源；3) 纯token提取淘汰机制；4) 懒重水化支持回溯时恢复被淘汰的KV状态。

Result: 在ToT类推理基准上，ArborKV实现高达~4x峰值KV内存缩减，同时保持近乎完整保留精度，使固定设备预算下可运行原本会OOM的更大搜索配置。

Conclusion: 利用树搜索结构特性进行KV缓存管理能有效突破ToT推理的内存瓶颈，在精度几乎无损的前提下大幅降低峰值内存占用，为更大规模搜索配置提供了可行性。

Abstract: Recent progress in LLM reasoning has increasingly shifted from single-pass generation to explicit search over intermediate reasoning states. Tree-of-Thoughts (ToT) organizes inference to tree-structured search with branching and backtracking, but it substantially amplifies the Key--Value (KV) cache: retaining KV states for a frontier of partial trajectories quickly becomes a memory bottleneck that limits throughput and constrains search depth and width under fixed hardware budgets. We address this challenge by observing that KV reuse in ToT-style inference is governed by search dynamics: near-term decoding depends primarily on the active branch and its ancestors, whereas inactive subtrees have low short-term reuse probability yet must remain recoverable for backtracking. Motivated by this, we propose ArborKV, a structure-aware eviction framework that couples a lightweight value estimator with a tree-aware allocation policy, and performs purely token-extractive eviction with lazy rehydration to support revisits. Experiments on ToT-style reasoning benchmarks show that ArborKV achieves up to ~4x peak KV-memory reduction while preserving near-full-retention accuracy, enabling larger search configurations under fixed device budgets that would otherwise run out of memory.

</details>


### [113] [Perception or Prejudice: Can MLLMs Go Beyond First Impressions of Personality?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22109)
*Caixin Kang,Tianyu Yan,Sitong Gong,Mingfang Zhang,Liangyang Ouyang,Ruicong Liu,Bo Zheng,Huchuan Lu,Kaipeng Zhang,Yoichi Sato,Yifei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Grounded Personality Reasoning (GPR)任务，构建MM-OCEAN数据集（1,104视频，5,320 MCQ），设计三层次评估框架及四类失败模式指标（PR、CR、IR、HR），对27个MLLM进行基准测试，发现51%的正确评分缺乏依据，揭示MLLM存在Prejudice Gap——评分正确但推理错误的系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM人格感知评测仅关注Big Five分数预测的数值准确性，未验证模型是否真正通过行为理解来感知人格，还是仅依靠表面模式匹配进行预判。需要填补这一评估空白。

Method: (1) 形式化GPR任务，要求MLLM将每个Big Five评分锚定于可观察证据（评分→推理→依据链条）；(2) 构建MM-OCEAN数据集，采用多人验证的多智能体管线生成时间戳行为观察、依据支撑的人格分析及七类线索依据MCQ；(3) 设计三层评估（评分、推理、依据）及四项样本级失败指标：偏见率(PR)、捏造率(CR)、整合失败率(IR)、整体依据率(HR)；(4) 在27个MLLM（13闭源、14开源）上进行基准测试。

Result: 发现显著的Prejudice Gap：整体上51%的正确评分缺乏检索到的线索依据；整体依据率(HR)仅0-33.5%。表明模型虽然能得出正确的人格评分，但推理过程并非基于真实行为证据。

Conclusion: 现有MLLM在人格感知中存在"正确评分但错误推理"的脱节问题，为建立具有扎实社会认知能力的MLLM指明了方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in human-facing roles where personality perception is critical, yet existing benchmarks evaluate this capability solely on numerical Big Five score prediction, leaving open whether models truly perceive personality through behavioral understanding or merely prejudge through superficial pattern matching. We address this gap with three contributions. (i) A new task: we formalize Grounded Personality Reasoning (GPR), which requires MLLMs to anchor each Big Five rating in observable evidence through a chain of rating, reasoning, and grounding. (ii) A new dataset: we release MM-OCEAN (1,104 videos, 5,320 MCQs), produced by a multi-agent pipeline with human verification, with timestamped behavioral observations, evidence-grounded trait analyses, and seven categories of cue-grounding MCQs. (iii) Benchmark and analysis: we design a three-tier evaluation (rating, reasoning, grounding) plus four sample-level failure-mode metrics: Prejudice Rate (PR), Confabulation Rate (CR), Integration-failure Rate (IR), and Holistic-grounding Rate (HR), and benchmark 27 MLLMs (13 closed, 14 open). The analysis uncovers a striking Prejudice Gap: across the field, 51% of correct ratings are not grounded in retrieved cues, and the Holistic-Grounding Rate spans only 0-33.5%. These findings expose a disconnect between getting the right score and reasoning for the right reason, charting a roadmap for grounded social cognition in MLLMs.

</details>


### [114] [Efficient Agentic Reasoning Through Self-Regulated Simulative Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22138)
*Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou,Lara Sá Neves,Varad Pimpalkhute,Taylor W. Killian,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric P. Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SR$^2$AM框架，将智能体推理分解为三个系统：反应式执行（System I）、模拟推理（System II，基于世界模型进行未来状态预测）和自我调节（System III，通过学习配置器决定何时以及多深地规划）。该框架避免端到端训练中规划隐式涌现导致的低效token使用问题，在多项任务中以更少推理token达到或超越超大参数模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将智能体构建为具有自适应计算（如思维链）的反应式策略，通过端到端训练期望规划隐式涌现，但这种方式无法控制规划的存在、结构或深度，导致推理长度急剧增加，token使用效率低下且准确率不可靠。

Method: 将决策分解为三个系统：System I（反应式执行，处理细粒度动作）、System II（模拟推理，通过世界模型进行未来状态预测以实现统一规划）、System III（自我调节，通过学习配置器决定何时以及以何种深度进行规划）。SR$^2$AM在LLM的思维链中实现两个阶段：v0.1通过提示多模块系统记录决策，v1.0从预训练推理LLM的轨迹中重构结构化规划，先监督学习后强化学习训练。

Result: v0.1-8B和v1.0-30B在Pass@1指标上与120-355B和685B-1T参数的系统竞争；v1.0-30B比同类智能体LLM节省25.8-95.3%的推理token；强化学习使平均规划视野增加22.8%，而规划频率仅增加2.0%。

Conclusion: 学习得到的自我调节实现了让智能体决定何时规划、规划多深的机制，该原则可扩展至智能体如何管理自身学习和适应，为构建更高效的推理系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: How should an agent decide when and how to plan? A dominant approach builds agents as reactive policies with adaptive computation (e.g., chain-of-thought), trained end-to-end expecting planning to emerge implicitly. Without control over the presence, structure, or horizon of planning, these systems dramatically increase reasoning length, yielding inefficient token use without reliable accuracy gains. We argue efficient agentic reasoning benefits from decomposing decision-making into three systems: simulative reasoning (System II) grounding deliberation in future-state prediction via a world model; self-regulation (System III) deciding when and how deeply to plan via a learned configurator; and reactive execution (System I) handling fine-grained action. Simulative reasoning provides unified planning across diverse tasks without per-domain engineering, while self-regulation ensures the planner is invoked only when needed. To test this, we develop SR$^2$AM (Self-Regulated Simulative Reasoning Agentic LLM), realizing both as distinct stages within an LLM's chain-of-thought, with the LLM as world model. We explore two instantiations: recording decisions from a prompted multi-module system (v0.1) and reconstructing structured plans from traces of pretrained reasoning LLMs (v1.0), trained via supervised then reinforcement learning (RL). Across math, science, tabular analysis, and web information seeking, v0.1-8B and v1.0-30B achieve Pass@1 competitive with 120-355B and 685B-1T parameter systems respectively, while v1.0-30B uses 25.8-95.3% fewer reasoning tokens than comparable agentic LLMs. RL increases average planning horizon by 22.8% while planning frequency grows only 2.0%, showing it learns to plan further ahead rather than more often. More broadly, learned self-regulation instantiates a principle we expect to extend beyond planning to how agents govern their own learning and adaptation.

</details>


### [115] [Ratchet: A Minimal Hygiene Recipe for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22148)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Ratchet 是一个单智能体循环系统，让冻结的 LLM 自主管理技能库（编写、检索、整理、淘汰），通过退休机制和元技能引导等机制，在 MBPP+ hard-100 上将 pass@1 从 0.258 提升至峰值 0.658，在 SWE-bench Verified 上提升 +0.22，而无需人类干预的技能策划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 技能库（如 Voyager）存在瓶颈：LLM 自编写的技能带来 +0.0pp 的提升，而人类策划的技能提升 +16.2pp，说明问题不在于技能创作而在于技能生命周期管理。因此需要自动化技能维护机制。

Method: Ratchet 是一个单智能体循环，包含四个候选卫生机制：(1) 基于结果的退休机制；(2) 有界活跃技能上限；(3) 元技能作者指导；(4) 模式规范化。冻结的 LLM 在此循环中自主完成技能编写、检索、整理和淘汰。

Result: 在 MBPP+ hard-100 上用 Claude Opus 4.7，pass@1 从 0.258±0.047 基线提升到滚动均值 0.584（峰值 0.658±0.042），增益 +0.328±0.018，而无技能对照组漂移仅 +0.002±0.005。SWE-bench Verified 上峰值提升 +0.22（20 轮）。

Conclusion: 八组消融实验（A1-A8）表明：退休机制和元技能先验是关键支柱，显式去重（规范化、覆盖保护）被元技能自身所包含。非发散性定理证明：有界上限和退休阈值共同确保期望性能不会低于无技能基线。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill libraries, pioneered by Voyager, let frozen LLM agents accumulate reusable knowledge without weight updates, yet recent evaluation shows that LLM-authored skills deliver $+0.0$pp over no-skill baselines while human-curated ones deliver $+16.2$pp: the bottleneck is not skill authoring but lifecycle management. We introduce \textbf{Ratchet}, a single-agent loop in which a frozen LLM writes, retrieves, curates, and retires its own natural-language skills. Ratchet integrates four candidate hygiene mechanisms: outcome-driven retirement, a bounded active-cap, meta-skill authoring guidance, and pattern canonicalisation. On MBPP+ hard-100 with Claude Opus 4.7, Ratchet lifts held-out pass@1 from a $0.258 \pm 0.047$ baseline to a late-window rolling mean of $0.584$ (peak $0.658 \pm 0.042$) across 100 rounds and 3 seeds, a $+0.328 \pm 0.018$ rolling-mean gain where the no-skill control drifts at $+0.002 \pm 0.005$; the same recipe transfers to an agentic solver on SWE-bench Verified ($+0.22$ peak lift over 20 rounds). Eight ablations (A1--A8) reveal that the minimal working recipe is smaller than our design suggests: retirement and the meta-skill authoring prior are load-bearing, while explicit deduplication (canonicalisation, cover-guard) is subsumed by the meta-skill itself. A non-divergence proposition shows that bounded cap and retirement threshold together prevent expected performance from drifting below the no-skills floor.

</details>


### [116] [IdleSpec: Exploiting Idle Time via Speculative Planning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22154)
*Daewon Choi,Kyunghyun Park,Woomin Song,Saket Dingliwal,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Jinwoo Shin,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IdleSpec是一种利用空闲时间进行推理以提升LLM智能体性能的通用方法，通过在等待环境反馈时迭代生成规划候选，并在观测到达后聚合这些候选来指导下一步推理，在GAIA、FRAMES和MLE-Bench等基准上显著提升了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体系统在调用工具或与环境交互时会产生大量空闲等待时间，而现有工作要么将其视为不可避免的开销，要么提出忽略不同工具调用计算预算差异和未来观测不确定性的受限方案，导致空闲时间利用效率低下。

Method: IdleSpec在空闲期间迭代生成规划候选，通过从后验反馈更新的学习分布中采样两种互补的草稿策略（渐进式和恢复式）来应对观测不确定性，一旦观测可用即聚合这些候选以指导下一步推理。

Result: 在GAIA和FRAMES上，IdleSpec搭配Gemini-2.5-Flash达到55.6%平均准确率，比未利用空闲时间的基线提升5.1%；在涉及代码执行延迟的MLE-Bench上，Any Medal率提升高达9.1%。

Conclusion: IdleSpec有效利用智能体系统中的空闲时间来提升性能，同时最小化延迟开销，并且对长时任务具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents solve complex tasks by leveraging multi-step reasoning with iterative tool calls and environment interactions, which incur idle time while waiting for observations. Despite the prevalence of idle time in most agentic scenarios, existing works treat it as an unavoidable overhead or propose restricted solutions that overlook varying computational budgets across different tool calls and future observation uncertainty, thereby leading to suboptimal utilization of idle time. In this paper, we introduce IdleSpec, a scalable and generic inference approach that leverages idle-time computation to improve agent performance while minimizing latency overhead. Specifically, IdleSpec iteratively generates plan candidates during idle periods and, once observations become available, aggregates them to guide the next reasoning step. For effective plan generation under observation uncertainty, IdleSpec samples between complementary drafting strategies (i.e., progressive and recovery) from a learned distribution that is updated via posterior feedback. Our experiments demonstrate that IdleSpec significantly improves agent performance in various agentic scenarios by effectively utilizing idle time. In particular, on the GAIA and FRAMES, IdleSpec achieves 55.6% average accuracy with Gemini-2.5-Flash, surpassing the vanilla baseline without idle-time usage by 5.1%. Furthermore, for MLE-Bench, which involves substantial delay from code executions, IdleSpec achieves performance gains of up to 9.1% on the Any Medal rate, highlighting its generalizability to long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [117] [ST-SimDiff: Balancing Spatiotemporal Similarity and Difference for Efficient Video Understanding with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22158)
*Bingjun Luo,Tony Wang,Chaoqi Chen,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ST-SimDiff的无训练框架，通过构建时空图并采用基于相似性和时序差异性的并行双选策略，在保留静态和动态关键内容的同时大幅减少视觉token数量，显著提升MLLMs长视频处理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs处理长视频时计算开销大，虽有剪枝或合并token的方法，但忽视了视频内容中的变化与转折点这一关键维度，且缺乏对时空关系的协同建模。

Method: 1) 构建时空图统一建模视觉token的复杂关联；2) 并行双选策略：基于相似性的选择利用社区检测保留代表性token压缩静态信息；基于时序差异性的选择精确定位内容变化点以捕获关键动态转变。

Result: 在多个基准上显著优于现有最先进方法，同时大幅降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 相似性用于识别冗余，差异性用于捕获关键事件，这一新视角为高效视频token选择提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face significant computational overhead when processing long videos due to the massive number of visual tokens required. To improve efficiency, existing methods primarily reduce redundancy by pruning or merging tokens based on importance or similarity. However, these approaches largely overlook a critical dimension of video content, i.e., changes and turning points, and they lack a collaborative model for spatio-temporal relationships. To address this, we propose a new perspective: similarity is for identifying redundancy, while difference is for capturing key events. Based on this, we designed a training-free framework named ST-SimDiff. We first construct a spatio-temporal graph from the visual tokens to uniformly model their complex associations. Subsequently, we employ a parallel dual-selection strategy: 1) similarity-based selection uses community detection to retain representative tokens, compressing static information; 2) temporal difference-based selection precisely locates content-changing points to preserve tokens that capture key dynamic shifts. This allows it to preserve both static and dynamic content with a minimal number of tokens. Extensive experiments show our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches while substantially reducing computational costs. Our code is available in https://github.com/bingjunluo/ST-SimDiff.

</details>


### [118] [Adapting the Interface, Not the Model: Runtime Harness Adaptation for Deterministic LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22166)
*Tianshi Xu,Huifeng Wen,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Life-Harness 是一种生命周期感知的运行时框架，通过在不改变模型权重的情况下，将训练轨迹中的交互失败转化为可重用的干预措施，显著提升冻结 LLM 智能体在确定性环境中的性能，平均相对提升88.5%，并且具有跨模型迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体在确定性、规则驱动领域中的失败往往源于模型-环境接口不匹配，而非模型本身能力不足。现有方法主要更新模型参数，忽略了运行时框架（runtime harness）这一关键环节。

Method: 提出 Life-Harness，一种生命周期感知的运行时框架。它从训练轨迹中学习，将重复出现的交互失败转化为四类可重用干预措施：环境契约（environment contracts）、程序技能（procedural skills）、动作实现（action realization）和轨迹调控（trajectory regulation）。该框架在训练阶段演化，在评估阶段保持固定，无需修改模型权重或评估环境。

Result: 在来自 τ-bench、τ²-bench 和 AgentBench 的七个确定性环境中，Life-Harness 在 18 个模型骨干上的 126 个模型-环境设置中改进了 116 个，平均相对提升 88.5%。仅从 Qwen3-4B-Instruct 轨迹演化得到的框架可迁移至其他 17 个模型。

Conclusion: 运行时接口适应可以作为以模型为中心的智能体训练的有效补充方案。Life-Harness 捕捉的是环境侧的可复用结构而非模型特定行为，具有跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: LLM agents are shaped not only by their language models, but also by the runtime harness that mediates observation, tool use, action execution, feedback interpretation, and trajectory control. While existing agent adaptation methods mainly update model parameters, many failures in deterministic, rule-governed domains stem from mismatches at the model--environment interface. We propose Life-Harness, a lifecycle-aware runtime harness that improves frozen LLM agents without changing model weights or evaluation environments. Life-Harness evolves from training trajectories by converting recurring interaction failures into reusable interventions across environment contracts, procedural skills, action realization, and trajectory regulation, and remains fixed during held-out evaluation. On seven deterministic environments from $τ$-bench, $τ^2$-bench, and AgentBench, Life-Harness improves 116 out of 126 model--environment settings across 18 model backbones, with an average relative improvement of 88.5%. Harnesses evolved only from Qwen3-4B-Instruct trajectories transfer to 17 other models, showing that Life-Harness captures reusable environment-side structure rather than model-specific behavior. These results position runtime interface adaptation as a complementary alternative to model-centric agent training. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [119] [LLM-Metrics: Measuring Research Impact Through Large Language Model Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22176)
*Si Shen,Wenhua Zhao,Danhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-Metrics，一种利用大语言模型参数记忆来评估论文影响力的新指标。通过设计四类多选题探针，在549篇计算机科学论文和17个LLM上测试，发现LLM对论文的记忆强度与引用计数呈显著正相关（rho=0.1495, p=0.0004），为研究影响力评估提供了实时、跨学科、不依赖引用的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引用计数作为研究影响力评估指标存在时间滞后、学科偏差和马太效应等局限性，亟需一种更实时、公平的评估方法。

Method: 提出LLM-Metrics指标，基于大语言模型对论文的参数量化记忆强度。设计四类多选题探针（标题识别、作者识别、方法识别、会议/期刊识别），在17个0.5B至72B参数的LLM上评估549篇2023-2024年计算机科学论文，并与引用计数进行Spearman相关性分析。

Result: 17个模型中15个产生正向预测，9个统计显著（p<0.05），整体Spearman相关系数rho=0.1495（p=0.0004）。2024年论文相关性更强（rho=0.1880），作者识别探针区分力最强，模型规模与预测力呈非单调关系（Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct以rho=0.1829优于大多数更大模型）。

Conclusion: LLM-Metrics可作为实时、跨学科、不依赖引用的研究影响力评估新范式，其工作机制源于高影响力论文在训练数据中更频繁出现，使LLM形成更强的参数记忆。小模型的有限容量反而可作为有效的信息过滤器，支持选择性记忆假说。

Abstract: Citation counts remain the dominant metric for assessing research impact, yet they suffer from well-documented limitations: temporal lag, disciplinary bias, and Matthew effects. Here we propose LLM-Metrics, a research-impact assessment metric derived from the parametric memory of large language models (LLMs). The central hypothesis is that high-impact papers receive greater exposure in the academic community, that this exposure enters LLM training data in textual form, and that models consequently form stronger parametric memory of these papers. We designed four types of multiple-choice probes, covering title recognition, author recognition, method recognition, and venue recognition, and evaluated 549 computer science papers published in 2023-2024 across 17 LLMs spanning 0.5B to 72B parameters from six vendors. Of the 17 models, 15 produced positive predictions, 9 of which were significant at p less than 0.05, with an overall Spearman correlation of rho = 0.1495 and p = 0.0004 against citation counts. Three additional findings support the proposed mechanism. First, the predictive signal was stronger for 2024 papers, rho = 0.1880, whose citation counts were near zero at model-training time, reducing the plausibility of a simple reverse-causality explanation. Second, author-recognition probes showed the strongest discriminative power, consistent with an exposure-driven memory mechanism. Third, model scale and predictive power were non-monotonic: a 3B-parameter model, Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, with rho = 0.1829, outperformed most larger models, supporting a selective-memory hypothesis in which the limited capacity of smaller models can serve as an effective information filter. LLM-Metrics offers a real-time, cross-disciplinary, citation-independent paradigm for research assessment.

</details>


### [120] [Skill Weaving: Efficient LLM Improvement via Modular Skillpacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22205)
*Zhuo Li,Guodong Du,Zesheng Shi,Weiyang Guo,Weijun Yao,Yuan Zhou,Jiabo Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillWeave是一个模块化改进框架，通过将通用大模型的能力分解为轻量级领域特定模块（skillpacks），并在固定内存预算下实现多领域专业化部署。结合SkillZip压缩技术，在9B参数模型上超越了32B monolithic模型，并实现4倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在严格的内存和推理约束下平衡多领域能力，大语言模型需要一种能在固定预算下实现多领域专业化的方法。

Method: SkillWeave将通用模型的全能力划分为轻量级领域特定delta模块（skillpacks），重组和精炼模型内部知识；并集成SkillZip将skillpacks压缩为紧凑的推理就绪格式。

Result: 9B参数的SkillWeave模型在多任务和智能体基准测试中超越多个基线，甚至超过32B monolithic LLM，并实现高达4倍加速。

Conclusion: SkillWeave能在固定内存预算下有效实现LLM的多领域专业化，兼顾强性能和低延迟执行。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly require specialization across diverse domains, yet existing approaches struggle to balance multi-domain capacities with strict memory and inference constraints. In this work, we introduce SkillWeave, a modular improvement framework that enables LLMs to specialize under fixed memory budgets. SkillWeave partitions full capabilities of a general-purpose model into skillpacks -- lightweight, domain-specific delta modules -- that reorganize and refine the model's internal knowledge. For efficient deployment, SkillWeave integrates SkillZip to compress skillpacks into compact and inference-ready format, enabling strong multi-domain performance with low-latency execution. On multi-task and agentic benchmarks, a 9B SkillWeave model outperforms several baselines and even surpasses a 32B monolithic LLM, while achieving up to 4x speedup.

</details>


### [121] [CLORE: Content-Level Optimization for Reasoning Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22211)
*Yuyang Wu,Qiyao Xue,Guanxing Lu,Weichen Liu,Zihan Wang,Manling Li,Olexandr Isayev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLORE是一个内容级优化框架，通过对正确的强化学习轨迹进行局部编辑（删除重复、不清、无关内容及多余推理），结合无参考DPO目标和策略梯度训练，在保持答案正确性的同时提升大语言模型推理效率，并在五个数学推理基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习后训练虽提升了LLM的推理能力，但常产生冗长、重复或语义不透明的推理轨迹；已有的高效推理方法主要通过显式预算或长度奖励来约束响应长度，对中间推理内容缺乏监督。

Method: CLORE使用外部增强模型对正确的on-policy轨迹进行局部编辑（删除重复片段、不清或任务无关内容、以及答案确定后的多余推理），保留最终答案；编辑后的轨迹与原始轨迹构成对，通过辅助的无参考DPO目标与标准策略梯度训练联合优化；仅对正确轨迹进行增强且执行局部删除，使编辑后的轨迹贴近策略分布，减轻off-policy不匹配问题。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上，五个数学推理基准的实验表明CLORE改善了准确率-效率权衡，且与GRPO、DAPO、Training Efficient和ThinkPrune兼容；内容级分析显示CLORE减少了重复推理、不清内容和答案后探索。

Conclusion: CLORE证明了内容级监督作为长度级控制的互补方向是有效的。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training has improved the reasoning ability of large language models, but often produces unnecessarily long, repetitive, or semantically opaque reasoning traces. Existing efficient reasoning methods mainly regulate response length through explicit budgets or length-aware rewards, leaving intermediate reasoning content weakly supervised. We propose CLORE, a content-level optimization framework that improves reasoning efficiency by editing correct on-policy rollouts. CLORE uses an external augmentation model to delete repetitive segments, illegible or task-irrelevant content, and superfluous reasoning after the solution is established, while preserving the final answer. The resulting augmented--original pairs are optimized with an auxiliary reference-free DPO objective alongside standard policy-gradient training. By restricting augmentation to correct trajectories and performing local deletion, CLORE keeps edited rollouts close to the policy distribution and mitigates off-policy mismatch. Experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B and Qwen2.5-Math-7B across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that CLORE improves the accuracy--efficiency trade-off and remains compatible with GRPO, DAPO, Training Efficient, and ThinkPrune. Content-level analyses further show that CLORE reduces repetitive reasoning, illegible content, and post-answer exploration, supporting content-level supervision as a complementary direction to length-level control.

</details>


### [122] [Evaluating Large Language Models as Live Strategic Agents: Provider Performance, Hybrid Decomposition, and Operational Gaps in Timed Risk Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22238)
*H. C. Ekne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在定时多阶段Risk对战环境中，gemini-3.1-pro-preview在32局跨提供商锦标赛中赢得20局，显著优于其他模型；通过标准化执行层将规划与执行分离后，规划器性能差异消失，表明模型间差距主要来自端到端系统行为而非规划能力；机制分析显示Gemini更频繁关注终端目标且随胜利临近而增强，并能将更多回合转化为深度征服链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态基准无法反映大语言模型在实际系统中的真实表现，现实系统包含重复循环、时间限制、格式约束和故障模式，因此需要在更贴近真实部署环境的动态交互场景中评估LLM。

Method: 设计定时多阶段Risk环境，包含明确的胜利目标和重复规划-执行循环；进行32局跨提供商锦标赛（Gemini vs GPT vs Claude vs Kimi）；通过标准化执行层（Gemini Flash脚手架）分离规划与执行能力；分析规划与执行轨迹。

Result: Gemini赢得20/32局，胜率分布与等强度零假设显著不同（p≈1.5×10⁻⁵）；标准化执行后规划器差异不显著（p≈0.821）；Gemini更频繁引用终端目标且随胜利临近增强，能转化更多深度征服链。

Conclusion: 实时智能体性能取决于目标跟踪、执行转化、成本和运行时可靠性，LLM评估应在有界工作流中进行而非作为孤立基准测试对象。

Abstract: Static benchmarks capture only part of how large language models behave in practice. Real systems place models inside repeated loops with time limits, formatting constraints, and failure modes. We study this setting in a timed multi-phase Risk environment with explicit victory targets and repeated planning and execution cycles. In a replicated 32-game cross-provider championship under frozen rules, gemini-3.1-pro-preview won 20 of 32 games against gpt-5.1, claude-opus-4-7, and kimi-k2.6, and the pooled winner distribution differs strongly from an equal-strength null (p approx 1.5 x 10^-5). We then separate planning from execution by standardizing execution on a cheaper Gemini Flash scaffold. Under this design, a pooled 32-game planner bakeoff is consistent with near-equality (p approx 0.821), which indicates that much of the earlier provider spread came from end-to-end system behavior rather than planning alone. To study mechanism, we analyze saved planning and execution traces from the provider championship. Gemini refers to the terminal objective far more often than the other models and increases that focus as victory approaches. Gemini also converts more turns into deep conquest chains, even though it is not the cleanest runtime. These results show that live-agent performance depends on objective tracking, execution conversion, cost, and runtime reliability, and they support evaluating LLMs as components in bounded workflows rather than as isolated benchmark respondents.

</details>


### [123] [Unlocking Proactivity in Task-Oriented Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22240)
*Hongbin Zhang,Ning Gao,Yuqin Dai,Ruiyuan Wu,Jinpeng Wang,Rena Wei Gao,Bingdong Tan,Shuzheng Gao,Zongjie Li,Chaozheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于认知用户模拟器的非对称视角策略优化方法，通过建模用户的潜在关切点来增强任务导向对话系统的主动说服能力，解决了传统强化学习方法无法突破保守策略采样范围的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM在主动任务导向对话中表现保守，而基于奖励塑形的强化学习方法（如GRPO）仅能重新加权已有被动策略的采样结果，无法真正激发主动能力。作者发现，利用用户的潜在关切点作为训练信号可以突破这一限制。

Method: 提出认知用户模拟器，将每个用户建模为包含外部可观测特征和内部隐藏关切点的分层人格模型；提出模拟器诱导的非对称视角策略优化，包含两个目标：（1）非对称在线自蒸馏，将有关注点感知的行为从特权视角迁移到可部署的纯对话视角；（2）状态转移策略优化，利用模拟状态转移进行训练。

Result: 实验表明该方法能有效提升对话系统的主动说服能力，在有限轮次内更好地引导对话走向用户接受。

Conclusion: 用户潜在关切点是激发主动对话能力的关键训练时信号，基于认知用户模拟器的非对称视角优化方法为主动任务导向对话提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Proactive task-oriented dialogue (TOD), such as outbound sales, demands a persuasive agent that actively probes the user's concerns and steers the conversation toward acceptance within a bounded number of turns. Yet post-trained LLMs are inherently conservative, and reward-shaping RL (e.g., GRPO) struggles since it only re-weights what an already passive policy samples. We show that conditioning on the user's latent concerns unlocks proactive capability that no amount of sampling can undermine, establishing these concerns as a pivotal training-time signal. To operationalize this finding, we build the \textbf{Cognitive User Simulator}, which models each user as a stratified persona comprising observable external traits and hidden internal concerns. The simulator produces faithful and diverse interactions, while emitting per-turn state dynamics that track persuasion progress. We then introduce \textbf{Simulator-Induced Asymmetric-View Policy Optimization}, which converts the modeled concerns and the simulation state transition into complementary training objectives: (1) \emph{Asymmetric On-Policy Self-Distillation} that transfers concern-aware behavior from a privileged view of the same policy into its deployable, conversation-only view; and (2) \emph{State-Transition Policy Refinement} ...

</details>


### [124] [SciCore-Mol: Augmenting Large Language Models with Pluggable Molecular Cognition Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22287)
*Yuxuan Chen,Changwei Lv,Yunduo Xiao,Zhongjing Du,Daquan Zhou,Yukun Yan,Zheni Zeng,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciCore-Mol 是一个模块化框架，通过三个可插拔认知模块（拓扑感知模块、潜扩散分子生成模块、反应感知推理模块）弥合大语言模型与异构科学数据（分子、反应）之间的语义鸿沟，在分子理解、生成、反应预测和化学知识等任务上，8B参数开源系统达到甚至超越闭源大模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在处理分子等异构科学数据时，离散语言符号与拓扑分子结构或连续反应数据之间存在天然鸿沟，导致基于文本的推理出现显著信息损失和语义噪声，亟需一种能桥接该鸿沟的系统性框架。

Method: 提出SciCore-Mol模块化框架，包含三个可插拔认知模块：（1）拓扑感知感知模块；（2）基于潜扩散的分子生成模块；（3）反应感知推理模块。每个模块通过学习的表示接口与LLM主干耦合，实现比纯文本工具反馈更丰富的信息交换。

Result: 在多样化化学任务上，SciCore-Mol在分子理解、生成、反应预测和通用化学知识方面均取得强综合性能，8B参数开源系统在多个维度上与大型专有模型竞争力相当甚至超越。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM配备科学专业知识提供了系统蓝图，通过解耦、可插拔、灵活编排的模块化设计，对药物设计、化学合成及更广泛的科学发现具有直接意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are central to the one-for-all intelligent paradigm, but they face a fundamental challenge when dealing with heterogeneous scientific data such as molecules: the inherent gap between discrete linguistic symbols and topological molecular or continuous reaction data leads to significant information loss and semantic noise in text-based reasoning. We propose SciCore-Mol, a modular framework that bridges this gap through three deeply integrated pluggable cognitive modules: a topology-aware perception module, a latent diffusion-based molecular generation module, and a reaction-aware reasoning module. Each module is coupled to the LLM backbone through learned representation interfaces, enabling richer information exchange than is possible with text-only tool feedback. Our experiments on diverse chemical tasks demonstrate that SciCore-Mol achieves strong comprehensive performance across molecular understanding, generation, reaction prediction, and general chemistry knowledge, with an 8B-parameter open-source system that is competitive with and in several dimensions surpasses proprietary large models. This work provides a systematic blueprint for equipping LLMs with scientific expertise through decoupled, pluggable, and flexibly orchestrated modules, with direct implications for drug design, chemical synthesis, and broader scientific discovery.

</details>


### [125] [Cross-domain benchmarks reveal when coordinated AI agents improve scientific inference from partial evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22300)
*Fiona Y. Wong,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个跨学科科学基准，评估多智能体协调相比简单工作流的增值条件。结果表明，协调仅在跨通道数据融合时提升性能（如气候-向量疾病检测AUROC 0.944，系外行星验证AUROC 0.955），而在单一信号主导或表示性任务中，协调主要提升可解释性或表征价值，而非预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学证据常跨仪器、数据库和学科分布，单一来源无法完整记录现象，因此难以判断协调型AI智能体何时比简单科学工作流更具价值。

Method: 构建跨领域基准，涵盖四个科学任务（分子结构音乐映射、科学范式转移检测、媒介传播疾病识别、系外行星候选体验证），采用固定评估面板、预定义评分协议、显式基线、消融/空对照，并说明局限性。

Result: 定义了三种运行模式：1) 跨通道复合优于单通道基线（AUROC 0.944和0.955）；2) 系外行星任务中协调与强组合摘要基线持平；3) 单信号主导时协调主要提升可解释性和可追溯性；分子声化任务增益为表征性而非预测性。

Conclusion: 协调的价值仅在性能、溯源或表征主张有显式比较器支持时才被认定。ScienceClaw x Infinite提供了可审计的工件和溯源层。

Abstract: Scientific evidence often spans instruments, databases, and disciplines, so no single source records the full phenomenon. This makes it difficult to determine when coordinated AI agents add value over simpler scientific workflows. We evaluate this question with a cross-domain benchmark spanning four scientific tasks: mapping molecular structure into musical representations, detecting historical paradigm shifts in science, identifying vector-borne disease emergence, and vetting transiting-exoplanet candidates. Each case uses a frozen evaluation panel, predefined scoring protocols, explicit baselines, ablations or null controls, and stated limitations. The results define three operating regimes. When different disciplines each capture only part of the phenomenon, cross-channel composites improve over single-channel baselines: climate-vector emergence reaches AUROC 0.944 and exoplanet vetting reaches AUROC 0.955. However, the exoplanet workflow is effectively tied with a strong combined-summary baseline, showing that decomposition does not always improve top-line performance. When one signal dominates, as in paradigm-shift detection, coordination mainly improves interpretation and traceability. For molecular sonification, the gain is representational rather than predictive. ScienceClaw x Infinite provides the auditable artifact and provenance layer for this evaluation. The benchmark therefore assigns value to coordination only when the corresponding performance, provenance, or representation claim is supported by explicit comparators.

</details>


### [126] [Evaluation of Pipelines for Data Integration into Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22304)
*Marvin Hofer,Erhard Rahm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了KGI-Bench基准，用于评估将新数据集成到知识图谱中的不同管道的质量，通过覆盖率、正确性和一致性三个指标对12个管道进行了比较评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱集成涉及多种任务和工作流，存在众多可能的集成管道，但缺乏通用的方法来评估这些管道的整体质量和性能，以确定最佳选择。

Method: 提出了KGI-Bench基准，通过分析管道输出的更新后知识图谱，使用覆盖率、正确性和一致性三个互补质量指标进行评估，并提供了电影领域的基准数据集（种子知识图谱、三种格式的重叠输入数据、作为真实标注的参考知识图谱）。

Result: 对12个集成管道进行了比较评估，分析了它们在不同输入数据格式和设计选择下的行为表现。

Conclusion: KGI-Bench能够有效评估知识图谱集成管道的质量，帮助确定最佳管道选择。

Abstract: Integrating new data into knowledge graphs (KG) typically involves different tasks that are executed within workflows or pipelines There are many possible pipelines for a specific integration problem but there is not yet a general approach to evaluate the overall quality and performance of such pipelines to be able to determine the best choices. We therefore propose a new benchmark KGI-Bench to evaluate integration pipelines that ingest different kinds of input data into an existing KG. We evaluate pipelines by analyzing their output, i.e., the updated KG, with the three complementary quality metrics coverage, correctness and consistency. We also provide benchmark datasets (seed KG, overlapping input data of three formats, reference KG as a ground truth) for the movie domain. To demonstrate the applicability and usefulness of the proposed benchmark, we comparatively evaluate 12 pipelines and analyze their behavior across different input data formats and design choices.

</details>


### [127] [Meta-Soft: Leveraging Composable Meta-Tokens for Context-Preserving KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22337)
*Wei Luo,Yi Huang,Songchen Ma,Huanyu Qu,Jiang Cai,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Meta-Soft动态压缩框架，通过可学习正交基矩阵和Gumbel-Softmax选择器动态合成软令牌，并引入注意力流集成机制在保留令牌中重分布被移除令牌的语义信息，实现KV缓存高效压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存驱逐方法基于固定软令牌（如Judge Q），无法动态适应不同输入提示，难以精确捕捉复杂变化的任务相关性；且被驱逐的KV对被永久丢弃，造成不可逆的信息丢失和上下文断裂。

Method: 1) 构建包含可学习正交基矩阵L的元库；2) 使用Gumbel-Softmax选择器生成可微稀疏组合权重，从输入提示特征中动态合成最相关的k个软令牌；3) 将这些软令牌附加到输入序列末尾探测关键信息；4) 引入基于注意力流的集成机制，将被移除令牌的语义信息重新分布到保留令牌中。

Result: 在多个数据集上，该方法优于现有最先进的驱逐方法。

Conclusion: Meta-Soft为KV缓存压缩提供了一种新的有效解决方案，通过动态软令牌合成和上下文信息保持机制解决了现有方法的静态性和信息不可逆丢失问题。

Abstract: The KV cache used in large language models has linearly growing time complexity, so LLMs face memory blow-up and reduced decoding efficiency when they process long contexts.Current KV Cache eviction has become an important research direction; however, existing methods based on fixed Soft Tokens (e.g., Judge Q) rely on a static parameter set as the query to evaluate the importance of KV pairs, so they cannot adapt dynamically to different input prompts, and they cannot precisely capture complex and changing task relevance.Also, evicted KV pairs are discarded permanently, so this causes irreversible information loss and context breaks. To address this problem, we propose Meta-Soft, a dynamic compression framework based on probe-driven context integration. Specifically, we build a meta-library with a learnable orthogonal basis matrix $\mathcal{L}$, and we use a selector network with Gumbel-Softmax to produce differentiable sparse combination weights, so we dynamically synthesize the most targeted $k$ Soft Tokens from the input prompt features.We append these Soft Tokens to the end of the input sequence to probe key information. We also introduce an attention-flow based integration mechanism, which redistributes the semantic information of removed tokens into retained tokens, and this keeps the dropped context information effectively.Experiments on multiple datasets show that our method outperforms existing state-of-the-art eviction methods and provides a new solution for KV Cache compression.

</details>


### [128] [Epicure: Navigating the Emergent Geometry of Food Ingredient Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22391)
*Jakub Radzikowski,Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Epicure是一个基于多语言食谱语料库（11个来源，7种语言，414万条食谱）从头训练的skip-gram食材嵌入模型家族。通过LLM增强管道将原始食材字符串归一化为1,790个规范条目。构建了食材共现图（203,508边）和FlavorDB食材-化合物图（80,019边），并基于三种Metapath2Vec变体（Cooc、Chem、Core）生成不同化学-食谱上下文光谱位置的嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食材嵌入模型多限于单语言或小规模食谱数据，缺乏对多语言跨文化食谱信息的整合；同时，如何在食材嵌入中融合化学知识（化合物组成）与食谱上下文（共现关系）仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 1) 从11个来源（7种语言）收集414万条食谱；2) 通过LLM增强管道将原始食材字符串归一化为1,790个规范条目；3) 构建203,508边的食材共现NPMI图；4) 构建80,019边的FlavorDB食材-化合物图（2,247个化合物节点，15个类别）；5) 基于Metapath2Vec训练三种skip-gram变体：Cooc（仅共现图游走）、Chem（仅化合物元路径游走）、Core（混合两种游走）

Result: 生成了三个位于化学-食谱上下文光谱不同位置的食材嵌入模型，分别侧重共现语义（Cooc）、化学结构语义（Chem）和两者混合语义（Core）。

Conclusion: Epicure提供了一个多语言、化学知识增强的食材嵌入框架，通过控制共现与化学信息的混合程度，可灵活地在不同应用场景下平衡食谱上下文与化学语义。

Abstract: We present Epicure, a family of three sibling skip-gram ingredient embeddings retrained from scratch on a multilingual recipe corpus. We aggregate 4.14M recipes from 11 sources spanning seven languages, English, Chinese, Russian, Vietnamese, Spanish, Turkish, Indonesian, German, and Indian-English, and normalise the raw ingredient strings to 1,790 canonical entries via an LLM-augmented pipeline. A 203,508-edge ingredient-ingredient NPMI graph and an 80,019-edge typed FlavorDB ingredient-compound graph, 2,247 typed compound nodes across 15 categories, seed three Metapath2Vec variants that share architecture and hyperparameters and differ only in the random-walk schema: Cooc walks the co-occurrence graph only, Chem walks the typed compound metapaths only, and Core blends both via injected ingredient-ingredient walks at controlled mixing, placing each model at a distinct point on the chemistry-vs-recipe-context spectrum.

</details>


### [129] [S2ED: From Story to Executable Descriptions for Consistency-Aware Story Illustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22448)
*Sijing Yin,Jiamou Liu,Xiao Tang,Yaser Shakib,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出S2ED框架，通过可执行描述将故事转化为多帧插图，无需训练即可提升角色一致性和序列连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多帧故事插图需要长程连贯性，包括叙事分解、角色身份、布局和情感的持续一致，现有单图像文生图方法无法满足这一需求。

Method: S2ED是一个无需训练、模型无关的提示层框架，协调三个智能体分别负责叙事分割、角色属性锚定、空间与情感线索丰富，通过可编辑的可执行描述实现提示传播和局部修复。

Result: 在Flintstones和Shakoo Maku数据集上，S2ED在自动指标和人工评估下均优于强提示方法、大模型规划方法和基于参考训练的方法。

Conclusion: S2ED有效提升了多帧故事插图的序列一致性和角色保真度，并已部署到端到端的故事绘本系统中。

Abstract: Multi-frame story illustration requires long-horizon coherence beyond single-image text-to-image generation, including narrative decomposition and persistent character identity, layout, and affect across frames. We propose Story-to-Executable Descriptions (S2ED), a training-free, model-agnostic, prompt-layer framework that converts a full story into a sequence of explicit, editable executable descriptions for more consistent rendering. S2ED coordinates three agents to segment the narrative, ground canonical character attributes, and enrich spatial and affective cues, enabling interpretable prompt-carried state propagation and local edits to repair drift without retraining the generator. Experiments on Flintstones and Shakoo Maku show that S2ED improves sequence-level consistency and character fidelity over strong prompting, large-model planning, and a reference training-based method, under both automatic metrics and human judgments. We also deploy S2ED in an end-to-end story-to-storybook system for children's illustrated stories, with a supplementary video.

</details>


### [130] [KAPPS: A knowledge-based CPPS Architecture for the Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22457)
*Etienne Hoffmann,Jan-Felix Klein,Sören Weindel,Max Goebels,Sebastian Behrendt,Daniel Hernández,Ratan Bahadur Thapa,Jürgen Fleischer,Kai Furmans,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KAPPS架构，一种基于知识图谱的语义集成架构，用于解决循环制造中异构产品状态和动态重构流程带来的IT架构挑战，通过本体驱动的知识图谱作为统一数据骨干和语义接口层，实现约束执行、事件驱动规划和人机知识交换，并在异常检测和模块化输送带约束执行两个用例中验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性制造依赖均质材料和预定义流程，而循环制造需要处理具有异质性和不确定性的回收产品。现有制造IT架构设计面向稳定结构和确定性执行，无法在运行时表示和管理单个组件的独特性，难以满足循环制造对可变产品状态、动态可重构流程以及人机知识集成的需求。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法，从五个互补视角推导出14条需求，据此设计KAPPS架构。该架构以本体驱动的知识图谱作为统一数据骨干，结合语义接口层实现异构系统间的数据集成、推理和通信，将知识图谱从集成层提升为工厂的权威写入时状态。内置约束执行和事件驱动规划模块，支持执行计划的增量自适应调整和人机知识交换。

Result: 通过两个用例验证：①基于知识图谱中介服务的异常检测与学习；②模块化输送带系统中的运行时约束执行。并对14条需求进行了评估。

Conclusion: KAPPS架构有效解决了循环制造中知识图谱从集成层到写时状态的范式转变，为循环制造中信息物理生产系统提供了可行的参考架构。

Abstract: While linear manufacturing relies on homogeneous materials and predefined process sequences, circular manufacturing reintroduces used products with heterogeneous and uncertain conditions. This shift demands manufacturing systems capable of handling variable product states, dynamically reconfigurable processes, and the integration of human and machine knowledge. Conventional manufacturing IT architectures, designed for stable structures and deterministic execution, are unable to meet these requirements, as they cannot adequately represent and manage the uniqueness of individual components at runtime. Following a design science methodology for developing a Cyber Physical Production System for circular manufacturing, we derive 14 requirements from five complementary perspectives. Based on these requirements, we design KAPPS, a knowledge-based architecture that uses an ontology-grounded knowledge graph as a unifying data backbone, combined with a semantic interface layer to enable consistent data and information integration, reasoning, and communication across heterogeneous systems and services, turning the knowledge graph from an integration layer into the factories authoritative write-time state. KAPPS incorporates modules for constraint enforcement and event-driven planning, enabling incremental adaptation of execution plans under uncertainty and human-machine knowledge exchange. The applicability of KAPPS is demonstrated through two implemented use cases: (i) Anomaly detection and learning through knowledge graph mediated services and (ii) runtime constraint enforcement in a modular conveyor system. Subsequently, the architecture is evaluated against the 14 requirements (ed. abstract shortened)

</details>


### [131] [Compiling Agentic Workflows into LLM Weights: Near-Frontier Quality at Two Orders of Magnitude Less Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22502)
*Simon Dennis,Rivaan Patil,Kevin Shabahang,Hao Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前Agent编排框架（如LangGraph、CrewAI等）普遍采用外部协调器+LLM的模式，但该模式存在上下文窗口消耗、依赖前沿模型、暴露专有流程等缺陷。作者提出将流程编译进小型微调模型权重中（"地下Agent"）的方案，并针对三个感知障碍进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent编排架构（外部协调器在上、LLM在下）存在三大问题：消耗上下文窗口、每个对话需前沿模型、向第三方暴露专有流程。需要一种更高效、更安全的替代方案。

Method: 提出"地下Agent"（subterranean agent）方法：将流程编译进小型微调模型的权重中，而非在运行时通过系统提示注入指令。在旅行预订（14节点）、Zoom支持（14节点+产品知识）、保险理赔（55节点+6个决策枢纽）三个任务上进行实证评估。

Result: 实证结果验证了该方法的有效性，克服了开发者感知的三个障碍，表明小型微调模型可替代外部编排+前沿LLM的组合。

Conclusion: 尽管开发者更倾向编排框架，但将流程编译进模型权重的方法在效率、成本和隐私方面具有显著优势，应得到更多采用。

Abstract: Agent orchestration frameworks have proliferated, collectively exceeding 290,000 GitHub stars across LangGraph, CrewAI, Google ADK, OpenAI Agents SDK, Semantic Kernel, Strands, and LlamaIndex. All follow the same pattern: an external orchestrator above the LLM, injecting instructions and routing decisions every turn. Recent work has shown this architecture is dominated for procedural tasks by simply providing the procedure in a frontier model's system prompt [Dennis et al., 2026a], at the cost of consuming the context window, requiring a frontier model for every conversation, and exposing proprietary procedures to third-party providers. Compiling the procedure into the weights of a small fine-tuned model -- creating a subterranean agent -- should resolve all of these concerns, and prior work (SimpleTOD, FireAct, SynTOD, WorkflowLLM, Agent Lumos) has shown the technique works. Yet developer adoption has overwhelmingly favored orchestration. We identify three perceived barriers and address each empirically across travel booking (14 nodes), Zoom support (14 nodes, product-specific knowledge), and insurance claims (55 nodes, 6 decision hubs).

</details>


### [132] [LACO: Adaptive Latent Communication for Collaborative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22504)
*Tianhao Chen,Yuheng Wu,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LACO，一种无需训练的潜在通信范式，通过迭代潜在推理、跨视野显著性归因和结构化语义知识蒸馏，解决协同驾驶中语言通信的高延迟和信息丢失问题，在CARLA实验中显著降低通信和推理延迟，同时保持强协同驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同驾驶方法从共享视觉特征演进到基于语言推理的通信，但语言通信存在两大挑战：自回归解码导致的高延迟，以及将丰富内部表示压缩为离散token造成的信息损失。本文旨在解决这些问题，探索多智能体潜在通信的可行方案。

Method: 提出LACO范式，包含三个核心模块：1) Iterative Latent Deliberation (ILD) 用于潜在空间推理；2) Cross-Horizon Saliency Attribution (CHSA) 用于通信高效的信息选择；3) Structured Semantic Knowledge Distillation (SSKD) 用于稳定自我中心的决策。

Result: 在CARLA闭环实验中，LACO显著降低了通信和推理延迟，同时保持了强大的协同驾驶性能。

Conclusion: LACO作为一种训练免费的潜在通信范式，能够无缝将预训练驾驶模型适配到协同驾驶场景，有效解决了身份混淆问题，兼顾了低延迟和高效信息传递。

Abstract: Collaborative driving aims to improve safety and efficiency by enabling connected vehicles to coordinate under partial observability. Recent approaches have evolved from sharing visual features for perception to exchanging language-based reasoning through foundation models for behavioral coordination. Though communicating in language provides intuitive information, it introduces two challenges: high latency caused by autoregressive decoding and information loss caused by compressing rich internal representations into discrete tokens. To address these challenges, we analyze latent communication in collaborative driving under inherent limitations of multi-agent settings. Our analysis reveals agent identity confusion, where direct fusion of latent states entangles decision representations across vehicles. Motivated by this, we propose LACO, a training-free \textbf{LA}tent \textbf{CO}mmunication paradigm that seamlessly adapts pretrained driving models to collaborative settings. LACO introduces Iterative Latent Deliberation (ILD) for latent reasoning, Cross-Horizon Saliency Attribution (CHSA) for communication-efficient information selection, and Structured Semantic Knowledge Distillation (SSKD) to stabilize ego-centric decision making. Closed-loop experiments in CARLA show that LACO notably reduces communication and inference latency while maintaining strong collaborative driving performance.

</details>


### [133] [Towards Direct Evaluation of Harness Optimizers via Priority Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22505)
*Kai Tzu-iunn Ong,Minseok Kang,Dongwook Choi,Junhee Cho,Seungju Kim,Seungwon Lim,Geunha Jang,Minwoo Oh,Bogyung Jeong,Sunghwan Kim,Taeyoon Kwon,Jinyoung Yeo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出优先级排序（priority ranking）方法，通过让优化器对harness组件进行重要性排序来直接评估其能力，无需昂贵的部署或人工检查，并构建了包含182个人工验证场景的Shor基准。实验表明排序性能与实际优化能力高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅通过目标智能体的性能提升间接评估harness优化器，忽略了优化器在中间步骤的错误行为，无法区分优化是来自明智的更新还是简单的试错。需要一种直接、低成本的评估方法。

Method: 提出优先级排序（priority ranking）方法：让优化器对harness中各组件的改进潜力进行排序，量化其在单步层面的决策能力。同时构建Shor基准，包含182个人工验证的优化场景，覆盖多领域、多设计、多时间阶段。

Result: 优化器的排序性能与其在多步harness优化中提升智能体的实际能力之间存在显著相关性，验证了优先级排序作为优化能力可靠预测指标的有效性。

Conclusion: 优先级排序是一种简单、低成本的直接评估harness优化器的方法，能够可靠预测优化器的实际优化能力，弥补了现有间接评估范式的不足。

Abstract: Harness optimization enables automated agent creation by having an optimizer agent iteratively update the harness of target agents. Despite its success, current studies evaluate optimizers solely by observing target agents' performance gains. This indirect end-improvement evaluation neglects optimizers' actions at intermediate steps, which are often erroneous and hinder agent performance. Therefore, it is unclear whether harness optimization is driven by optimizers' informed update actions or simply trial-and-error. This necessitates direct evaluation of harness optimizers. However, evaluating harness optimizers directly is non-trivial and costly due to the lack of oracle harnesses. To address this, we present a simple, low-cost design to directly evaluate them, namely priority ranking. By asking harness optimizers to rank components (e.g., tools) in a given harness by their potential to improve/hinder agent performance when updated, our design quantifies optimizer ability at the step level without expensive rollouts or manual examination. More importantly, optimizers' ranking performance correlates with their ability to improve agents in actual multi-step harness optimization, establishing priority ranking as a reliable predictor of optimization ability. Priority ranking is enabled by Shor, a collection of 182 human-verified optimization scenarios spanning across domains, designs, and time stages. Codes and data can be found at https://github.com/k59118/Harness_Optimizer_Evaluation.

</details>


### [134] [A Subjective Logic-based method for runtime confidence updates in safety arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22530)
*Benjamin Herd,Jessica Kelly,Clarissa Heinemann,João-Vitor Zacchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态定量保障方法，通过结合设计时证据和运行时安全性能指标（SPI），在主观逻辑框架下实现安全案例的连续置信度更新，并在基于ML的施工区域辅助功能仿真中验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的静态安全案例无法在运行时动态响应系统行为变化，缺乏对置信度的持续量化评估能力，尤其是在ML组件部署后性能可能漂移的场景下，需要一种能够结合运行时证据动态更新保障置信度的方法。

Method: 方法基于主观逻辑（SL）构建统一保障案例，融合设计时证据与滑动窗口内的运行时SPI证据；设计了一种非贝叶斯的置信度更新规则，在无违规时提升置信度，发生违规时立即施加惩罚，优先保证安全响应速度。

Result: 通过施工区域辅助功能的仿真实验（聚焦于ML-based施工锥检测组件），展示了置信度在运行时随着SPI证据的观测而动态演化的过程，验证了该方法能够有效反映安全性能的变化。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了从静态安全案例到动态、运行时驱动的置信度更新的转变，通过权衡响应速度与精确性，为包含ML组件的安全关键系统提供了一种实用的动态定量保障手段。

Abstract: We present a method for dynamic quantitative assurance that enhances static safety cases with continuous, runtime-driven confidence updates. The method quantifies and propagates confidence across the development lifecycle by integrating design-time evidence and windowed runtime Safety Performance Indicators (SPIs) within a single Subjective Logic (SL)-based assurance case. At runtime, SPI evidence is continuously evaluated, and targeted claims are updated using a rule that increases confidence in the absence of violations and imposes prompt penalties when violations occur. This design prioritizes safety-relevant responsiveness over exact classical Bayesian posterior updates. We demonstrate the method using a simulation-based construction zone assist function, focusing on an ML-based construction cone detection component, and show how confidence evolves as SPI evidence is observed in operation.

</details>


### [135] [TerminalWorld: Benchmarking Agents on Real-World Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22535)
*Zhaoyang Chu,Jiarui Hu,Xingyu Jiang,Pengyu Zou,Han Li,Chao Peng,Peter O'Hearn,Earl T. Barr,Mark Harman,Federica Sarro,He Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TerminalWorld是一个从真实终端录制中自动逆向工程生成评测任务的数据引擎，基于80,870条终端记录构建了包含1,530个验证任务的基准测试，涵盖18个真实场景类别和1,280种独特命令。对前沿模型和智能体的评测显示，当前系统在真实终端工作流中最高通过率仅62.5%，且与现有基准的相关性很弱（r=0.20）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端任务评测基准多由专家人工构建，难以真实反映开发者实际使用终端的复杂性和多样性，缺乏自动化的、可扩展的高保真评测方法。

Method: 提出TerminalWorld数据引擎，从80,870条"野外"终端录制中自动逆向工程生成评测任务，经自动验证和人工审核，构建包含1,530个验证任务（及200个精选任务子集）的基准测试。

Result: 在TerminalWorld-Verified上对8个前沿模型和6个智能体进行评测，最高通过率仅62.5%。与现有专家构建基准（Terminal-Bench）的得分相关性较弱（Pearson r=0.20），表明捕获了不同的真实终端能力。

Conclusion: TerminalWorld通过自动化数据引擎实现了真实性与可扩展性，能够随着开发者实践演进持续评测智能体在真实终端环境中的能力，且补充了现有专家构建基准的评估维度。

Abstract: We introduce TerminalWorld, a scalable data engine that automatically reverse-engineers high-fidelity evaluation tasks from "in-the-wild" terminal recordings. Processing 80,870 terminal recordings, the engine yields a full benchmark of 1,530 validated tasks, spanning 18 real-world categories, ranging from short everyday operations to workflows exceeding 50 steps, and covering 1,280 unique commands. From these, we curate a Verified subset of 200 representative, manually reviewed tasks. Comprehensive benchmarking on TerminalWorld-Verified across eight frontier models and six agents reveals that current systems still struggle with authentic terminal workflows, achieving a maximum pass rate of only 62.5%. Moreover, TerminalWorld captures real-world terminal capabilities distinct from existing expert-curated benchmarks (e.g., Terminal-Bench), with only a weak correlation to their scores (Pearson r=0.20). The automated engine makes TerminalWorld authentic and scalable by construction, enabling it to evaluate agents in real-world terminal environments as developer practices evolve. Data and code are available at https://github.com/EuniAI/TerminalWorld.

</details>


### [136] [Think Thrice Before You Speak: Dual knowledge-enhanced Theory-of-Mind Reasoning for Persuasive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22602)
*Minghui Ma,Bin Guo,Runze Yang,Mengqi Chen,Yan Liu,Jingqi Liu,Yahan Pei,Xuehao Ma,Qiuyun Zhang,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于心理理论的说服对话任务ToM-PD，构建大规模标注数据集ToM-BPD，并设计知识增强的逐步推理框架TTBYS，在信念、欲望和说服策略预测上分别超越GPT-5达1.20%、22.80%和16.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMs在说服对话中由于依赖简单的提示策略且缺乏ToM知识，无法捕捉心理状态间的内在依赖关系，导致表征碎片化和推理不稳定。

Method: 提出ToM-PD任务，基于BDI框架显式建模多轮对话中心理状态的序列依赖；构建ToM-BPD数据集捕获细粒度心理状态和说服策略；设计TTBYS框架，利用显式和隐式先验经验分步推理。

Result: Qwen3-8B+TTBYS在欲望、信念和说服策略预测上分别超出GPT-5 1.20%、22.80%和16.97%。

Conclusion: 所提方法增强了推理的可解释性和一致性，验证了显式建模心理状态依赖关系对说服对话的有效性。

Abstract: Persuasive dialogue requires reasoning about others' latent mental states, a capability known as Theory of Mind (ToM). However, due to reliance on simple prompting strategies and insufficient ToM knowledge, existing LLMs often fail to capture the intrinsic dependencies among mental states, leading to fragmented representations and unstable reasoning. To address these challenges, we introduce the ToM-based Persuasive Dialogue (ToM-PD) task, grounded in the Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) framework, which explicitly models the sequential dependencies among mental states in multi-turn dialogues. To facilitate research on this task, we construct a large-scale annotated dataset, ToM-based Broad Persuasive Dialogues (ToM-BPD), capturing fine-grained mental states and corresponding persuasive strategies. We further propose Think Thrice Before You Speak (TTBYS), a knowledge-enhanced stepwise reasoning framework that leverages both explicit and implicit prior experiences to improve LLMs' inference of desires, beliefs, and persuasive strategies. Experimental results demonstrate that Qwen3-8B equipped with TTBYS outperforms GPT-5 by 1.20%, 22.80%, and 16.97% in predicting desires, beliefs, and persuasive strategies, respectively. Case studies further show that our approach enhances interpretability and consistency in reasoning.

</details>


### [137] [Spreadsheet-RL: Advancing Large Language Model Agents on Realistic Spreadsheet Tasks via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22642)
*Banghao Chi,Yining Xie,Mingyuan Wu,Jingcheng Yang,Jize Jiang,Zhaoheng Li,Shengyi Qian,Minjia Zhang,Klara Nahrstedt,Rui Hou,Xiangjun Fan,Hanchao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Spreadsheet-RL，一个基于强化学习微调的框架，用于训练专业电子表格智能体。该框架包含自动化数据收集流程、领域特定评估基准Domain-Spreadsheet，以及支持多轮强化学习的Spreadsheet Gym环境。实验表明，该方法在SpreadsheetBench和Domain-Spreadsheet上分别将Pass@1从12.0%提升至23.4%和从8.4%提升至17.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格智能体依赖通用大语言模型的专门提示工程，虽能处理简单操作，但难以应对真实世界中复杂的多步工作流。因此需要一种专门针对电子表格任务的强化学习微调框架来提升智能体的自动化能力。

Method: 1) 自动化管道：从在线论坛大规模收集配对的起始-目标电子表格数据；2) Domain-Spreadsheet基准：包含金融、供应链管理等领域的评估任务；3) Spreadsheet Gym：基于Python沙箱暴露丰富的Excel功能，并配备综合工具集和精心设计的工具路由规则，支持多轮强化学习训练。

Result: 在SpreadsheetBench上，Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507的Pass@1从12.0%提升至23.4%；在Domain-Spreadsheet数据集上，Pass@1从8.4%提升至17.2%。

Conclusion: Spreadsheet-RL显著提升了AI智能体在通用和领域特定电子表格任务上的性能，展现出良好的泛化能力和实际应用潜力，为推进基于LLM的数据界面交互奠定了基础。

Abstract: Spreadsheet systems (e.g., Microsoft Excel, Google Sheets) play a central role in modern data-centric workflows. As AI agents grow increasingly capable of automating complex tasks, such as controlling computers and generating presentations, building an AI-driven spreadsheet agent has emerged as a promising research direction. Most existing spreadsheet agents rely on specialized prompting over general-purpose LLMs; while this design has potentials on simple spreadsheet operations, it struggles to manage the complex, multi-step workflows typical of real-world applications.
  We introduce Spreadsheet-RL, a reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning framework designed to train specialized spreadsheet agents within a realistic Microsoft Excel environment. Spreadsheet-RL features an automated pipeline for scalable collection of paired start-goal spreadsheets from online forums, as well as domain-specific evaluation tasks in areas such as finance and supply chain management, which we compile into the new Domain-Spreadsheet benchmark dataset. It also includes a Spreadsheet Gym environment designed for multi-turn RL: Spreadsheet Gym exposes extensive Excel functionality through a Python sandbox, along with a refined harness that incorporates a comprehensive tool set and carefully designed tool-routing rules for spreadsheet tasks. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that Spreadsheet-RL substantially enhances AI agent's performance on both general and domain-specific spreadsheet tasks: it improves Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507's Pass@1 on SpreadsheetBench from 12.0% to 23.4%, and raises Pass@1 from 8.4% to 17.2% on our curated Domain-Spreadsheet dataset. These results highlight Spreadsheet-RL's strong potential for generalization and real-world adoption in spreadsheet automation, and broadly, its promise for advancing LLM-based interactions with data interfaces in everyday work.

</details>


### [138] [AtelierEval: Agentic Evaluation of Humans & LLMs as Text-to-Image Prompters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22645)
*Hanjun Luo,Zhimu Huang,Sylvia Chung,Yiran Wang,Yingbin Jin,Jialin Li,Jiang Li,Xinfeng Li,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AtelierEval是首个统一评估文本到图像系统中提示器（人类或MLLM）提示能力的基准，包含360个专家任务；AtelierJudge是其配套的基于技能、记忆增强的智能评估器，Spearman相关系数达0.79；实验表明模仿优于规划，提示器应朝图像增强方向发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅固定提示词评估T2I模型，忽略了上游提示器（人类或MLLM）将用户意图转化为详细提示的提示能力，缺乏对这一关键组件的量化评估。

Method: 基于认知视角构建包含三个任务类别的360个专家级任务基准AtelierEval，并提出AtelierJudge——一种技能驱动的、记忆增强的智能评估器，用于对提示-图像对进行主观和客观评分。

Result: AtelierJudge与人类专家的Spearman相关系数达0.79，接近人类水平；实验对8个MLLM和48个人类用户在4种T2I后端上进行基准测试，揭示了模仿优于规划的现象。

Conclusion: AtelierEval可作为稳健的诊断工具，验证了提示器能力评估的必要性，并建议未来提示器应朝图像增强方向发展。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) systems increasingly rely on upstream prompters, either humans or multimodal large language models (MLLMs), to translate user intent into detailed prompts. Yet current benchmarks fix the prompt and only evaluate T2I models, leaving the prompting proficiency of this upstream component entirely unmeasured. We introduce AtelierEval, the first unified benchmark that quantifies prompting proficiency across 360 expert-crafted tasks. Grounded in a cognitive view, it spans three task categories and instantiates tasks using a taxonomy of real-world challenges, with a dual interface for both humans and MLLMs. To enable scalable and reliable evaluation, we propose AtelierJudge, a skill-based, memory-augmented agentic evaluator. It produces subjective and objective scores for prompt-image pairs, achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.79 with human experts, approaching human performance. Extensive experiments benchmark 8 MLLMs against 48 human users across 4 T2I backends, validate AtelierEval as a robust diagnostic tool, and reveal the superiority of mimicry over planning, advocating for an image-augmented direction for future prompters. Our work is released to support future research.

</details>


### [139] [WorkstreamBench: Evaluating LLM Agents on End-to-End Spreadsheet Tasks in Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22664)
*Thomson Yen,Julian Poeltl,Harshith Srinivas Gear,Yilin Meng,Joshua Fan,Adam Shen,Yili Liu,Ali Bauyrzhan,Siri Du,Haoyang Liu,Daniel Guetta,Hongseok Namkoong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了LLM智能体在端到端电子表格任务上的表现，聚焦金融建模与情景分析。现有基准仅覆盖问答或单公式编辑，而本文构建了包含准确性、公式、格式三个维度的评估体系。Claude系列表现领先，但即使是顶尖智能体在复杂任务上也远未达到专业金融标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前端AI实验室已开发出能从头构建完整电子表格的智能体，但现有基准无法评估这一高级能力。金融领域核心工作流（如财务建模、预测、情景分析）常通过电子表格完成，亟需对其端到端生成质量进行系统评估。

Method: 提出包含三个维度的评估分类体系：准确性（Accuracy）、公式（Formula）和格式（Format），每个维度包含细粒度专业标准。以金融建模和情景分析为测试任务，对多个LLM智能体进行定性定量评估。

Result: Claude系列在基准测试中领先，输出在定性评估中最为专业。但随着任务难度增加（超过少数链式计算），所有智能体的表现急剧下降，远未达到专业金融标准。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体尚不能可靠地生成达到现实工作流复杂度要求的专业质量电子表格，在端到端金融任务上仍有显著差距。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly expected to carry out end-to-end workflows, producing complete artifacts from high-level user instructions. To meet enterprise needs, frontier AI labs have developed agents that can construct entire spreadsheets from scratch. This is especially relevant in finance, where core workflows such as financial modeling, forecasting, and scenario analysis are commonly conducted through spreadsheets. Yet, existing spreadsheet benchmarks do not measure this advanced capability, focusing instead on question-answering or single-formula edits. To address this gap, we provide one of the first evaluations of agents on end-to-end spreadsheet tasks, focusing on economically critical financial workflows such as modeling and scenario analysis. Since deliverables therein are routinely reviewed and revised by multiple stakeholders, judging their quality necessarily involves high-level criteria such as readability or ease of modification. To reflect the multidimensional nature of solution quality, we develop an evaluation taxonomy comprising three dimensions: Accuracy, Formula, and Format, each comprising fine-grained criteria that reflect professional standards. The Claude family leads the benchmark and produces the most professional-looking outputs in our qualitative review, but even the strongest agents frequently fall short of professional finance standards and degrade sharply as the difficulty increases beyond a few chained calculations. This suggests that current agents are not yet able to reliably produce professional-quality spreadsheets at the level of complexity real-world workflows demand.

</details>


### [140] [Is Capability a Liability? More Capable Language Models Make Worse Forecasts When It Matters Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22672)
*Nick Merrill,Jaeho Lee,Ezra Karger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在预测超线性增长和存在体制转换尾部风险的时间序列时出现逆扩展现象：能力更强的模型产生更差的分位数预测。该现象出现在FBSim基准、合成SIR疫情预测及其线性对照、以及COVID-19、麻疹、房地产市场、恶性通胀等真实数据中。失败集中在分布上尾，更强模型过度外推增长趋势。Llama-3.1家族研究表明模型规模和训练后阶段均独立促成该效应。单阈值指标无法捕捉该问题，而尾部包容性指标会反转能力-准确率关系符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测评测主要依赖单阈值指标，忽略了分布预测尾部质量的评估，尤其是当时间序列具有超线性增长和体制转换风险时，模型能力与预测质量之间可能出现反常关系。

Method: 构建无污染的模拟世界基准FBSim，包含合成SIR流行病预测和匹配线性对照任务；在COVID-19、麻疹、房地产市场、恶性通胀等真实数据集上验证；使用分位数分解分析上尾和下尾表现差异；在Llama-3.1家族中进行模型规模和训练后阶段的效果分离研究。

Result: 能力更强的模型在超线性增长和尾部风险任务上产生更差的分位数预测；失败集中于上尾（过度外推增长），下尾保持不变；模型规模和训练后阶段独立贡献逆扩展；领域知识不能可靠改善校准；单阈值指标完全掩盖此问题，而尾部包容性指标会反转能力-准确率关系符号。

Conclusion: LLM预测评测应采用连续且无界的准确率指标，辅以有界二元阈值指标，以全面评估模型在分布尾部尤其是上尾的预测质量。

Abstract: We document inverse scaling in LLMs on forecasting problems whose underlying time series exhibit superlinear growth and tail risk of regime change, a structure common in finance and epidemiology. On these tasks, more capable models produce worse distributional forecasts. The pattern appears on ForecastBench-Sim (FBSim), a contamination-free, simulated-world benchmark we release, in forecasting synthetic SIR epidemics with a matched linear control, and replicates in real-world datasets on COVID-19, measles, housing markets, and hyperinflation. A per-quantile decomposition shows the failure concentrates at the upper tail, which more capable models shift upward to track aggressive extrapolations of growth, while the lower tail stays put. A within-family study of Llama-3.1 shows that both model scale and post-training independently contribute to this effect. Domain knowledge does not reliably rescue calibration. This inverse scaling does not appear on single-threshold metrics common in LLM forecasting benchmarks, reversing the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on identical outputs. Single-threshold scoring at conventional cutoffs misses the upper-tail cost; tail-inclusive scoring reverses the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on the same outputs. We recommend that LLM forecasting evaluations use continuous (and unbounded) measures of accuracy alongside bounded binary threshold metrics.

</details>


### [141] [Forecasting Scientific Progress with Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22681)
*Sean Wu,Pan Lu,Yupeng Chen,Jonathan Bragg,Yutaro Yamada,Peter Clark,David Clifton,Philip Torr,James Zou,Junchi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构建CUSP基准评估框架，系统研究了AI系统在科学进展预测方面的能力。在4,760个科学事件上的测试表明，当前AI模型在可行性评估、机制推理、方案生成和时间预测等方面存在系统性局限，无法可靠地预测科学进展及其时间表，且表现出过度自信和响应偏差。性能在不同科学领域差异显著（AI领域进展时间预测优于生物、化学、物理），而知识暴露（训练截止日期前后）并非主要限制因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI系统是否能够预见科学进展，即在受控知识约束条件下评估AI对科学进步的预测能力，填补该领域系统性评估框架的空白。

Method: 提出CUSP（Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress）基准框架，包含多学科、事件级别的评估体系，从四个维度（可行性评估、机制推理、生成式解决方案设计、时间预测）对前沿AI模型进行测试，并引入时间锚定评估（训练截止日期前后）和受控信息访问实验。

Result: 1) 模型能从竞争候选中识别合理研究方向，但无法可靠预测科学进展是否实现及何时发生；2) 性能高度异质：AI领域时间预测优于生物、化学、物理；3) 训练截止日期前后表现无显著差异，说明局限非仅因训练数据知识暴露；4) 额外知识提升性能但无法弥补与完全信息条件的差距（高引用进展更明显）；5) 模型存在系统性过度自信和响应偏差。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统尚不能作为科学进展的可靠预测工具。先验知识无法转化为可靠的预测能力，模型从事后信息中获益多于前瞻性预测，表明其科学预测能力存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in scientific discovery, yet whether it can anticipate scientific progress remains unclear. To study this question, we introduce a temporally grounded evaluation framework for forecasting scientific progress under controlled knowledge constraints. We present CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), a multi-disciplinary and event-level benchmark that evaluates scientific forecasting in AI systems through feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. Across 4,760 scientific events, we observe systematic and domain-dependent limitations in current frontier models. While models can identify plausible research directions from competing candidates, they fail to reliably predict whether scientific advances will be realized and systematically misestimate when they will occur. Performance is highly heterogeneous across domains, with the timing of AI progress more predictable than advances in biology, chemistry, and physics. Performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after the training cutoff, suggesting these limitations cannot be explained solely by knowledge exposure in training data. Under controlled information access, additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance but does not close the gap to full-information settings, which becomes more pronounced for high-citation advances. Models also exhibit systematic overconfidence and strong response biases, indicating unreliable uncertainty estimation. Taken together, current AI systems fall short as predictive tools for scientific progress. Access to prior knowledge does not translate into reliable forecasting, and performance benefits more from post-event information than from forward-looking prediction.

</details>


### [142] [AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22714)
*Sid-ali Temkit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了对话历史中已有的正面/负面评价极性会显著影响LLM后续判断（AMEL效应），即使面对完全相同的测试项。效应在模型不确定性高时更强，存在负面不对称性（负面历史偏差影响是正面的1.62倍），但偏差不随上下文长度增长。最简单的修复方法是为每个项目使用新上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用作自动评估器（代码审查、内容审核、评分等），通常在同一对话流中评估多个项目。研究者关注一个关键问题：对话历史中已有的评价极性是否会污染后续判断，导致评估结果偏离公正性。

Method: 在75,898次API调用中，使用11个模型（来自OpenAI、Anthropic、Google及四个开源模型），将相同的测试项分别以隔离形式和跟在正面或负面饱和历史后呈现。通过对比基线判断与历史影响后的判断差异，量化AMEL效应。进一步通过三个追踪实验（token概率分布分析、负性不对称机制分解、位置效应测试）探究机理。

Result: 模型判断显著偏向对话历史的主导极性（d = -0.17, p < 10^-46）。效应集中出现在模型基线不确定性高的项目上（高熵项d = -0.34 vs 确定性项d = -0.15）。偏差不随上下文长度增加（5轮与50轮历史效果相同，Spearman |r| < 0.01）。存在负性不对称：负面历史造成的偏差是正面的1.62倍。模型规模扩大可缓解但无法消除偏差（如Opus -0.17 vs Haiku -0.22）。偏差是连续累加而非阈值触发，位置无关。

Conclusion: 对话历史中的评价极性会通过连续累加机制偏倚LLM的判断，负面偏倚更强。为保障评估公正性，最佳实践是每个项目单独使用新上下文；若必须批处理，则应平衡历史中的正面/负面评价。

Abstract: Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations.
  Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17).
  Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.

</details>


### [143] [Parametric Modular Answer Set Programs Made Declarative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22716)
*Jorge Fandinno,Yuliya Lierler,Torsten Schaub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了一阶回答集编程（ASP）中的模块化概念，提出了参数化模块逻辑程序的形式化方法，支持带参数和内涵性声明的子程序定义，并展示了如何捕获带集体控制的clingo程序的语义，为模块化ASP提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ASP缺乏模块化支持，难以对复杂程序进行结构化组织和子程序复用。本文旨在通过引入参数化模块逻辑程序，解决ASP中模块化编程的形式化基础问题。

Method: 提出参数化模块逻辑程序（Parametric Modular Logic Programs）形式化框架，支持子程序带有参数和内涵性声明（intensionality statements）；通过语义映射，证明该框架可以捕获clingo-programs中集体控制（collective control）特性的语义；建立与传统的非模块化ASP之间的理论联系。

Result: 成功定义了参数化模块逻辑程序的形式语义；证明了该框架能表达clingo中集体控制功能的语义；建立了模块化ASP与传统ASP之间的理论桥梁。

Conclusion: 本文为ASP引入模块化编程提供了坚实的理论基础，参数化模块逻辑程序不仅支持结构化子程序定义，还能统一现有clingo的扩展特性，拓展了ASP在实际应用中的可表达性和可维护性。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP). We introduce a new formalism called parametric modular logic programs, which allows defining subprograms with parameters and intensionality statements. We demonstrate how this formalism can capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, a feature that enables structuring and instantiating subprograms. We provide theoretical foundations for modular ASP, illustrate its usefulness, and connect to traditional non-modular ASP.

</details>


### [144] [Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22763)
*George Tsoukalas,Anton Kovsharov,Sergey Shirobokov,Anja Surina,Moritz Firsching,Gergely Bérczi,Francisco J. R. Ruiz,Arun Suggala,Adam Zsolt Wagner,Eric Wieser,Lei Yu,Aja Huang,Miklós Z. Horváth,Andrew Ferrauiolo,Henryk Michalewski,Codrut Grosu,Thomas Hubert,Matej Balog,Pushmeet Kohli,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模评估了利用大语言模型结合Lean形式化验证工具解决开放数学问题的能力。最先进的智能体以每个问题几百美元的成本，自主解决了353个开放Erdős问题中的9个，证明了44/492个OEIS猜想，并已在组合学、优化、图论、代数几何和量子光学研究中部署应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学推理方面表现日益出色，但其不可靠性限制了在数学研究中的应用。为了提升LLM在数学研究中的实用价值，研究者探索利用LLM生成形式化证明（如Lean语言）来弥补其推理不可靠的问题。

Method: 采用智能体架构，交替进行基于LLM的定理生成和基于Lean的形式化验证；并对比了基础版智能体（简单交替生成与验证）与更先进的智能体在成本效益上的差异。

Result: 最先进的智能体自主解决了353个开放Erdős问题中的9个（每个问题成本约几百美元），证明了44/492个OEIS猜想，并在多个数学研究领域成功部署。基础智能体虽能复现Erdős问题的成功，但在最难问题上成本更高。

Conclusion: AI辅助的形式化证明搜索具有强大潜力，能够有效解决开放数学问题，同时揭示了实现这一能力的智能体设计特征。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly excel at mathematical reasoning, but their unreliability limits their utility in mathematics research. A mitigation is using LLMs to generate formal proofs in languages like Lean. We perform the first large-scale evaluation of this method's ability to solve open problems. Our most capable agent autonomously resolved 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at the per-problem cost of a few hundred dollars, proved 44/492 OEIS conjectures, and is being deployed in combinatorics, optimization, graph theory, algebraic geometry, and quantum optics research. A basic agent alternating LLM-based generation with Lean-based verification replicated the Erdős successes but proved costlier on the hardest problems. These findings demonstrate the power of AI-aided formal proof search and shed light on the agent designs that enable it.

</details>


### [145] [LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22786)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Momin Abbas,Prasanna Sattigeri,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LCGuard提出了一个用于多智能体LLM系统中KV缓存潜通信的安全框架，通过表示级转换防止敏感信息泄漏，同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统通过KV缓存进行潜在通信虽然效率更高且保留更丰富的任务相关信息，但KV缓存编码了上下文输入、中间推理状态和智能体特定信息，形成了一个不透明通道，敏感内容可能通过该通道在智能体间传播而无明确文本披露，存在隐私泄漏风险。

Method: LCGuard将共享KV缓存视为潜在工作记忆，在缓存工件跨智能体传输前学习表示级转换。通过对抗训练框架：对抗解码器学习重建敏感输入，LCGuard学习保留任务相关语义同时减少可重建信息的转换。

Result: 在多个模型家族和多智能体基准上的实验表明，LCGuard在保持与标准KV共享基线相当的任务性能的同时，持续降低了基于重建的泄漏和攻击成功率。

Conclusion: LCGuard有效解决了多智能体LLM系统中KV缓存潜通信的安全隐私问题，实现了安全性与任务性能之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.

</details>


### [146] [Gated DeltaNet-2: Decoupling Erase and Write in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22791)
*Ali Hatamizadeh,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Gated DeltaNet-2 通过引入通道级擦除门和写入门，解耦了Delta规则记忆编辑中的擦除与写入操作，在语言建模、推理和长上下文检索任务上优于Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet、KDA和Mamba-3等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力模型（如Gated DeltaNet和KDA）在记忆编辑时使用单一标量门控同时控制擦除旧内容和写入新内容，导致两者耦合，限制了模型对压缩记忆的精细控制能力。

Method: 提出Gated DeltaNet-2，将擦除门b_t和写入门w_t解耦为独立的通道级门控；设计了融合通道级衰减的非对称擦除因子的chunkwise WY算法，以及支持高效并行训练的门控感知反向传播。

Result: 在1.3B参数、100B FineWeb-Edu tokens上训练，Gated DeltaNet-2在语言建模、常识推理和检索任务上取得最佳综合表现；在长上下文RULER多键检索任务上优势最为显著。
